7. **Receptionist phone lookup.** `PhoneLookupField` + `lookupPersonByPhoneAction` (`students/actions.ts`, `teachers/actions.ts`) — the exact same `resolveContact`/`describeMatch` duplicate-match pair the full add-forms' own submit already runs, reachable from a phone number alone, before the rest of the form is filled in. Rate-limited by a new `lib/rate-limit.ts` (an in-process sliding window, 20 calls/minute per staff member — explicitly documented as a Phase-1-scale simplification, not a distributed limiter). Returns a `MatchCandidate` — the same minimum-fields shape `MatchPanel` already renders — and "Use this person" only pre-fills the name/contact fields; nothing is linked until the receptionist submits the real form.
8. **Reception scan shows the student's photo.** `reception/page.tsx` resolves a short-lived signed URL (`lib/db/storage.ts`'s `signedUrlFor`, 10-minute TTL) for the selected student's `photoUrl` — for both the QR-scan and the search-result path — and `CheckInForm` renders it as a round avatar beside the check-in prompt, or a plain placeholder icon when no photo is on file. Closes spec §7.1's second verified gap; see §7.1 for the full note.
9. **Seed rewrite.** `prisma/seed.ts` now seeds one `Organization` — **"Center 60"** — with **two branches** ("Center 60 — Downtown", "Center 60 — Nasr City"), so `npm run check:branches`-shaped multi-branch behaviour has realistic data to exercise, not just the gate's own throwaway fixtures. The global academic catalog (M13) gets **grades 1–12** (not just the three secondary ones the old seed had) and six subjects spread across several of them, every one bilingual (`nameAr` + `nameEn`); both branches pick the same catalog via their own `CenterCatalogSelection` rows. Every person the seed creates — the owner, both branches' managers/receptionists/teachers/students — has an English (Latin-script) name; the organization/branch names are English too. The owner's real `Membership` names the first branch as "home," resolving to the second via the existing M11 org-level widening (`getUserCenterRelations`), not a second row. Idempotent by slug/email/name, same as before.

## M19 — Data-visibility window + centre lifecycle states (delivered)
**Goal:** bound what a centre-side role can read, and give a centre a real lifecycle.
**Build:** **`CenterSettings.dataVisibilityDays`** (default 30, a rolling day-count ending today — not a calendar-month anchor) — no centre-side role (OWNER/MANAGER/RECEPTIONIST/ACCOUNTANT) reads data older than 1 calendar month: analytics, money, old sessions, attendance history. **Platform admin bypasses**, via `ctx.isPlatformAdmin`. Per-centre configurable from the centre's own `/settings` screen (`Settings.general`'s new "Data visibility window" card) **and** adjustable by a platform admin from `/admin/centers/[id]` (`updateCenterVisibilityWindowAction`, same column). Enforced **in the data layer** (`lib/db/visibility.ts`'s `visibilityWindowFloor`/`clampRangeToVisibility`/`isWithinVisibilityWindow`), never in the UI: Sessions/Analytics/Expenses clamp their own resolved date range before it reaches a query, an out-of-window session 404s by direct `/sessions/[id]` URL, and `lib/db/scoped.ts`'s `refunds.list` injects the floor directly (the one reader with no caller-supplied range to clamp). **Carve-outs: settlement/payout records and audit logs stay visible** (`scoped(ctx).settlements`/`auditLogs` apply no date filter at all) — **refunds are not carved out**, since a refund is money moving, not a payout. **Lifecycle:** `Center.active` → `lifecycleState: ONBOARDING / ACTIVE / GRACE / SUSPENDED / ARCHIVED` (migration backfill: `true → ACTIVE`, `false → SUSPENDED`); `ACCESSIBLE_LIFECYCLE_STATES` (ONBOARDING/ACTIVE/GRACE) is what `getUserCenterRelations()` gates sign-in on, replacing `center: { active: true }`. Transitions follow a stated table (`LIFECYCLE_TRANSITIONS` in `lib/db/platform.ts`): ARCHIVED is terminal; every non-terminal state can jump straight to SUSPENDED/ARCHIVED; only ONBOARDING/GRACE/SUSPENDED recover to ACTIVE — enforced by `transitionCenterLifecycle` server-side, not just by which options `/admin/centers/[id]`'s form renders. A freshly admin-provisioned center now starts ONBOARDING. **`Center.plan` removed entirely** — the column, `updateCenterPlan`, the action, the `/admin/centers/[id]` editor, the `check:admin` assertion, and every doc reference. **No module entitlements.**
**Done when:** an out-of-window read is refused by **direct URL and direct API call**, not merely hidden; settlement and audit rows in the same range remain readable; **nothing is deleted** — widening the window restores full visibility immediately; and no reference to `Center.plan` survives anywhere in code or docs.
**Gates:** `npm run check:visibility` (new — data-layer denial modelled on the receptionist profit-denial shape, both carve-outs plus the refund non-carve-out, and an explicit "the rows still exist" assertion). `npm run check:admin` updated for lifecycle-transition validity (valid and invalid transitions, ARCHIVED terminality), the removed plan editor/action, and `platformCenterCounts`' new per-state shape.

# Pre-pilot product revisions (R-series)

> M0–M19 built what the spec asked for. The R-series (R1–R10) revises it from pre-pilot product review, **before** the pilot centre's first live day — each one a small, separately-gated change. They interleave with M20 (deployment) rather than extending the M-numbering, because they change *what* the product does, not whether it can be deployed. Each R-entry carries its own Goal / Build / Done when / Gates, like the milestones above, and **stays inside its own scope**: an R-item's "not here" list names the R-item that does own it.

## R1 — Attendance statuses + configurable payment methods (delivered)
**Goal:** attendance has exactly three statuses, and payment methods are the centre's own configurable list rather than a hardcoded enum.
**Build:** **Remove Excused** — `AttendanceStatus` becomes `PRESENT / LATE / ABSENT`, the migration converting any existing excused row to ABSENT first; drop `ClassGroup.allowExcused`, its class-form toggle, the roster button, the server-side refusal path and every excused count (receipt, analytics, print, teacher/student/parent portals) and their message keys. **Remove `ClassGroup.attendanceRequired`** (no behaviour read it) from the class form, the teacher's new-class request forms and the Approvals card, with old `ChangeRequest` payloads still carrying the key tolerated. **Configurable payment methods** — a new `CenterPaymentMethod` model (`name`, `active`, cascade with `Center`); `Payment.method` → `paymentMethodId` + **`methodLabelSnapshot`** frozen at record time; the enum dropped. Migration order: create Cash / InstaPay / Wallet / Visa for every existing centre → backfill each payment's id and label from its old enum value → `NOT NULL` → drop the column and enum. New centres get Cash / InstaPay / Vodafone Cash. At least one active method always; delete = hard only when unreferenced, otherwise deactivate. `recordPayment` resolves the method in its own transaction, scoped by centre **and** `active`. A Settings → Payment methods screen (add via dialog, rename, activate/deactivate, delete), gated by the existing `center.settings.edit`, every change audited. **Not here:** automatic late/absent marking and session closing (**R2**).
**Done when:** no reference to the removed status, `allowExcused`, `attendanceRequired` or the old method enum remains in code, messages or docs, except historical notes in this file (and the decision record in `00-master-product-spec.md` §5.1); a payment keeps its original method label after the method is renamed; a centre cannot record a payment with another centre's method id or an inactive one, by direct call; the last active method cannot be deactivated or deleted.
**Gates:** `npm run check:payments` passing with **fixture-shape changes only** (a real method id in place of the old `"CASH"` literal — no assertion edited); new `npm run check:payment-methods`; every other `check:*` gate plus `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .`, `prisma validate` and `check:rtl`.
**Delivered:** one migration, `prisma/migrations/20261012000000_r1_attendance_statuses_payment_methods` (seed/demo data only existed when it ran; on the dev database it converted 0 excused rows and backfilled 197 payments, all CASH → each centre's own "Cash" row). **Statuses:** `consecutiveAbsenceCount` needed no change — it always counted only ABSENT rows and stopped at any other mark, so an excused session used to *break* a streak; with three statuses PRESENT/LATE break it and ABSENT extends it, which is the intended behaviour. `components/center/attendance-control.tsx` now exports the three-status order and tints, and Reception's check-in form uses them instead of its own copy. **Methods:** `lib/db/payment-methods.ts` owns every method write and both invariants — the last-active check runs under a `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` lock on the centre's method rows, so two concurrent deactivations cannot together leave none (asserted); a delete on a referenced method deactivates it instead and says so on the row (`ActionState.status: "deactivated"`), and the FK is `RESTRICT` as a backstop. `scoped(ctx).paymentMethods` exposes reads only (`list`, `listActive`, `findById`), so no generic `update`/`remove` exists to route around those rules. `recordPayment` returns `invalidMethod` for a foreign, inactive or made-up id, after the existing attendee/session checks and before any write. Reception and the session roster read the centre's active methods server-side; the roster's "Paid · method" line, the void audit row and the record-payment audit row all carry the frozen snapshot. Receipts, the refunds ledger and analytics never displayed or grouped by method, so they had nothing to switch — there is no by-method breakdown anywhere. `createCenterWithSettings` and `prisma/seed.ts` (`ensureDefaultPaymentMethods`, idempotent) seed the defaults; the seed's demo history now records against the branch's first active method. **Settings:** `/settings/payment-methods` reuses `EntityManager`, which gained an opt-in `addVia="dialog"` mode (docs/04 §5 "Dialog") — rooms keep the inline add card. Create / rename / activate / deactivate / delete write `paymentMethod.*` audit rows. **No new permission action** — the screen and its four actions use `center.settings.edit`, exactly like rooms. **Gates:** `npm run check:payment-methods` (`scripts/payment-methods-check.ts`, new, in `package.json` and CI) — the Prisma, zod and database enums all lack the removed status and a raw write of it is refused; new-centre defaults and order; the label snapshot surviving a rename (and a later payment taking the new name); cross-centre, inactive and made-up method ids refused with no row written; cross-centre rename/deactivate/delete refused; hard-delete vs. deactivate-instead; the FK backstop; the last active method refused for both deactivate and delete; the concurrent-deactivation race. `check:classes` swapped its two M10 toggle assertions (they tested the removed feature) for one asserting a stale form's `allowExcused`/`attendanceRequired` keys are dropped. `check:approvals` deliberately keeps a legacy `attendanceRequired` key in its `NEW_CLASS` fixture payload, as the "old payloads still resolve" case. CI also gained `check:visibility` (M19) and `check:filters`, which existed and passed but were never wired in.

## R2 — Session lifecycle: end/lock, auto-late, auto-absent, change log (delivered)
**Goal:** a session becomes a closed record once it ends, Late/Absent are automatic, and live status changes are logged so nobody can quietly rewrite what happened.
**Build:** `Session.closedAt`/`closedBy`/`closeMode` (`SessionCloseMode` MANUAL | AUTO), closing stores `status: COMPLETED`. A new `SessionAttendanceChange` (centre-scoped, cascade) for every live status change. **One closure guard in one module** (`lib/db/session-lifecycle.ts`) called first by every write path — attendance create/correct, payment, void, refund, cancel, reschedule, cut override, add-attendee/check-in — refusing a closed session by direct call; a session whose day ended in the centre's timezone with no `closedAt` is closed **lazily** (no cron) on its next read or write, AUTO, with enrolled no-shows written as ABSENT exactly once, and the write is refused. **Automatic Late** at check-in (start + the class's threshold, centre default as fallback; one function, clock-injectable) and **no status picker** on any check-in surface. **Live Present/Late → Absent on a paid student voids the payment in the same transaction**, optional reason, change row + `attendance.correct` + `payment.void` audit rows. **End session** (`session.close`: OWNER/MANAGER/RECEPTIONIST, backfilled for existing centres) with a can't-be-undone confirmation, a closed banner, every control locked, and the receipt — now with LATE count, close time/mode and a "Changes during session" section — leading the page with a Print button (`/sessions/[id]/print`, the teacher print layout reused). **Settlements:** a session settlement refuses a live session; a period settlement counts only closed sessions and says so. No reopen, no delete. **Not here:** the reception desk's own End-session button and the page layout (**R7**, which reuses `EndSessionButton`/`endSessionAction`), rooms/capacity (**R3**).
**Done when:** a closed session refuses attendance create/update, payment, void and refund **by direct call**; an unclosed session whose day ended auto-closes on its next touch with `closeMode: AUTO`, the note visible, and enrolled no-shows Absent exactly once; a check-in after start + threshold is stored Late without anyone choosing it, within it Present; a live Present→Absent on a paid student voids the payment atomically with an optional reason, the change row and both audit rows, and the voided money is out of analytics and the receipt; the printed receipt shows the changes section.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:session-lifecycle`; every other `check:*` gate, `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .`, `prisma validate` and `check:rtl`.
**Delivered:** one migration, `prisma/migrations/20261012120000_r2_session_lifecycle` — the close fields and enum, `SessionAttendanceChange`, `Settlement.closedSessionsOnly` (backfilled `false` on the zero settlements the dev database held, `true` by default), and `session.close` `PermissionGrant` rows for OWNER/MANAGER/RECEPTIONIST at every existing centre. Nothing existing was rewritten: past sessions with no `closedAt` close on their next touch. **The guard** (`ensureSessionOpenOrAutoClose`) takes `FOR SHARE` on the session for ordinary writes and `FOR UPDATE` to close or to change the session row, so a rush of check-ins on one session still runs concurrently while a close waits for in-flight writes and later writes see it; payments and corrections also lock the student's roster row (session first, then attendee), closing a pre-existing race (a payment slipping in while the student is switched to Absent). *(Corrected at SEC-2b: this entry also claimed to close the "second active payment" race. It did not — the lock query read its payment check from a snapshot taken before the lock was granted, so two desks still both recorded a payment, SEC-05. SEC-2b re-reads it in a fresh statement after the lock and adds the partial unique index `Payment_one_active_per_attendee`.)* A check-in is one `INSERT … SELECT … FOR SHARE` statement, atomic with the closure check. Cancelled and rescheduled sessions keep M15's refusals for new attendance/payment but still allow a void/refund of money they already carry (cancelling never touches money). *(Reversed at SEC-2b, D-07: a cancel or reschedule is refused while the session holds an active payment, terminal sessions close at the end of their day, and void/refund refuse them.)* M15's `SessionClosedError` moved into the lifecycle module (re-exported from `lib/db/attendance.ts`) and gained `sessionClosed`. **Display status** now reads the centre's timezone and agrees with the stored close: COMPLETED once closed or once the day is over; LIVE from start until the session is ended or its day ends (it still accepts corrections and payments until then). **End session** is refused before start, on cancelled/rescheduled and on already-closed sessions; the AUTO close writes its own actor-less `session.close` audit row. **Void-on-absent** needs `payment.void` as well as `attendance.correct` (re-checked under the lock); the change row and its `attendance.correct`/`payment.void` audit rows are written inside `changeAttendanceStatus`'s transaction, so a failed void rolls all of it back. A refunded payment can no longer also be voided (it would be taken out of revenue twice). **Settlement interaction (item 10 — what was found first):** before R2, `createSessionSettlement` accepted a still-live session and `voidPayment` never touched settlements, so a DRAFT settlement could keep counting a payment voided afterwards; the live database held **zero** settlements, so no existing data was affected. Since R2, a settlement only ever counts closed sessions, whose money cannot be voided or refunded — but a period settlement's *date range* can still contain a live session it did not count, and the M14 refund adjustment subtracted on date scope alone, which would have under-paid the teacher. Both void and refund now go through one rule (`adjustUnpaidSettlementsForReversal`): adjust a DRAFT/APPROVED settlement only if its total actually counted the payment (recorded before the settlement, and — for an R2 settlement — its session already closed then). What a still-live session left out of a period settlement should do next was **OD-8** — resolved at SEC-2b (D-05): settlements claim payments, so a later overlapping period picks it up. **Fixtures:** gates and the seed write history against past-dated sessions through a scripts-only `historicalBackfill` option (explicit status for `createAttendance`, closure check skipped, cancelled/rescheduled refusal kept), asserted absent from `app/`; `classes-check`/`enrollment-check` pin `now` for their cancel/reschedule fixtures (generated or hard-dated December sessions would otherwise close under the new rule); the seed closes its history sessions after writing them. **No assertion was edited.** `check:performance` needed one fixture-level change: its `recordPayment` transaction now passes `WRITE_TRANSACTION_OPTIONS`, the same acquire budget the production money actions now use (`02-architecture.md` §14) — on a ~275 ms link the extra lock round trips pushed Prisma's 2 s default acquire wait over the edge; with it, 30 concurrent check-ins finish in about a tenth of the serial extrapolation. **No session delete path exists** (deleting a class, the only cascade, is already refused while it has sessions) and **no reopen exists** — both asserted statically. `npm run check:session-lifecycle` (`scripts/session-lifecycle-check.ts`, in `package.json` and CI): the guard on every write path by direct call; the lazy close at the Cairo day boundary with an injected clock (a 23:30 session, 00:05 local = still the previous day in UTC); ABSENT materialization (removed and never-enrolled students excluded) and its idempotency under repeat and a three-way race; the late boundaries (exactly at threshold, one minute after) in the pure function and as stored rows; live void-on-absent atomicity, the optional reason, the permission refusal, and rollback when the void itself fails (a temporary trigger forces it); change-log and audit rows; voided money out of the receipt and analytics; settlement refusal on a live session, success after close, period-counts-closed-only, the legacy void adjustment and the live refund that must not adjust; cross-centre isolation on the new model; `session.close` defaults, refusal without it, and the backfill; the static no-`historicalBackfill`-in-`app/`, no-delete and no-reopen assertions.

## R3 — Joinable rooms, multi-room sessions, capacity from rooms, slot-conflict confirmation (delivered)

**Build:** a centre declares which rooms can be **combined** (`RoomLink`, stored once per pair, `roomAId < roomBId`, same centre, never self); a class or a session uses a primary room plus extras each linked to the primary (a star, not transitive), and its seats are the rooms' **sum**. **`ClassGroup.capacity` is dropped** — the enrolment cap is the room set's capacity only, reversing M15's min(class, room); attendance stays uncapped; new and edited classes need a primary room. **Double-booking** (room or teacher) is refused under DENY and, under WARN, refused **with the conflict list** until `confirmConflicts` is set — then saved and audited. **Not here:** the class/session dialog re-layout (R6), removing the class date-range/generator (R4), one-off sessions (R5).

**Delivered:** one migration, `prisma/migrations/20261013090000_r3_room_sets` — `RoomLink` (unique pair + a `CHECK (roomAId < roomBId)` backstop), `ClassGroupRoom`, `SessionRoom` (all `centerId`-indexed, cascading), and `ClassGroup.capacity` dropped (no production data). All three models are in `lib/db/scoped.ts` (reads) and `supabase/m10-rls-and-storage.sql` (RLS backstop list). **One module per rule:** `lib/rooms.ts` (pure: `effectiveRoomIds`, `effectiveCapacity` — a room at 0 leaves the set uncapped — `isValidCombination`), `lib/capacity.ts`'s `enrollmentCap(roomSetCapacity)`, `lib/db/room-sets.ts` (the only writer of the three models; link/unlink with the "in use by an active class or a non-closed future session" refusal naming counts; `resolveRoomSet`, the one validator), `lib/db/slot-conflicts.ts` (the one occupancy loader for sessions and weekly slots — whole room sets, cancelled and rescheduled originals free, closed occupied — plus `describeConflicts` and the `schedule.conflict.confirm` audit writer). `findSessionConflicts`/`findScheduleConflicts` now return a teacher clash **and** one room clash per shared room, with room/teacher, class, date or weekday and time window. **Contract:** `decideConflicts(count, policy, confirmConflicts)`; every slot writer takes the flag — schedule create/edit and class teacher/room-set change (the class's existing slots re-checked), `rescheduleSession` (copies the original's room set unless another valid one is picked), the new `updateSessionRooms` (one session only, never the class's cap), `generateSessions` (clean occurrences always created, conflicting ones held back until a confirmed re-run, extras copied to `SessionRoom`; cron never confirms), `resolveSessionRequest` (NEW_SESSION approval, class room set copied). Confirmed conflicts are audited in the write's own transaction. An **omitted** flag at the `lib/db` level is the pre-R3 script path (policy only) — kept so the seed and the pre-R3 WARN assertions in `check:classes`/`check:approvals`/`check:enrollment` keep their meaning; `check:rooms` asserts every `app/` call passes the flag. **UI:** `RoomSetPicker` (class form, reschedule, session "Rooms"), `SlotConflictNotice` + the debounced `checkSlotConflictsAction` preview (class edit, schedule rows, reschedule, session rooms; generator and approval show the server's list), Settings → Rooms "Rooms that can be combined" card with pair previews; deactivate/delete of a room in a combined set refused with counts; capacity removed from the class form, both teacher request forms, the Approvals card and every list (lists show enrolled / seats of the room set). No new permission action: `center.settings.edit` (links), `class.manage`, `session.manage`, `approvals.manage` as before. **Seed:** Center 60 — Downtown links Room 1 + Room 2 and runs its first class on both (40 seats); the generator copies the extra room to every upcoming session. Open question recorded: **OD-9** (should a class's room-set change follow its already-generated sessions?).

**Gate changes — for review.** `npm run check:rooms` (`scripts/rooms-check.ts`, in `package.json` and CI) is new. Existing gates:
- *Expected (M15 class-capacity assertions in `check:enrollment`), edited minimally:*
  - `enrollmentCap: class=0 (unlimited), room=20 → cap is 20` → `enrollmentCap: room set of 20 → cap is 20` (`enrollmentCap(20) === 20`)
  - `enrollmentCap: class=15, room=0 (no room / unlimited) → cap is 15` → `enrollmentCap: room set 0 (no room / no capacity set) → unlimited (0)` (`enrollmentCap(0) === 0`)
  - `enrollmentCap: class=15, room=20 → cap is the smaller, 15` → `enrollmentCap: no room set at all → unlimited (0)` (`enrollmentCap(null) === 0`)
  - `enrollmentCap: class=20, room=15 → cap is the smaller, 15 (the room is the hard cap)` → removed (no class value to compare)
  - `enrollmentCap: both 0 → unlimited (0)` → removed (covered by the `0` case above)
  - label only: `The class's own capacity is 0 (unlimited), yet the first two instant-enrolls into a room-capacity-2 class both succeed` → `The first two instant-enrolls into a room-capacity-2 class both succeed`
  - label only: `A third instant-enroll is refused as classFull — the ROOM's capacity is the hard cap, not the class's own (unset) target` → `… — the ROOM's capacity is the hard cap`
- *Outside `check:enrollment` — flagged, not silently adjusted:*
  - `check:classes` "A class at capacity blocks a second enrollment (capacity check)": `activeCount >= classA.capacity` → `activeCount >= roomA1.capacity` (fixture: classA's capacity 1 moved onto its room, `roomA1` created with `capacity: 1`). The same reversed decision, but not in the file the brief named.
  - `check:classes` "The class schema drops the removed allowExcused/attendanceRequired keys (R1)": the fixture's `capacity: "0"` replaced by `roomId: "r"`, because a class now requires a room — without it the parse fails for the wrong reason.
  - `check:classes` "The class schema rejects a percentage above 100" and "…rejects a negative capacity": **not edited**, still pass — but now partly for the wrong reason (their fixtures carry no room, so the parse fails on `roomRequired`; the negative-capacity one tests a field that no longer exists). Suggested follow-up: add `roomId` to the first and retire the second.
  - Fixture shape only (capacity off `ClassGroup` creates): `check:catalog`, `check:parent`, `check:ratings`, `check:performance`, `check:enrollment`'s `makeClass`, and `check:student-portal`'s `makeClass` (a "capacity-N class" is now a class on a capacity-N room; its assertion is unchanged).
- *Not edited, by design:* the four pre-R3 assertions that WARN saves a conflicting slot (`check:classes` generator and reschedule, `check:approvals` NEW_SESSION, `check:enrollment` whole-window reschedule) call the `lib/db` writers **without** the flag, i.e. the script path above. If that path should go too, those four need `confirmConflicts: true` added — a deliberate follow-up, not done here.

## R4 — Class date ranges + centre term presets; session generator removed (delivered)
**Goal:** a class has a start and an end date, and creating it creates every session in that range — the rolling-window generator, its buttons and its cron go.
**Build:** `ClassGroup.startDate`/`endDate` (date-only, required; migration backfills from each class's earliest/latest session, else its `createdAt` day) and a new **`CenterTerm`** preset model (name, dates, active; unique per centre case-insensitively in the app layer; may overlap). Remove the generator surface: both "Generate sessions" buttons and their action, the rolling 42-day window, `app/api/cron/generate-sessions`, `CRON_SECRET`, any Vercel cron entry (there was no generation-window setting). **One engine**, `lib/db/class-sessions.ts`: `materializeClassSessions` (from `max(startDate, today in the centre's timezone)` through `endDate`, one per matching weekday, holidays skipped, never in the past, room set copied) and `resyncClassSessions` (pristine future sessions follow the class; everything else kept and reported), with the whole range as **one** conflict decision (WARN: refuse with the full list, nothing written, unless confirmed; DENY: refuse). `confirmConflicts` **required** on every data-layer slot writer. Settings → **Terms** (add via dialog, rename, redate, activate/deactivate, hard delete; `center.settings.edit`; audited). Class form: a **Class period** section — "Choose a term" (copies the dates) or "Custom dates" — required in zod, end ≥ start, ≤ 400 days; the period and session count on the class list and detail. Editing slots, dates, teacher or rooms resyncs (resolves **OD-9**). **Deactivate** (pristine future deleted, the rest cancelled, started/closed untouched, enrolments kept), **reactivate** (only before the end date; regenerates under the contract), **delete** (only a class that never ran). Deactivated classes excluded at the data layer from every forward reader; history readers unchanged. Seed: Center 60 terms + every class a period, sessions from the engine. **Not here:** the class dialog layout and time pickers (**R6**), one-off sessions (**R5**).
**Done when:** no generator button, cron route, `CRON_SECRET`, rolling-window function or Vercel cron entry remains in code, config or docs (historical notes here excepted); a 9 Oct 2026 – 1 Apr 2027 class with two weekly slots creates exactly the matching dates from today, skipping holidays, none in the past, with its room set; a term preset fills the dates and editing the term later moves no class; whole-range conflicts behave per the contract by direct call with nothing created on refusal; editing slots, dates, teacher or rooms resyncs pristine future sessions and reports the kept ones, and a re-run changes nothing; a deactivated class has no forward-looking sessions and is in no picker or upcoming list while its history stays in analytics; a class that has run, or has enrolments, cannot be deleted by direct call.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:class-periods`; every other `check:*` gate, `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .`, `prisma validate` and `check:rtl`.
**Delivered:** one migration, `prisma/migrations/20261014090000_r4_class_periods_terms` (no production data; the dev database's 13 classes all had sessions, so each period became exactly the span it already had). **Engine:** `lib/db/class-sessions.ts` — one read-only `computeSync` (classify future sessions, plan the missing dates, one conflict list for the whole range against dated occupancy) feeds both the write path and the live preview (`previewClassSessions` ← `checkSlotConflictsAction`), so the list a person confirms is the list the save checks; refusals throw inside the write's transaction and roll the class write back too. Deletes and re-rooms re-assert "still pristine" in their own `WHERE`. "Future" means **not yet started** in the centre's timezone, so today's slot that already began is never created (it would otherwise auto-close at midnight with every enrolled student Absent, R2). Weekly slots are no longer compared with weekly slots — the dated sessions are the whole truth (`lib/db/slot-conflicts.ts`'s `loadScheduleOccupancy`/`weeklySlotConflicts`/`loadClassSlotPlan` removed; `lib/scheduling.ts`'s pure `findScheduleConflicts` kept for `check:classes`). `scoped(ctx)` lost its generic class/slot/holiday writers (every write goes through the engine) and gained read-only `terms`. **Forward readers:** `OPEN_CLASS_SESSION` ("class active, or the session is a closed record") on today's lists, the teacher's list and counts, and conflict occupancy; `active: true` on the student's upcoming sessions, both approvals queues, `confirmReservation`, `resolveSessionRequest` and the staff enrol action (Discover and `reserveOrEnroll` already had it). **Holidays:** a holiday added later deletes that day's pristine sessions in the same transaction; removing one restores nothing — recorded as **OD-10**. **UI:** `ClassPeriodFields` (segmented "Choose a term / Custom dates", the shared `DatePicker`, a link to Settings → Terms when none exist), `SessionSyncNotice` (created / removed / moved / cancelled, and the amber "kept" count) under the class form, the schedule builder, the activate/deactivate control and the holiday calendar; the class page's **Sessions** card (period, count, kept list) replaced the generator card; the delete refusal names each blocker and offers Deactivate; `EntityFields` gained a `date` kind for Settings → Terms. **Seed:** both Center 60 branches get Semester 1 / Semester 2 / Full year; every class copies Full year (its start reaching back over the 4 weeks of history) and is created through `createClassWithSessions`. **The create dialog still has no slot rows** (R6 re-lays it out), so from the UI a new class's sessions appear as slots are added on its page.

**Gate changes — for review.** `npm run check:class-periods` (`scripts/class-periods-check.ts`, in `package.json` and CI) is new. Existing gates:
- *Expected (assertions on the removed generator), edited minimally:*
  - `check:classes` — `generateSessions(prisma, centerA.id, "WARN", classA.id)` ×4 (first run, idempotent re-run, holiday run, clean regeneration) → `materialize(centerA.id, classA.id)` (= `materializeClassSessions` over a 6-week class **period** from a pinned today, `confirmConflicts: true`); the fixture classes get that 42-day period instead of the generator's 42-day window. Assertions unchanged (12, 0, Mondays − 1, 6 with no conflicts).
  - `check:classes` — `generateSessions(…, "WARN", classB.id)` → `materialize(centerA.id, classB.id)`; assertion unchanged (6 created, conflicts reported).
  - `check:classes` — `generateSessions(…, "DENY", classB.id)` → the centre's policy set to DENY, `materialize(centerA.id, classB.id)`, policy reset (the engine reads the centre's policy instead of taking one); assertion unchanged (0 created, conflicts reported).
  - `check:rooms` static — slot writers `["rescheduleSession", "updateSessionRooms", "generateSessions", "resolveSessionRequest"]` → `generateSessions` replaced by the engine's app-facing writers `createClassWithSessions`, `updateClassWithSessions`, `writeClassSlot`, `reactivateClass`.
  - `check:rooms` "Generator under DENY…" — `genDenied.created === 0 && conflicts > 0 && !needsConfirmation` → refused as `conflict`, conflicts > 0, and the class has 0 sessions.
  - `check:rooms` "Generator under WARN without the flag…" — `created === 0 && needsConfirmation && conflicts > 0` → refused as `conflictsNeedConfirmation`, conflicts > 0, 0 sessions.
  - `check:rooms` "Generator under WARN with the flag…" — unchanged in substance (`created > 0`, every session carries the extra room), read from the engine's report. "…audited" unchanged. Fixture: the two generator classes get a 7-Monday period (1 Mar – 12 Apr 2027) and a pinned clock; the centre's policy is toggled instead of passed.
- *R3 follow-ups the brief asked for (fixture-only):* `check:classes` "The class schema rejects a negative capacity" **removed** (it tested a field R3 dropped); "rejects a percentage above 100" fixture `capacity: "0"` → `roomId: "r"` + a period, so it fails on the percentage only.
- *`confirmConflicts` now required (fixture-only, assertions unchanged):* `check:classes` 7 `rescheduleSession` calls, `check:enrollment` 3, `check:session-lifecycle` 1 — `{ now }` → `{ now, confirmConflicts: true }`; `check:approvals` 7 `resolveSessionRequest` calls gain `"en", { confirmConflicts: true }`. These include the four pre-R3 WARN assertions R3 left on the omitted-flag path.
- *Fixture shape only (a class period is a required column):* `startDate`/`endDate` on every direct `classGroup.create` in `check:admin`, `check:analytics`, `check:approvals`, `check:branches`, `check:catalog`, `check:classes`, `check:enrollment`, `check:parent`, `check:payment-methods`, `check:payments`, `check:ratings`, `check:rooms`, `check:performance`, `check:session-lifecycle`, `check:settlements`, `check:student-portal`, `check:teacher-portal`, `check:visibility`; the two "drops allowExcused" / percentage schema fixtures in `check:classes` gain a period.
- *Flagged, NOT edited (resolved in R5 Step 0 — see R5's gate changes):* `check:rooms` "Class schedule/class-edit actions decide conflicts with the confirmation flag" counts `decideConflicts(…, confirmConflicts)` calls in `app/[locale]/(center)/classes/actions.ts` (≥ 3). R4 moves that decision into the data layer as the brief requires (`lib/db/class-sessions.ts`, one whole-range decision inside the write's transaction), so the actions no longer call `decideConflicts` and this assertion now fails. Not an assertion on the generator, so it is left as it was for review. Suggested replacement: assert the decision in `lib/db/class-sessions.ts` takes `options.confirmConflicts` (`decideConflicts(conflicts.length, sync.policy, options.confirmConflicts)`) — the app-side half (every class action passes `confirmConflicts` to the engine) is already covered by the writers-list assertion above.
- *Flagged, NOT edited (resolved in R5 Step 0 — see R5's gate changes):* `check:session-lifecycle` "No session delete path exists anywhere in app/ or lib/" greps for `.session.delete(`/`.deleteMany(`. R4 requires deleting **pristine** future sessions (a resync, a deactivation, a holiday added later, a class that never ran), so `lib/db/class-sessions.ts` now matches and this R2 assertion fails. The R2 rule it guards — a **closed** session is never deleted — still holds: every engine delete carries `closedAt: null` (and no attendees, payments, settlements, ratings, override or reschedule link) in its own `WHERE`. Not routed around (no raw SQL to dodge the grep), left as it was for review. Suggested replacement: allow `session.deleteMany` only in `lib/db/class-sessions.ts`, and assert every such call's `where` spreads `STILL_PRISTINE` (which pins `closedAt: null`) — plus a DB assertion that a closed session survives a resync, deactivation and holiday.

## R5 — Manual sessions: an extra session on a class, and one-time sessions (delivered)
**Goal:** staff can add (a) an extra session on an existing class and (b) a standalone one-time session that belongs to no class, from the Sessions tab.
**Build:** `Session.classGroupId` nullable (FK + cascade kept; the `(classGroupId, date, startTime)` unique stays — NULLs are distinct) plus `title`, `subjectId`/`gradeId` (catalog FKs, like `ClassGroup`'s) and `isManual` (default false). Invariant: no class ⇒ title, teacher, price and both cut fields set — enforced in `lib/db/sessions.ts`. **Money:** `recordPayment` freezes a one-time session's own cut and refuses `missingCut`; `lib/money.ts` untouched. **One label helper,** `lib/session-label.ts`, replacing every direct `session.classGroup.*` read. **`isManual` in the pristine predicate** (resync, end-date change, holiday insertion, class edit never touch a manual session; deactivation follows the normal delete-if-pristine-else-cancel rule); `resolveSessionRequest` sets `isManual`. `createManualSession` / `createOneOffSession` / `deleteManualSession` in `lib/db/class-sessions.ts` (one writer), centre-scoped, conflict contract required, `SessionRoom` extras, audited; no backdating in the centre's timezone; a holiday never blocks one. **UI:** "New session" on the Sessions tab (a two-mode dialog, live conflict preview + holiday notice), "One-time" / "Extra session" badges on lists and detail, Delete on the detail page only while allowed; receipts, print routes, reception, dashboard and every portal render one-time sessions through the helper; Discover/reservations never offer one. **Seed:** one one-time session tomorrow and one extra session on a class in Center 60 (Downtown). **Not here:** the class/session dialog re-layout (**R6**), the reception page (**R7**).
**Done when:** a one-time session can be created, takes drop-in attendance and payment, freezes its own cut at payment, closes with a receipt, and appears in teacher/student/parent history with its title and no null errors; an extra session survives class edit, slot move, end-date shortening and holiday insertion, and deactivation handles it per the rules; a past date and an unconfirmed clash are refused by direct call (the clash with its list); a payment on a one-time session with a missing cut is refused; no code path dereferences `session.classGroup` without null handling.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:one-off-sessions`; every other `check:*` gate, `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .`, `prisma validate` and `check:rtl`.
**Delivered:** one migration, `prisma/migrations/20261015090000_r5_manual_one_off_sessions` (no production data; existing rows `isManual = false`), hand-written and checked against the live schema with a **read-only** `migrate diff --from-url` (no shadow database — CLAUDE.md working rule 7, added in this pass), then applied with `migrate deploy`. **Engine** (`lib/db/class-sessions.ts`): `writeManualSession` is the shared transaction (re-resolve ids in the centre, `pastDate`, `duplicateSlot`, the invariant, R3's decision with a class-less candidate so a manual session is checked against its own class and other one-time sessions too, extras, `session.create` + `schedule.conflict.confirm` audits); `previewManualSession` behind `checkSlotConflictsAction`'s new `newSession` kind (also returns `holiday`); the pristine predicate split into `UNTOUCHED` → `NO_OVERRIDE` → `STILL_PRISTINE` (`isManual: false`), a `"manual"` disqualifier, manual sessions skipped in `computeSync`'s kept report, `deactivateClass` deleting under `NO_OVERRIDE`, `removeSessionsOnHoliday` counting one-time sessions as kept; `deleteManualSession` + `manualSessionDeleteBlockers`/`manualSessionDeleteState`. **Null-class plumbing:** `lib/db/session-lifecycle.ts`'s gate query `LEFT JOIN`s the class (late threshold falls back to the centre default; `materializeAbsences` writes nothing), `OPEN_CLASS_SESSION` matches `classGroupId: null` first, `findSessionConflicts` never matches `null === null` and gained `excludeSessionId` (reschedule/rooms of a one-time session), `updateSessionShareOverride` refuses to clear a one-time cut (`cutRequired`), `rescheduleSession` copies title/subject/grade/`isManual` onto the replacement, the session list's subject filter and the teacher list's grade filter match a one-time session's own catalog ids. **Label call sites changed** (all now via `lib/session-label.ts`, or null-safe): Sessions list + row, session detail, both print routes, reception live rail, dashboard today list (and its enrolled count), refunds ledger, centre settlements, teacher sessions list + detail, teacher student history, teacher settlements, student schedule (upcoming + history), parent portal, the rating pop-up (`attendedSessionsToRate` returns `title`), and conflict lines (`loadSessionOccupancy`); null-safe class reads in `searchAddAttendeeCandidatesAction`, `resolveEnrollment`, `isBlockedFromSession`, reception check-in, the session detail roster/absence streaks and the teacher detail streaks. `sessionDisplayName` moved out of `lib/db/sessions.ts`.

**Gate changes — for review.** `npm run check:one-off-sessions` (`scripts/one-off-sessions-check.ts`, in `package.json` and CI) is new. **Step 0 — the two R4 follow-ups, as decided:**
- `check:rooms` — **old:** "Class schedule/class-edit actions decide conflicts with the confirmation flag" = at least 3 `decideConflicts(…, confirmConflicts)` calls in `app/[locale]/(center)/classes/actions.ts`. **New:** "Class schedule/class-edit conflicts are decided inside lib/db/class-sessions.ts with the confirmation flag" = at least one `decideConflicts` call in `lib/db/class-sessions.ts`, and every one of them passes `options.confirmConflicts`.
- `check:session-lifecycle` — **old:** "No session delete path exists anywhere in app/ or lib/" = no file matches `.session.delete(`/`.deleteMany(`. **New:** two assertions — "Session deletes exist only in lib/db/class-sessions.ts (the engine and R5's deleteManualSession)" (exactly one file matches, and it is that one), and "Every session delete passes the pristine predicate: not closed, no attendees/payments/settlement/ratings, no share override, no reschedule link" (every `session.delete`/`deleteMany` call there spreads `STILL_PRISTINE` or `NO_OVERRIDE` or uses `deleteManualSession`'s `pristineWhere`; `UNTOUCHED` pins SCHEDULED, `closedAt: null`, no attendees/payments/settlements/ratings, no reschedule link either way; `NO_OVERRIDE` adds both cut fields null; `pristineWhere` is `NO_OVERRIDE` for a class session and `UNTOUCHED` + `classGroupId: null` for a one-time session — whose cut is its own, not an override of a class's). Closed sessions stay undeletable; `check:one-off-sessions` also asserts it by direct call.
- *Fixture/null-safety only, assertion unchanged:* `check:catalog` "Centre A's own session-list read resolves the same subject rename through the class" — `sessionListRowsA[0]?.classGroup.subject` → `sessionListRowsA[0]?.classGroup?.subject` (the relation is nullable since R5, so the old expression no longer typechecks).
- No other assertion was edited. `check:class-periods`'s pure pristine fixture predates `isManual`; the field is optional in `sessionDisqualifiers`, so the fixture needed no change.

## R6 — Class & session dialogs: sectioned layout, searchable pickers, one time control, class-with-slots, inline new teacher (delivered)
**Goal:** the class and session dialogs are quick to fill and hard to get wrong — one wide sectioned shell, a searchable teacher/class picker, one time control that accepts what people type, weekly rows in the create dialog, and a new teacher created inline — all over features that already exist.
**Build:** **Shared shell** (`components/shared/dialog-shell.tsx`): `DialogShell` (wide `max-w-4xl`, body scrolls between a fixed header and footer; full-screen sheet below `sm`; one Cancel + one primary, both 44px), `FormSection`/`DialogSections` (titled cards, two columns from `lg`), `DialogLauncher` (a fresh instance per open); used by New class, Edit class, New session, Edit rooms, Reschedule, and the End/Delete-session confirmations. **`ActionBar`/`ActionBarButton`** for the session detail (Reschedule · Edit rooms · Cancel · Delete — same size, destructive in colour only, two columns at 375px). **`Combobox`** (`components/ui/combobox.tsx`, on Base UI's Combobox) + `TeacherPicker`: type to filter by name **and** phone (`lib/picker-search.ts` → `lib/phone.ts`'s new `phoneMatchesQuery`/`phoneSearchDigits`, any format, Arabic digits), keyboard, "no results", clear; also the class picker in New session. **`TimePicker`** + `TimeRangeFields` replacing every native time input (class rows, New session, Reschedule, the schedule builder, the three teacher-portal request forms): hour/minute/AM-PM popover with 00/15/30/45 quick picks and a typeable field parsed by `lib/time-input.ts` (`4`, `4pm`, `16:30`, `4:30 pm`, Arabic digits, ص/م; invalid → inline error, nothing posted; output `"HH:mm"`), the end suggested from the start. **Weekly rows** in the New class dialog (`WeeklyScheduleRows`; at least one, overlap on a weekday rejected by `weeklySlotsError`, the live `newClass` conflict preview and `SlotConflictNotice` inside the schedule card). **`createClassWithSessions`** now also validates the rows; **`createClassWithNewTeacher`** + `previewNewClassSessions` (`lib/db/class-sessions.ts`); **`addTeacherMembership`** extracted to `lib/db/teacher-membership.ts` — the one teacher-creation path, called by `createTeacherAction` and by the class transaction (`lib/db/identity.ts` now relative-import and client-injectable); `NewTeacherFields` shared by the Add teacher form and the class dialog. **Edit class** is a dialog on the class page (the inline "Class record" card is gone); the class page's weekly-slot editor stays. **Not here:** the Add student/Add teacher dialogs' own redesign and the detail pages (**R10**), the reception page (**R7**), any schema change (there is none).
**Done when:** New class at 1280px shows basics, teacher, rooms, period, schedule and pricing with little or no scrolling and at 375px is a full-screen sheet with a sticky footer and no sideways scroll; a teacher is found by part of a name or a phone in `+20…` / `0100…`; every time field is the `TimePicker` and stores `"HH:mm"`; a class with two rows creates class, schedules and all sessions in one step; a class with a brand-new teacher creates both, a forced class-step failure leaves no teacher row, and a duplicate phone shows the match; the session buttons are one size and wrap cleanly.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:class-creation` (`scripts/class-creation-check.ts`, in `package.json` and CI) — see `00-master-product-spec.md` §2.1.C; every other `check:*`, `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint .`, `npx prisma validate` and `npm run check:rtl` pass.

**Decisions taken in R6 (for review).** (1) **Edit class carries no weekly-row editor:** an existing class edits its slots on its own page exactly as before (each slot resyncs the period); only *New class* collects rows, so there is one row editor per path and `updateClassWithSessions` is untouched. (2) **"Privileged" for the inline new-teacher flow** means a platform administrator or a holder of another role at *this* branch; it is refused up front (the parent-link `isPrivilegedPhone` is broader — any active membership anywhere — and would have refused every teacher of another branch, contradicting "a duplicate phone links"). The Add teacher dialog keeps its M3 behaviour. (3) A person who already teaches here is returned as `alreadyTeacher` with their identity and the form offers to **select** them, rather than silently creating the class with them. (4) A bare typed hour ("4") reads as the meridiem of the value being edited, else on the 24-hour clock; a leading zero or an hour above 12 is always 24-hour.

## R7 — Reception desk: layout, session-first check-in, teacher names, end session from reception, scanner reliability (delivered)
**Goal:** the reception desk is built around "which session am I admitting students to?" — find-a-student on top, today's sessions below as cards, a session you can attend and scan students into with one confirm, teacher names everywhere a session is offered, End session from the card, and a scanner path that tolerates what scanners actually send.
**Build:** **Layout** — *Find a student* full-width at the top (autofocused; refocused after every completed action), *Today's sessions* below as a card grid (stacked below `md`, 3-up at `lg`): name, teacher, time, rooms, Live / Upcoming / Ended / Auto-closed badge, checked-in count (Present + Late, **one grouped query**); order Live → upcoming by start → ended; a quick filter row and a client-side name/teacher filter. **Session mode** — `?session=<id>`, re-validated every request (`validateReceptionSession`); sticky "Attending: …" banner (Change session / Exit); a scan or typed student → confirm panel (photo, name, code, enrolled or drop-in, price, payment method defaulting to the last used this visit) → one Enter/tap through the existing `checkInAction`; already-checked-in / blocked / inactive / not found / another branch's card each get their own message; a "Recent check-ins" list (last 8) with the running count and a roster link, refreshed by `revalidatePath`. **Student-first flow** — unchanged in shape, but every session reads "name · teacher · time", closed/cancelled/rescheduled are not offered, and exactly one open enrolled session is preselected (still needs the confirm; with none nothing is preselected and the submit is disabled). **End session** — the card reuses R2's `EndSessionButton` (gained `disabled` and `onClosed`) → `endSessionAction`; on success it opens the receipt (R2's `SessionReceipt`, fed by `loadSessionReceiptView`) in the shared dialog with Print (the R2 staff print route); the card flips to View receipt by soft navigation (`?receipt=`). **Data layer** — `lib/db/reception.ts` (`02-architecture.md` §11). **Scanner** — see findings. No schema change; no new permission.
**Done when:** at 1280px find-a-student is on top with the cards below, at 375px everything stacks; selecting a session and scanning or typing a student checks them in with one confirm, photo shown, Present/Late automatic, payment through the existing path, input ready for the next; a duplicate scan, a blocked student, another branch's card and an unknown code each show their own message and write nothing; every session picker shows the teacher; a receptionist on default grants can end a live session from its card, see and print the receipt, after which the card shows View receipt and every check-in control for it is gone; a raw handheld-style burst resolves to the right student in the gate.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:reception` (`scripts/reception-check.ts`, in `package.json` and CI) — see `00-master-product-spec.md` §2.1.C; every other `check:*`, `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint .`, `npx prisma validate` and `npm run check:rtl` pass. **No assertion was edited.** The strict `parseStudentQrPayload("1001") === null` assertion in `check:people` is kept as it was: a bare code is *not* a card payload, so the tolerant reader is a second export of the same module (`parseScannedStudentInput`) rather than a change to the first.

**Scanner root causes (investigated before fixing).**
1. **The parser only accepted a perfectly clean string.** `parseStudentQrPayload` was `trim().split("|")` with an exact `KAYEDU` prefix: any scanner prefix/suffix (an AIM symbology id such as `]Q1`, STX/ETX, a configured tag), invisible or bidi characters, full-width or Arabic-Indic characters, or a lower-case prefix returned `null` — and the page then ran the same string through the fuzzy text search, which answered "no students match" with no hint that a *card* had failed. A bare student code could never resolve as a card at all. **Fixed:** one cleaner (`cleanScannedText`: NFKC, `lib/digits.ts`, control/invisible characters dropped, whitespace controls → spaces) and one reader that finds `KAYEDU` anywhere in the string and takes the id and code after it; `parseScannedStudentInput` classifies the input as payload / bare code / unreadable / text. The payload format is untouched.
2. **A card from another branch was indistinguishable from "no match",** and nothing proved the answer did not depend on whether the code exists elsewhere. **Fixed:** the centre id is compared first, case-insensitively, before any lookup; the refusal is one message, identical for a real and an invented code.
3. **Enter could select the wrong student.** In `TypeAheadSearch` a *hover* set the highlighted suggestion (`onMouseEnter`) and typing did not clear it, so a resting mouse over the panel plus a scanner's Enter navigated to a stale suggestion instead of submitting the scan. **Fixed:** only the arrow keys highlight, any new character clears the highlight, and Enter selects only an arrow-key highlight. This is a change to the shared component, so it applies to every search box (hover still styles a row; click still selects).
4. **A submit did not cancel the debounce.** Enter arrives inside the 300 ms window, so the pending search still fired after the scan — a spinner and a late result panel over the next screen. **Fixed:** a submit clears the timer, invalidates any in-flight request and closes the panel. The plain GET form already submitted the input's own DOM value (so the stale-state hazard was not present there); session mode's submit is written to read `inputRef.current.value` for the same reason, asserted statically.
5. **Reception checked nobody against `StudentBlock`** (only the roster's add-student paths did), **accepted a session id that was not today's**, and **a simultaneous scan at two desks surfaced the unique-key violation as an error page.** **Fixed:** `checkInAction` refuses a blocked student through the shared rule, re-validates the session (today in the centre's timezone, open), and treats the race as "already checked in".
6. **"Today" on the desk was the UTC day** (`startOfUtcDay(new Date())`): between 00:00 and 02:00/03:00 Cairo time the page listed *yesterday's* sessions. **Fixed** on the desk (`receptionToday`, since folded into the one helper — see R7b below, which swept every other "today" reader).
7. **The picker preselected the first session unconditionally** (`sessions[0]`), a one-tap wrong check-in. **Fixed:** nothing is preselected unless exactly one open session is on the student's enrolments.

**Keyboard-layout garbling — what a scanner sends under an Arabic layout.** A keyboard-wedge scanner sends key *positions*; the OS turns them into characters with whatever layout is active. Under an Arabic layout the letters of `KAYEDU` and the hex letters of the centre id therefore arrive as Arabic letters (on the standard Arabic 101 layout `K A Y E D U` are expected to come out as `ن ش غ ث ي ع`, and the shifted forms differ again — **not verified on real hardware**), while the digits arrive as ASCII or as Arabic-Indic digits depending on the layout (both are folded). **The full payload therefore cannot be read reliably under an Arabic layout, and this build does not claim it can.** What it does: a payload-shaped scan whose prefix is unreadable is reported as "that card couldn't be read — make sure the keyboard language is English and scan again" instead of "no student" (and is never matched on the code alone, because student codes restart at 1001 in every centre); a bare **digits-only** code — typed, or from a numeric-only scan — works under any layout. The durable fix is a layout-independent payload, which changes every printed card: backlog entry 19. Until then the desk's scanner should be used with the English layout active, or configured to a layout-independent mode (serial/CDC or Alt-code emulation).

**Decisions taken in R7 (for review).** (1) **The receipt opens through the URL** (`?receipt=<id>`, soft navigation, the receipt server-rendered inside the shared dialog) rather than a Server Action returning JSX: no unverified runtime path, no `window.print` on a dialog — Print opens R2's A4 print route. Only a *closed session on today's list* can be opened this way; the URL is untrusted. (2) **An empty Enter confirms** an open confirm panel (the "one Enter" the brief asks for); a scan or typed value always replaces an open panel and removes it at once, so an Enter in the gap cannot confirm the previous student. (3) **Cancelled and rescheduled sessions no longer appear on the desk at all** (the rail used to list a rescheduled session); a *closed* session stays as an Ended card because that is where View receipt lives. (4) **A bare numeric code is tried as a student code first, then as a phone/name fragment**; a name fragment matching several students lists them to pick from. (5) **The card's End control is shown disabled before the session starts** (with the reason) and hidden without `session.close`; Start attending is hidden without `attendance.record`. (6) Money snapshots are asserted through the shared helpers both entry points call, plus a static assertion that the reception actions import no money arithmetic — the Server Action itself cannot be called outside Next, so it is covered by static assertions rather than by direct call.

## R7b — "Today" is the centre's calendar day everywhere (delivered)
**Goal:** R7 fixed the UTC-day bug on the reception desk only. Between 00:00 and 03:00 Cairo time (+03:00 until the late-October DST end, +02:00 after) every other page still showed *yesterday*. One helper now owns "today", and every reader resolves against the **centre's** `CenterSettings.timezone`. No schema change, no migration, no money logic, no stored data touched.
**Build:** **The helper** — `lib/scheduling.ts` `todayInTimezone(timeZone, now?)` returns the centre's calendar day as the UTC-midnight marker `Session.date` is stored as (so it filters and compares directly); the offset comes from `Intl`/IANA data, never a constant. Beside it: `centreTimeZone(settings)` (the one settings→zone read, with the unknown-zone fallback), `commonTimeZone(zones)` (the one combined-value rule), `startOfSessionDay`, `deviceTodayKey` (cosmetic only). R7's `receptionToday` is **removed** (folded in, not re-exported). **Range helpers** — `weekRangeUtc`/`monthRangeUtc`/`lastNDaysUtc`/`monthToDateUtc` keep their meaning but take a day *marker* from the helper and read no clock; `resolveSharedDateFilter(filter, timeZone, now?)` takes the zone as a required argument so a caller cannot fall back to a UTC day by omission. **Visibility floor** — `visibilityWindowFloor` counts back from the centre's day. **Call sites** — see the list below.
**Done when:** at 00:30 Cairo the centre dashboard, sessions list, analytics/expenses defaults, teacher and student schedules and the visibility floor all use the Cairo date; at 23:30 Cairo they still use the same Cairo date (not tomorrow); no file outside `lib/scheduling.ts` derives a calendar day from UTC.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:timezone` (`scripts/timezone-check.ts`, in `package.json` and CI; pure, no database) — 00:30 and 23:30 Cairo under both offsets, either side of exact midnight, the DST-end and first-of-month rollovers, a second centre east (Dubai) and west (New York) of Cairo, the shared-filter presets, the visibility floor, the teacher dashboard's per-centre anchors, the rating pop-up's per-centre window (through a stub database), and a static sweep (allowlist: `lib/scheduling.ts` only) whose own rules are first checked against known-bad and known-good snippets. Every other `check:*`, `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint .`, `npx prisma validate` and `npm run check:rtl` pass. **No assertion was edited** — fixture shape only: `check:visibility` gained `timezone` on its three settings stubs (the context type now requires it) and `check:reception` calls `todayInTimezone(TZ, now)` where it called `receptionToday(now, TZ)`.

**Call sites changed.** Centre dashboard (`dashboard/page.tsx`); sessions list (`sessions/page.tsx` — filter, floor, both `todayIso`s); analytics and expenses pages (filter, floor, `todayIso`; the expense form's default date now comes from the server instead of the browser's UTC clock); session detail (`dateKey`); the reception desk (`lib/db/reception.ts`); the shared date-filter resolver; `visibilityWindowFloor`; student schedule (per-centre day in `loadCenterSchedule`); parent page (the selected child's centre); teacher dashboard (`teacherDashboardAnchors`, **per centre**) and teacher sessions list; the student rating pop-up's 7-day window (`attendedSessionsToRate`, per session's centre); the student profile's absence streak; the two "Printed on" dates (session sheet, student paper print); the teacher edit-deadline action (now measured from the centre's start of day, `startOfSessionDay`, not UTC midnight); the lifecycle callers that hand-built a "today" marker from `zonedClock(...).dayKey` (`closeEndedSessions`, `createPeriodSettlement`, `room-sets`, `class-sessions`'s clock); the validation round-trip in `class-period.ts`; the demo seed. **`now` is injectable** on every changed function: `todayInTimezone`, `resolveSharedDateFilter`, `visibilityWindowFloor`, `attendedSessionsToRate`, `teacherDashboardAnchors`, `loadReceptionSessions`, `closeEndedSessions` and `loadCenterSchedule` (new parameter).

**Deliberately left, with the reason.** (1) `lib/db/sessions.ts` `startOfUtcDay(next.date)` — it normalises a *stored* date marker, not a clock; no "today" involved. (2) `lib/db/platform.ts` adoption trend — platform-wide monthly buckets over centre `createdAt` instants; there is no centre whose day it could be. (3) `DatePicker`/`Calendar`'s fallback when a caller passes no `todayIso` (`deviceTodayKey`) — the browser's *local* date (it was the UTC date), used only for the "today" ring and the month the calendar opens on; nothing is submitted or filtered from it. Every filter bar and the expense form pass the server's centre-day. (4) Instant arithmetic that is not a calendar day: the 7-day reservation cooldown, invite expiry, `joinedAt`/`resolvedAt` stamps. (5) Gate fixtures in `scripts/` that build their own `startOfUtcDay(new Date())` data (self-consistent; the static sweep scans `app`, `lib`, `components` and `prisma` only).

**Decisions taken in R7b (for review).** (1) **The rating pop-up's window is [today − 6, today] in each session's own centre** — the old rule (`date ≥ now − 7×24h`, `date ≤ now`) is the same set of days at midday and excluded today's sessions between 00:00 and 03:00 Cairo. (2) **The "Whole Management" analytics roll-up shares one range across branches**, resolved once against the *active* branch's timezone; each branch's own query is still scoped by its `centerId` (rule 1). (3) **The student schedule's one combined value — the date picker's "today" ring — uses `commonTimeZone`**: the zone all of the student's centres share, otherwise the platform default (Africa/Cairo); each centre's own upcoming/past split uses that centre's day. (4) **The teacher edit deadline moved from UTC midnight + N hours to centre midnight + N hours** — it is the one behavioural change in the sweep that is not a straight "same value, right day" fix: a 48-hour deadline now closes three hours *earlier* in summer (two in winter) than it did, because UTC midnight is 03:00 (02:00) Cairo time.

## R8 — Global catalog: platform-admin editing, bilingual centre renames, suggestion queue removed (delivered)
**Goal:** the catalog stays global, but a centre that needs an entry **contacts us** and the **platform admin adds it directly** — no suggestion queue. Centres keep picking entries and renaming them for themselves, now in **both languages**. Reverses the suggestion half of M13. No money logic touched.
**Build:** **Schema** — `CenterCatalogSelection.displayName` → `displayNameAr` + `displayNameEn` (both optional); `CatalogSuggestion` and `CatalogSuggestionStatus` dropped (`CatalogEntryKind` stays — the selection's `kind` still uses it; the RLS script held no line for either table, so there was nothing to remove there). Migration `20261016090000_r8_catalog_admin_bilingual_names`, applied with `prisma migrate deploy` only and verified with a read-only `migrate diff --from-url` (empty) — never a shadow database (CLAUDE.md rule 7). **No production data exists** (the shared dev database held 0 suggestions and 0 renamed selections), so the backfill is a faithful rule, not a rescue: each non-blank old value goes to `displayNameAr` if it contains an Arabic-script character (U+0600–06FF, 0750–077F, 08A0–08FF, FB50–FDFF, FE70–FEFF), else to `displayNameEn`. **Label resolution** — `lib/catalog.ts`'s `catalogLabel`/`catalogLabelWithSelection` is the one resolver (Arabic UI: `displayNameAr` else `nameAr`; English UI: `displayNameEn` else `nameEn`; never across languages); every `selections` select is now `CATALOG_OVERRIDE_SELECT`. **Platform admin** — `lib/db/catalog-admin.ts` is the only writer of a global row (create, edit names/parent/`defaultFixedCenterShare`, deactivate, reactivate, delete), re-checking `isPlatformAdmin` on every call and writing `platform.catalog.*` audit rows with before/after in the same transaction; both names required (trimmed, 1–80), case-insensitive duplicate refusal per parent (English and Arabic each, inactive siblings included), delete only when unreferenced (otherwise refused with counts and Deactivate offered). `/admin/catalog` is now the management screen (tabs, type-ahead search, add/edit dialog with side-by-side English/Arabic fields, usage counts, inline deactivate/delete confirmations). **Centre** — `/settings/catalog` loses every suggest control; adds two rename fields (canonical names as placeholders, clearing reverts), a "No longer offered" badge, and the copy-only hint "Can't find what you need? Contact us"; renames and price changes are audited (`catalog.selection.update`). **Deactivated entries** leave every picker (`scoped().x.list({ active: true })` now means pick-active **and** entry-active), the Discover filters and the "add" list, are refused as a *new* assignment on every write path (class create/edit, student create/edit/move/promote, the student's own profile, one-time sessions, teacher class requests and the centre directory) while an existing record keeps the one it has, and keep rendering everywhere through the resolver. **Seed** — a few subject selections carry Arabic-only, English-only and both-language overrides.
**Done when:** a centre cannot write any global row and cannot create a suggestion (no UI, no model, no action) by direct call; a platform admin can add, edit, deactivate and delete entries with both names and a referenced entry cannot be hard-deleted; renaming a subject for the centre in Arabic shows only in the Arabic UI while the English UI keeps the canonical (or English-override) name, and another centre is unaffected; nothing in code, messages or docs (this file's historical notes aside) still refers to the suggestion model or the single-name column.
**Gates:** `npm run check:catalog` extended (see below); `check:payments` passes **untouched**; every other `check:*`, `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint .`, `npx prisma validate` and `npm run check:rtl` pass. The expected gate change, listed old → new:
- *Suggestion submitted → appears in the admin review queue* → **removed** (no queue); replaced by *the generated client has no `CatalogSuggestion` model* and *the database has no such table or enum*.
- *Approving a new-entry suggestion succeeds* / *approval creates a real `CatalogSubject` with the proposed names under the proposed parent* → **removed**; replaced by *a platform admin can add a subject under a grade / an education system with its default cut*, stored with both names and audited `platform.catalog.create`.
- *The suggestion is marked ACCEPTED with a resolver and timestamp* → **removed**; replaced by the audit-row assertions (create, update, deactivate, delete: actor, no `centerId`, before/after).
- *Approving an already-resolved suggestion is refused* → **removed**; replaced by *deactivating twice is refused (`alreadyInState`)* and *deleting what no longer exists is `notFound`*.
- *Rejecting a suggestion succeeds* / *a rejected suggestion never creates a canonical row* → **removed**; replaced by *every role's direct global write is refused as `forbidden` and changes nothing*.
- *Centre B's suggestion queue holds only its own rows* → **changed** to *Centre B's catalog selections hold only its own rows, none carrying Centre A's renames*.
- *Centre A's own view reflects its rename + price* / *Centre B's selection is untouched* → **changed** from the single name to `displayNameAr`/`displayNameEn`.
- *`catalogLabel` renders the Arabic/English name with no override* → **kept**; *`catalogLabel` prefers a centre's display-name override in either locale* → **changed** to *each language's own override in that language* (plus the cross-language and blank cases, new).
- *Centre A's class-list / session-list / Discover reads resolve its rename (en)* → **changed** to resolve the English rename in `en` and the Arabic one in `ar`; *Centre B's read shows the canonical name* → **kept**, now asserted in both locales.
- **New:** both names required (empty, whitespace, over 80, exactly 80 after trimming); case-insensitive duplicate refusal per parent (and not across parents; deactivated siblings count; self-save is not a duplicate); delete blocked per reference kind (selection, class, one-time session, student, child) and allowed when free; deactivate hides from pickers/filters/new picks while class list, session list and Discover still resolve; the migration backfill — `backfillLegacyName` over 13 samples **and** the migration file's own `UPDATE` statements run over the same samples in a temp table, which must agree; static scans (no suggestion identifier anywhere, nothing reads the dropped column, no code outside `lib/db/catalog-admin.ts` writes a global row, every exported write starts with the admin check, every admin action calls `requirePlatformAdmin()`).
- Fixture-shape only: `check:one-off-sessions` builds its subject fixtures with `displayNameAr`/`displayNameEn` instead of the single name.
**Decisions taken (for review).** (1) **Moving a grade/subject to a different parent is allowed** from the edit dialog (typos happen), re-running the per-parent duplicate rule; it changes no centre's pick. (2) **The teacher *history* filter keeps its existing `{ active: true }` meaning, which now also hides an entry the platform deactivated** — the teacher sessions grade filter cannot filter by a retired grade; the centre class/session list filters were left showing every entry. Flagged rather than widened. (3) **Promoting students may move them *out of* a deactivated grade, never into one.** (4) **A centre can still deactivate or remove its pick of a deactivated entry** (existing rules); the "add" list never offers it again.

## R9 — ID-card editor fixes + styling options; Users & Roles shows centre staff only (delivered)
**Goal:** the card editor is trustworthy to drag and rich enough to brand a card (text style, a custom branch text, card colours), and Users & Roles stops listing teachers, who already have their own page. **No schema change** (everything is inside the existing `cardLayout` JSON); the static QR payload and its parser are locked and untouched; no money logic touched.
**Build:** **Part A** — (1) *Drag from anywhere on an element.* The old editor drew a small label chip per element, so only the chip could be grabbed. The preview is now `renderIdCardSvg(…, { annotate: true })` — each element's markup wrapped in a `<g data-card-el>` — and a transparent drag target is laid over each element's measured bounds (`getBBox`), so a logo, a photo or a text is grabbed by any part of it. Pointer capture on the target, a 4px threshold (a press without travel is a click that selects; past it, a drag that moves), logical `insetInlineStart` so Arabic needs no special case, arrow keys nudge. (2) *Stable selection — root cause.* The card container cleared the selection from its `onClick`. After a drag the element has moved out from under the pointer, so `pointerdown` and `pointerup` landed on different elements and the browser dispatched the `click` to their common ancestor — the card — which deselected, and the properties strip vanished on release. Selection now changes only on pointer-down on an element, pointer-down on empty card space, or Escape; never on release, click, blur or hover. (3) *Text style* (size, weight, alignment, colour, font) for the four text elements, with an optional text-box width for alignment to work in. (4) *Custom branch text* (blank = real name, which is the placeholder; max 60). (5) *Card colours*: background, an optional header band, the bottom line — swatches (theme colour first), a colour input, a reset, and a non-blocking WCAG AA warning. (6) *One render path* — preview = print = stored file. (7) `cardLayoutSchema` extended strictly (`#RRGGBB` only, fonts from a closed list, text ≤ 60, bounded sizes, unknown keys refused, no CSS strings); `resolveCardLayout` stays the single resolver and old layouts resolve and render byte-for-byte as before. (8) Below `md`: the stacked list, now one collapsible row per element with the same controls (position included); drag stays desktop-only; the live preview is shown at every width. **Part B** — Users & Roles lists, invites and re-roles Owner / Manager / Receptionist / Accountant only: data-layer filters (`lib/db/staff-filter.ts`), TEACHER absent from both dropdowns, and refused by direct call in `createInvitationAction` / `changeMemberRoleAction` (and in the activate/deactivate action). The permissions matrix keeps its Teacher column. **Seed:** Center 60's Nasr City branch gets a styled card layout (solid background, header band, custom bottom line, custom branch text, per-text style); Downtown keeps the default card.
**Done when:** dragging a logo, a photo or any text by any part of it moves it; a press without travel selects and the strip stays until empty card space, another element or Escape; a text's size / weight / alignment / colour / font show in the preview, in print and in a generated card, in both locales; custom branch text replaces the name and blank falls back; background, accent and footer colours apply; a poor pairing warns without blocking save; an old saved layout renders exactly as before; Users & Roles lists no teachers, offers no TEACHER role, and a direct action call assigning TEACHER is refused; the permissions matrix still has its Teacher column.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:card-layout` (wired into `package.json` and CI) — see `00-master-product-spec.md` §2.1.C for what it asserts; every other `check:*` (the existing assertions all unchanged), `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint .`, `npx prisma validate` and `npm run check:rtl` pass.
**Decisions taken (for review).** (1) *"Accent/header colour" = an optional header band* (the card had no other accent surface); off by default, so nothing changes until a centre turns it on. (2) *Fonts: a closed list of three* — only Cairo is a web font the app loads (the other loaded font, Geist Mono, has no Arabic glyphs), so the list is Cairo plus a system sans and a system serif, both of which ship Arabic and Latin. (3) *The branch text is one string for both locales.* (4) *The fallback name is `Center.name`* — what the card used before; `Center.nameEn` is not consulted. (5) A pending TEACHER invitation is hidden from Users & Roles but can still be revoked by direct id and still accepted from its link — "existing invitations are untouched".

## R10a — Design foundation: shell, language toggle, dashboard, analytics, approvals, auth, list consistency, nav cleanup (delivered)
**Goal:** one coherent look across the shell and the non-detail screens — **presentation only**. **No change** under `prisma/`, `lib/db/`, `lib/money.ts` or `lib/auth/`, to any server action's logic, any zod schema, any message *key's* meaning or any gate assertion. The detail pages and the add-student / add-teacher dialogs are **R10b** and were not touched. Survey first: `06-design-pass-survey.md` (written before any code, one entry per page).
**Build:** (1) **`LocaleToggle`** (`components/shared/locale-toggle.tsx`) replaces the two-segment `LocaleSwitcher` everywhere (app shell, auth layout, 404): a circular button showing the code of the language you would switch *to*, same route **and query string** (the old links were `href={pathname}` and dropped the query), via next-intl's existing `Link locale=` — no second mechanism. (2) **Shell:** sidebar nav grouped (Front desk / Academics / Money; `NavGroups` messages), icon rail shows hairlines instead of headings, drawer's close button moved onto the brand row; scrims use the sidebar token. (3) **`PageHeader`** wraps its actions (end-aligned from `sm`, wrapping under the title below); `ListHeader`, `FilterBar`/`FilterField`, `AuthCard`, `PasswordField` are the new shared parts (docs/04 §5, §12); nine copies of the list-header row and eight hand-labelled filter fields now use them. (4) **Dashboard / KPI:** `StatCard` lost its `accent` prop — no green/amber/red/blue top borders (semantic colours are for status only, docs/04 §2); Today's sessions card gives its vertical padding to its rows (hairline-separated); `StatGrid` fills every row at 375 / 768 / 1280 (the third card spans the row at `sm`; five-up is a six-column track). Ten `CardContent … pt-6` (double padding) removed. (5) **Analytics:** one filter row — labelled `DateFilter` (the only date control) and a labelled `Select`, one baseline. (6) **Approvals:** the empty state links to Teachers; the two actions are an equal-width pair on all four request queues.
