(7) **Auth:** every screen on `AuthCard`; password show/hide; the branch you are already in is marked on `/select-center`. (8) **Centre initials:** three screens drew `name.charAt(0)` instead of `BrandAvatar` — fixed. (9) **Bare muted "empty" sentences** (settlements, reception recent check-ins, parent, admin centres) are `EmptyState`s. (10) **Nav:** the one `disabled: true` item (`/teacher/analytics`, "Soon") is removed; the `disabled` field and the "Soon" rendering are gone from `nav.ts` / `nav-item.tsx`.
**Done when:** the circle toggle is on every shell and auth page, shows the other language's code, keeps route and query, and exists once in code; no dashboard tile or filter is coloured green/amber/blue decoratively and Today's sessions has even padding; no KPI row has an orphan at 375/768/1280; Analytics has a single aligned filter row, one date control and no native select; no nav item is "Soon" or disabled for any role; zero diff under `prisma/`, `lib/db/`, `lib/money.ts`, `lib/auth/`.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:design` (`scripts/design-check.ts`, in `package.json` and CI's `static` job — no database): one locale toggle and no other locale-switch; no disabled nav; no native `<select>`; no hex / arbitrary / palette-scale colour class outside a justified allowlist; no `StatCard` accent; `PageHeader` on every non-auth/print page; the survey covers every page; sidebar, header and switcher take one `brand.name` and nothing hand-draws an initial. Every existing `check:*`, `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint .`, `npx prisma validate` and `npm run check:rtl` pass unchanged.
**Decisions taken (for review).** (1) *The generic-word rule in `centerInitial` was kept* (`مركز النور` → `ن`): it is a recorded decision (docs/04 §5 "Avatar"), and the sidebar/header/switcher already used one name — the disagreement was the three `charAt(0)` screens. Say if the sidebar should show the first letter literally instead. (2) *Nav sections reorder one item*: Approvals moved up beside Reception and Sessions ("Front desk") so each group is contiguous. (3) *`StatCard` accent removed outright* rather than restricted — every use was decorative. (4) *A one-card KPI row stays full width* (the receptionist's dashboard "Collected today") — there is nothing to pair it with without moving a metric.

## R10b — Add student / Add teacher dialogs and the four detail pages (delivered)
**Goal:** the last piece of the design pass — **presentation only**. **No change** under `prisma/`, `lib/db/`, `lib/money.ts` or `lib/auth/`, to any server action's logic, any zod schema, any message *key's* meaning or any gate assertion. Survey first: `06-design-pass-survey.md` (the R10b entries, written before any code). Behaviour, permission gates and lifecycle gating (a closed session, a deactivated class, a role without permission) are unchanged.
**Build:** (1) **Initials** — `lib/initials.ts` is now one rule: the first letter of the name as displayed, in whichever locale, no leading-word skipping (`مركز النور` → `م`). No existing gate asserted the old rule. (2) **Add student / Add teacher** are the R6 `DialogShell` with titled `FormSection`s (docs/04 §11.19): Person · Grade · Parent contact · Photo (a note) for a student, one wide Person card for a teacher; the phone leads and the duplicate-match panel sits directly under it (`ContactFields`' new `belowPhone` slot); "Use this person" is the panel's own solid button and the dialog's primary is held back while a match waits; both forms submit through `startTransition` so a refused submit keeps what was typed. (3) **Detail pages** — `PageHeader` gained `back` / `leading` / `meta`; `ActionBar` gained `inHeader`; new shared `DetailLayout` + `DetailCard` (main two thirds, side one third, one column below `lg`), `FormGroup`, `PersonAvatar` and `DeleteRecordCard` (docs/04 §5 "Detail page"). Class: header with period and rooms + **Edit class**, schedule and roster in `main`, Key facts and Sessions in a sticky `side`. Session: all five verbs are one header action bar, roster in `main`, details and receipt in a sticky `side`. Teacher and student: header with avatar and badges, one grouped record form, a side column, and Delete moved out of the form into a danger zone at the foot. (4) **Mobile**: one column, the action bar directly under the header (two columns of 44px buttons), the danger zone last. (5) New message keys in both locales; `Classes.context` re-worded.
**Done when:** Add student and Add teacher open as wide sectioned dialogs, full-screen sheets on a phone, with the match panel directly under the phone field; the four detail pages use the header + main/side layout with no stranded half-empty column at 1280px and one clean column at 375px; no behaviour, permission or lifecycle gating changed; zero diff under `prisma/`, `lib/db/`, `lib/money.ts`, `lib/auth/`.
**Gates:** `npm run check:design` extended (docs/00 §2.1.C): the four detail pages and both dialogs use `PageHeader` / `DetailLayout` / `DialogShell`, no native `<select>` and no hardcoded colour in any file, the initials rule is *run* over Arabic and Latin names, Delete is out of the edit forms, the session verbs keep their gates. Every other `check:*`, `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint .`, `npx prisma validate` and `npm run check:rtl` pass unchanged.
**Controls whose place changed (none dropped):** *Back to list* — header action → `PageHeader`'s `back` link (all four). *Class:* Edit class — loose header row → the header action bar; the Active badge → `meta`; the kept-sessions warning — inside the Sessions card → full width under the header. *Session:* Reschedule / Edit rooms / Cancel / Delete — bottom of the side facts card → the header action bar; End session and its hint — a strip between header and body → the first button of the bar, hint as muted text under the header; Print receipt (closed) stays in the receipt card. *Teacher:* Delete — the edit form's footer → a danger zone at the foot of the page; the trusted-teacher switch stays in the one form, under its own heading. *Student:* Print ID card / Print paper form — the ID-card panel → the header action bar (the stored-card *Generate* stays in the panel); Delete — the edit form's footer → a danger zone; the code badge and Active badge → `meta`.
**Not done, and why (for review).** (1) **No new data.** The brief's layout lists sections the pages do not have data for today — a class's *upcoming sessions* list and *requests/approvals*, a teacher's *upcoming/recent sessions* and *students*, a student's *attendance and payment history*. Showing them means new reads (and, for money, a decision about who may see them), which is not presentation; they are not built. The class page still shows its kept sessions, and the sessions count. (2) **No photo field in Add student.** The create action takes no file and the upload needs an existing profile; the dialog has a Photo card that says so instead of a control. (3) **The class roster sits in `main` beside a sticky `side`**, not in a full-width row, so the brief's structure holds; if a very large roster makes the sticky column feel wrong, the answer is `DetailLayout`'s `below` slot. (4) **The branch cut on the class page is shown only to a role with `center.profit.view`** (the receipt's rule), though the Edit class dialog already shows it to anyone who can manage classes — gated conservatively rather than assumed. (5) The two teacher-portal detail pages (`/teacher/sessions/[id]`, `/teacher/students/[id]`) are marked R10b in the survey but are not among the four pages this pass was asked for, and were not touched. (6) `ContactFields` now leads with the phone on every form that uses it (including the student's own profile-completion screen) — a consequence of the shared component, not a separate change.
**Decisions taken (for review).** (1) *Initials show the literal first letter* — `النور` shows `ا`, because the article `ال` is not skipped either; say if that should change. (2) *Delete moved out of the forms* into one `DeleteRecordCard` rather than being left beside Save; it posts only the record id and locale, which is all either action reads. (3) *`startTransition` submit* for both add forms — the old `<form action>` was reset by React 19 after every refused submit.

## P1 — Nav fix at the root, realistic demo dataset, safe dev wipe, placeholder cleanup (delivered)
**Goal:** the demo world is trustworthy to click through — every role sees the nav it should — and the dev database can be reset and re-seeded safely by its owner. **No schema change, no migration, no change to `can()`, the ceilings, `lib/money.ts` or any money logic.**
**Root cause (diagnosed first, read-only — `npm run diagnose:nav`).** Logging in as Center 60's Owner or a branch Manager showed only *Dashboard*. Evidence from the live dev database: both Center 60 branches had **0 `PermissionGrant` rows** (70 of the 70 baseline rows missing at each; settings and payment methods were present). The old `prisma/seed.ts` created its centres with a bare `prisma.center.upsert` + `centerSettings.upsert` + `ensureDefaultPaymentMethods` and never wrote the grants that `createCenterWithSettings` (Super Admin provisioning, M12) does; the M12 migration's backfill only reached centres that existed *then*. The matrix is deny-by-default (docs/02 §6.1), so with no rows every gated nav item is hidden and only the ungated Dashboard remained — for the Owner (its non-ownership actions are stored rows too), the Manager, the Receptionist and, for its gated items, the Teacher. The other four hypotheses were tested and cleared: (2) every centre nav item already matched its page gate (two other mismatches were found and fixed, below); (3) the R10a nav filter drops nothing the permissions allow — with the baseline in place the Owner and Manager get 12/12 items, the Receptionist 4/4, the Accountant 3/3; (4) the Owner's org-level widening reaches Nasr City correctly — it just landed on a branch with no grants, the same root cause; (5) both branches were ACTIVE. So this was a data defect from a second, divergent way of creating a centre, not a permissions-logic defect.
**Build.** (1) **`provisionCenterBaseline(tx, centerId)`** (`lib/db/provision.ts`) — settings, the `DEFAULT_ROLE_GRANTS` rows and the default payment methods, idempotent and non-destructive; `createCenterWithSettings` is refactored onto it (one transaction) and the seed uses it; `centerBaselineGaps` reports what a centre lacks and **nothing backfills existing centres silently**. (2) **Nav ⇄ page-gate alignment** (docs/02 §6.2): `components/shell/nav.ts` now takes its permission from the page's own gate; two differences fixed — teacher *Classes* (nav had none, page requires `changeRequest.create`) and the settings *Permissions* link (nested under `center.settings.edit`, page asserts only `permission.manage`). (3) **`lib/auth/relations.ts`** — the pure relation resolution lifted out of `tenant.ts` (behaviour unchanged) so scripts can run the real thing. (4) **`scripts/diagnose-nav.ts`** (read-only). (5) **`scripts/wipe-dev-data.ts`** + `npm run db:wipe-dev` — `TRUNCATE … RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE` over the `public` application tables in one transaction; refuses unless `ALLOW_DEV_WIPE=yes` **and** the project ref in `DATABASE_URL` (and `DIRECT_URL`, and the Supabase API URL) equals `DEV_PROJECT_REF`, prints the target host and table count, requires the person to type the project ref at an interactive prompt, and refuses if any table outside `public` has a foreign key into one it would truncate. It never touches `_prisma_migrations`, the `auth` / `storage` schemas, RLS settings or Supabase-owned objects. Run by the user, never by Claude (CLAUDE.md rule 7). (6) **Seed rewrite** (`prisma/seed.ts` + `prisma/seed-data/`) — see below. (7) **`scripts/seed-verify.ts`** + `npm run seed:verify` (read-only). (8) **Placeholder cleanup** — see below. (9) `docs/dev-accounts.md` (its accounts table is regenerated for the configured domain by `npm run seed:docs`). (10) **`scripts/seed-preflight.ts`** + `npm run seed:preflight` — run it BEFORE the wipe: it checks that `SEED_PASSWORD` is ≥ 6 characters, that `DEV_PROJECT_REF` matches `DATABASE_URL` / `DIRECT_URL` / the Supabase API URL, that the service-role key works, that no seed address already belongs to an auth user the seed did not make, and then creates ONE probe auth user (`probe-<random>@<SEED_EMAIL_DOMAIN>`, with the seed's own password), reads it back and deletes exactly that id in a `finally`; it prints PASS or FAIL with Supabase's exact error text and lists up to 20 other auth users (emails only). The seed runs the same check as its very first step, before any database write. The email domain is `SEED_EMAIL_DOMAIN` (default `example.com`; the preflight confirmed Supabase accepts it on the dev project).
**The seed.** Wholly fictional, every sign-in `@example.com`, every password `SEED_PASSWORD` (required, ≥ 6 characters; no default). Auth users are created or reset through the Supabase admin API, server-side, for the seed's own 23-account list only — an auth user not on it is never modified or deleted and is listed in the summary. Each account the seed creates carries a marker (`app_metadata.kayedu_seed`, settable only through the admin API) and only marked accounts are ever reset to `SEED_PASSWORD`: an auth user that already has a seed address but no marker is refused (the preflight fails, the seed will not run) rather than have its password changed. It refuses to run unless `DATABASE_URL`'s project ref equals `DEV_PROJECT_REF` (a database on this machine is also accepted, and then Supabase Auth is skipped entirely — how the seed was dry-run). Every record goes through the function that owns it: catalog rows through `createCatalogEntry` (as the seeded platform admin), classes and their sessions through the R4 engine, extra / one-time sessions through `createManualSession` / `createOneOffSession`, teachers through `createTeacherMembership` (the shared and cross-organization teachers are one `User` with a second `Membership`), attendance and payments through `createAttendance` / `recordPayment` (money frozen by `lib/money.ts` at record time; history uses the scripts-only `historicalBackfill`, then the real `closeSession`, so closed sessions are closed *records*), live absent-switches through `changeAttendanceStatus` (payment voided in the same transaction, a `SessionAttendanceChange` row), settlements through `createPeriodSettlement` / `approveSettlement` / `paySettlement`, reservations through `reserveOrEnroll` / `confirmReservation` / `rejectReservation`, parent links through `originateParentLinks` / `verifyParentLinkPlaceholder`. The only direct writes are rows with no writer in `lib/db` (rooms, holidays, expenses, catalog picks, change requests), in the exact shape the app's own actions write them. Idempotent **and resumable**: every phase finds what it would create and skips it, and the long ones make progress per item — a finished session is left alone, one the run was interrupted inside is picked up where it stopped (existing roster rows kept, missing ones and their payments added, nothing ever deleted), and settlements, expenses, reservations and change requests are each found before being written — so a run that dies half-way is simply run again. Tested by killing the seed at eleven different moments (three of them inside the history phase, one inside a single session), finishing it, and checking that `seed:verify` passes with no duplicate users, classes or active payments, every past session closed or cancelled, and every present/late attendee paid. **Dataset:** a platform admin; a bilingual catalog (Egyptian National — Primary 1–6, Preparatory 1–3, Secondary 1–3; American — Grades 9–12; IGCSE — Years 9–11; 3 systems, 19 grades, 71 subject rows, each system with a `defaultFixedCenterShare`); organization **Center 60** (Downtown — visibility window 30 days, a custom payment method; Nasr City — 60 days, a styled ID card), an org-level Owner and a Manager / Receptionist / Accountant per branch, six teachers per branch (one at both, one also at Al Nokhba), five rooms with two joinable pairs, ~45 students and ~10 classes each (FIXED and PERCENTAGE cuts; a class on a combined room pair, one `requiresConfirmation`, one not accepting new students, one deactivated with history, one next-term intake); organization **Al Nokhba Academy** (20 students, 4 classes — the isolation test bed; one student studies at both organizations); an ONBOARDING and a SUSPENDED centre with one staff account each; three terms, Egyptian holidays (fixed-date ones exact, Sham El-Nessim computed from Orthodox Easter, Islamic ones labelled "approx."); the last five weeks of closed sessions (≈ 80% present / 10% late / 10% absent, payments across every method, a few live absent-switch voids, one auto-closed and one cancelled session per branch), then today relative to the run — a live session (started earlier today, not ended, partly checked in and paid), an upcoming session when the clock allows, a one-time and an extra session, one rescheduled session; settlements (PAID / APPROVED / DRAFT), expenses (Rent, Salaries, Utilities, Supplies, Marketing), ratings inside the last seven days, reservations (pending / confirmed / rejected inside the cooldown), change requests (NEW_CLASS, NEW_SESSION, and a zero-centre teacher's request to teach), and parents (one with 2 VERIFIED + 1 PENDING child, one whose child is at Al Nokhba).
**Placeholder cleanup (frontend), the full list.** A sweep of `app/` and `components/` for mock arrays, lorem text, sample numbers, fake names, "coming soon" cards and visible TODOs found: (1) **`/teacher/sessions/[id]` "Files" card** — the M7 "uploading files is coming in a later milestone" placeholder → **removed** (card, and `uploads.*` in both message files); (2) **the "Requires confirmation" hint** on the class form still said the discovery/reservation flow was "a later milestone" (it shipped at M8) → **reworded** to what it does; (3) **the ID-card editor's sample student** ("Sample Student", "Grade 10", code 1000) is legitimate → **kept**, now with a visible caption "The preview uses a sample student — it is not a real record." in both languages. Nothing else was found: no mock arrays, lorem text, sample numbers, fake names or TODO text exist in user-visible code (the two `name@example.com` input hints on the auth forms are format hints, not content). Input `placeholder` examples ("Mona", "Kamel", "01012345678") are field hints and stay.
**Done when:** a freshly provisioned centre's Owner, Manager, Receptionist, Accountant and Teacher each see exactly their expected nav; no nav item shows on a permission other than its page's; `createCenterWithSettings` and the seed both call `provisionCenterBaseline`; the wipe refuses without the flag, on a ref mismatch, off a terminal, and on a mistyped ref; the seed's accounts all resolve and `npm run seed:verify` passes (baseline per centre, nav per account, analytics = sum of frozen snapshots per branch, no cross-tenant or orphan rows, counts); no user-visible placeholder remains.
**Gates:** `npm run check:permissions` extended (new part 7 — assertions only, no existing assertion edited: `provisionCenterBaseline` yields the full baseline and is idempotent without undoing a switched-off grant; `createCenterWithSettings` and the seed call it and neither re-implements it, statically; a freshly provisioned centre's owner / manager / receptionist / accountant / teacher get their expected nav through the shell's own functions; the same for the seed's path; the regression — a centre created *without* the baseline shows its Owner only the Dashboard; every nav item matches its page's gate). New `npm run seed:verify` (read-only, run after the seed). Every other `check:*`, `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx eslint .`, `npx prisma validate`, `npm run check:rtl` and `npm run check:design` pass. Fixture-shape change only: none needed.
**The commands for the user, in order:** `npm run seed:preflight` → set `ALLOW_DEV_WIPE=yes` → `npm run db:wipe-dev` (type the project ref) → `npm run db:seed` → `npm run seed:verify` → remove `ALLOW_DEV_WIPE`. Claude did **not** wipe, reset or seed the dev database; the seed and every gate were dry-run against a throwaway local Postgres.
**Decisions taken (for review).** (1) *A class that is already five weeks into its run starts on its first history day, not its term's start* — Term 1 begins 26 September, only 17 days before the seed's "today", so five weeks of history needs a typed start (the class form allows one); the end date is still the term's. (2) *Downtown's 30-day window hides the oldest ~5 days of the 35-day history from centre-side roles* (Nasr City's 60 shows all) — deliberate, it is the window feature on display. (3) *The "live session" is an extra (manual) session whose start is computed from the clock at run time* (today's regular sessions have fixed times, so one could not be guaranteed live); skipped before 00:20 Cairo. One is created per active branch, three in all. (4) *Receipts are backdated by clock stamp only* — `SessionAttendee.recordedAt` and `Payment.recordedAt` are set to the session's own check-in time after the fact (never an amount or a snapshot) so a three-week-old receipt does not read as checked in today. (5) *Existing dev centres lacking the baseline are reported, not repaired* — the wipe + reseed is the repair. (6) The seeded platform admin is `admin@<domain>`; a developer's own Supabase account that was `isPlatformAdmin` in the wiped `User` table comes back as an ordinary user on its next sign-in (its Auth login and password are untouched, but `syncUserFromAuth` creates a fresh blank `User`: no centre, no role) and must be re-flagged by hand if wanted. (7) *A seed address that already exists in Auth without the seed's marker stops the run* rather than being adopted — the alternative (resetting that person's password) is exactly what was ruled out.

## SEC-1 — Security & anti-fraud audit: report and reproductions, no fixes (delivered)
**Goal:** find every way the system can be breached, leak data across tenants or to the wrong person, or be used to cheat a centre, with evidence. **No application code, schema or migration changed.**
**Delivered:**
- `docs/07-security-audit.md`, containing:
  - the attack-surface inventory: 125 Server Actions, no route handlers, id-reading pages, raw SQL, cookies and headers, Supabase clients, uploads and HTML sinks;
  - 75 findings: 1 Critical, 10 High, 20 Medium, 32 Low, 12 Info;
  - a prioritized fix plan;
  - the NEEDS-DECISION register, D-01 to D-28, registered as **OD-11**;
  - a verified-safe list;
  - the live platform state;
  - a list of docs passages the audit contradicts. Those passages are corrected with their fixes, not here.
- `scripts/security-repro/SEC-NN.ts`, one or more scripts per Critical/High plus SEC-12/13. They use throwaway fixtures, clean up after themselves and are not wired into CI.

Every Critical and High was re-confirmed by the lead with `file:line` evidence.
**Done when:** every audit area has findings or verified-safe evidence; every Critical and High is confirmed; the repro scripts run (all print REPRODUCED except SEC-10, which is expected to be NOT REPRODUCED on the hand-locked dev project).
**Next:** the fix milestone or milestones follow `07-security-audit.md` §5. Phase 0 (operations) and Phase 1 (no decision needed) can start immediately. **Do not provision a production or CI Supabase project before the SEC-10 lockdown migration exists.** M20's "RLS applied" step depends on it. *(SEC-2a: the lockdown migration now exists — see below.)*

## SEC-2a — Security fixes, part 1: identity & access, data exposure, platform (delivered)
**Goal:** close the SEC-1 Critical/High findings outside money (SEC-01, 02, 03, 04, 09, 10, 11) and every Medium/Low/Info finding in the AuthN, AuthZ, tenant-isolation/IDOR, files & storage, secrets & config, Supabase RLS, privacy-of-minors and platform areas, under the decisions recorded in `00-master-product-spec.md` §5.1 "Security decisions (SEC-2a)". Money, concurrency and fraud findings are **SEC-2b**.
**Build:**
- **Identity (D-01, D-02).** `lib/auth/identity-link.ts` is the one linking rule: a login is never attached to a staff-created person by email or phone — a matching sign-up opens a PENDING `AccountClaim`, staff approve it in person on `/approvals`, and until then the login has no app user (`/account-pending`). `REQUIRE_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION=true` adds a confirmed-email requirement to every email-based link; a production deployment refuses to start without it, or while Supabase auto-confirms (`instrumentation.ts`). A phone is never an identity key: sign-up stores none, staff can link only through a person's global email (`isLinkableMatch`), and a contact another person holds is never copied onto a new `User`.
- **Parents (D-03 (d), D-04 (a), D-10 (a)).** The staff Verify stand-in refuses; `verifiedChildLinks` re-checks the child's active profile, the centre's lifecycle and the matched phone on every read; a parent-phone change revokes the links it no longer matches (`ParentLinkStatus.REVOKED`, audited).
- **AuthZ.** `roleForExperience` denies instead of falling back; `ctxCan` refuses a denied experience; every `(teacher)` action resolves through `actionContext("teacher")`; TEACHER `student.block`/`analytics.own.view` are enforced; OWNER holds every non-platform action; staff reactivation and invitation revoke are OWNER-only (D-09, D-22); invitation acceptance claims the row conditionally; settlement teacher ids are scoped; `lockAttendeeRow` is centre-scoped in SQL.
- **Lookups (D-08 (b)).** One Postgres-backed limiter (`RateLimitBucket`) behind `lib/rate-limit.ts`, consulted inside `resolveContact` so every name-revealing path is covered, plus sign-in, sign-up and confirmation-resend limits per email and per IP. A match known only outside the organisation is described without a name.
- **Data API lockdown (D-19 (a)).** One migration: RLS on every `public` table, zero policies, nothing for `anon`/`authenticated` now or via default privileges, the four buckets private with their caps. `supabase/m10-rls-and-storage.sql` is deleted. New `npm run check:rls`.
- **Files (D-25 (b), D-27 (b), D-28).** Logos live in the private `logos` bucket and reach the browser only through `/api/logos/[centerId]` (signed URL); uploads are typed by magic bytes (raster only); photos are capped at about 1 MB and shrunk in the browser first; photo changes are audited.
- **Platform & config.** Security headers (CSP report-only; enforcing it was `05-phase-2-backlog.md` entry 22, moved to M20 at SEC-2c), `poweredByHeader: false`, `vercel.json` (fra1), httpOnly Supabase cookies, next 16.3.8, CLIs moved to devDependencies with a CI `npm audit`, `db:migrate` disabled, `SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD` (D-21), a committed production denylist for the seed and wipe, `safeNextPath` hardened, an auth-confirm route and a "check your email" state with a rate-limited resend.

**Delivered:** two migrations — `20261017090000_sec2a_identity_claims_rate_limit` (`User.emailVerifiedAt`, `AccountClaim`, `RateLimitBucket`, `ParentLinkStatus.REVOKED` + `revokedAt`/`revokedReason`, `Center.logoUrl` → `logoPath`) and `20261017090100_sec2a_data_api_lockdown` — applied with `prisma migrate deploy`. `07-security-audit.md` records every finding as FIXED or OPEN with the reason; nine repro scripts were ported into `check:security` and deleted once they printed NOT REPRODUCED (SEC-05/06/07/08/12/13's stay for SEC-2b).
**Done when:** every in-scope finding is FIXED or OPEN with a reason; `check:security` asserts each fixed one as `SEC-NN`; `check:rls` passes on dev; every `check:*` gate, `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .`, `prisma validate`, `check:rtl` and `check:design` pass.
**Gates:** new `npm run check:security` and `npm run check:rls` (both in CI's database job, `check:rls` right after `migrate deploy`), plus every existing gate.

## SEC-2b — Security fixes, part 2: money integrity, concurrency, anti-fraud (delivered)
**Goal:** close the SEC-1 money Highs (SEC-05, 06, 07, 08) and every Medium/Low/Info finding in the concurrency & integrity, fraud/anti-cheat and numeric-bounds areas plus the money-adjacent ones (SEC-12, 13, 18, 19, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 50, 51, 58, 59, 60, 62, 74, and SEC-29's audit-content half), under the decisions recorded in `00-master-product-spec.md` §5.1 "Security decisions (SEC-2b)" (D-05, D-06, D-07, D-12, D-13, D-14, D-15, D-16, D-17, D-20, D-24, D-26). D-18, D-20's retention half (OD-2) and OD-6 stay open.
**Build:**
- **Ledger invariants in the database (SEC-05, SEC-12).** A partial unique index allows one non-voided payment per roster row and `recordPayment` inserts against it (`ON CONFLICT … DO NOTHING` → `alreadyPaid`); `lockAttendeeRow` takes the lock and re-reads the payment fact in a fresh statement; a CHECK keeps void and refund exclusive; void and refund lock the payment and re-read it; a double refund is `alreadyRefunded`, never a thrown unique error.
- **Settlement claims (D-05 (c), SEC-06; OD-8 resolved).** `SettlementClaim` — a settlement claims the payments it counts in its own transaction; one live claim per payment (partial unique); a per-(centre, teacher) advisory lock serializes creation; conditional transitions; overlapping periods are allowed when they claim unclaimed money; a DRAFT can be cancelled, releasing its claims (D-16); a period is strict, ends today at the latest and spans at most 366 days (SEC-23); a non-OWNER cannot approve or pay a settlement they created, an OWNER's self-approval is flagged (D-12, SEC-18).
- **Visibility (D-06 (a), SEC-07, SEC-19).** `/audit` (`audit.view`): voids, switches to Absent, refunds, settlement transitions, price/cut changes and present-but-unpaid check-ins, linked from the Dashboard and Analytics; the receipt nets refunds and counts and names voids, refunds and unpaid attendees (on screen, printed, teacher side).
- **Session lifecycle (D-07 (c), D-13, D-14 (b), SEC-08, 20, 21, 51).** Cancel/reschedule refused while a session holds active money; cancelled/rescheduled sessions close at day end; void/refund refuse them; a status change that voids a payment needs a reason; each session freezes the centre's timezone at creation (trigger) and its lifecycle reads it; reschedule and NEW_SESSION approval refuse a past date.
- **Money configuration (D-15 (c), D-17 (b), D-26 (b), SEC-22, 24, 58, 74).** One cut rule (`lib/money.ts`'s `centerCutError`) refuses a FIXED cut above the price everywhere, a lowered price included; 64-bit SQL sums for every money aggregate; prices capped at 100,000 EGP; strict EGP parsing; a free one-time session needs an explicit confirmation and is flagged.
- **Audit (D-20, SEC-60, SEC-29 part).** One writer (`lib/db/audit-row.ts`) on the money transaction; ids and amounts only (names, contacts, free text refused at runtime and swept statically; platform catalog rows exempt); `AuditLog` append-only by trigger; existing rows untouched.
- **Also:** expenses voided not deleted and backdated within the window only, category changes audited (D-24, SEC-51); one locked seat claim for every enrolment path (SEC-13); strict calendar dates in every typed date (SEC-50); ratings need real attendance inside the window (SEC-59).

**Delivered:** one migration, `20261018090000_sec2b_money_integrity` (the indexes, the CHECK, `SettlementClaim` + backfill, `SettlementStatus.CANCELLED`, `Session.timezone` + backfill + trigger, `Expense` void fields, the append-only trigger), applied with `prisma migrate deploy` after a read-only check found no duplicate data. The eight remaining repro scripts printed NOT REPRODUCED, were ported into `check:security` (concurrency ones with real parallel transactions) and deleted. `seed` drafts settlements as the manager and approves as the owner, adds a cancelled draft and a voided expense, writes audit rows through the app's writer, and — fixing a fresh-seed failure SEC-2a left (the disabled `verifyParentLinkPlaceholder` made a seed on an empty database stop at the parents) — marks its two demo verified parent links directly as fixture data; `seed:verify` checks the new invariants. A full seed + `seed:verify` was dry-run on a throwaway local Postgres. Two `check:session-lifecycle` assertions were edited with the person's approval (the void reason is now required, D-13; a cancelled session now closes at day end, D-07); `check:settlements` and `check:security` gained call-site arguments only (approve/pay take the actor); gate cleanups delete audit rows through `scripts/lib/audit-maintenance.ts`. `check:payments` passes unedited.
**Done when:** every in-scope finding is FIXED or OPEN with a reason in `07-security-audit.md` §1.1; `check:security` asserts each fixed one; every `check:*` gate, `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .` and `prisma validate` pass; `seed:verify` passes after a fresh seed.
**Gates:** `npm run check:security` (SEC-05/06/07/08/12/13/18/19/20/21/22/23/24/29/50/51/58/59/60/74 added), plus every existing gate.

## SEC-2c — Security fixes, part 3: validation, injection, availability, abuse limits (delivered)
**Goal:** close every finding still OPEN in `07-security-audit.md` that no decision blocks — input validation, injection hardening, availability limits, logging hygiene and the Info items worth fixing — plus **D-18**'s per-account and per-IP limits on reservations, self-enrolment and the teach-here request (CAPTCHA stays at M20); take the production dependency upgrades `npm audit` and patch/minor releases allow; and fix the gate problems the SEC-2a/2b reviews found.
**Build:**
- **D-18 (SEC-25, SEC-26).** `reserveOrEnroll` caps an account at 30 PENDING reservations and 30 ACTIVE enrolments when instant-enrolling (`SELF_SERVICE_CAPS`), serialized per account; the Discover action is limited per account and per IP, the teach-here request too. `requestToTeachAction` refuses a teacher who already teaches somewhere and a second PENDING request at the same centre. Each Approvals queue loads one page of 50 and counts the rest. Values and reasons: `00-master-product-spec.md` §5.1 "Security decisions (SEC-2c)".
- **Validation (SEC-49, SEC-52).** Profile completion resolves the grade through the centre's own selection, and the form offers only those grades. The 12 raw-argument actions parse a zod tuple before any query (`lib/validation/action-args.ts`); the teacher type-aheads filter relationally instead of preloading the roster.
- **Injection (SEC-61, SEC-71).** search tokens drop `%`, `_` and `\` (`likeSearchToken` — Prisma's `contains` cannot escape them, probed) and duplicate-name checks compare in memory by the one name key (`nameClashes`, `catalogNameKey`) instead of an insensitive `equals`, which is an `ILIKE`; the card `background` normalised like `accent`.
- **Availability (SEC-32).** Discover loads at most five centres at a time (`lib/concurrency.ts`).
- **Logging (SEC-75, half).** Prisma's minimal error format in production — no call arguments in the server log. `global-error.tsx` moves to M20 with Sentry.
- **Nav.** `/audit` in the centre nav, on `audit.view` — the page's own gate (OWNER/MANAGER by default).
- **Gates.** One gate client (`scripts/lib/gate-db.ts`) for every database gate: per-transaction `lock_timeout`/`statement_timeout` (Supavisor drops connection-level ones), a stall watchdog that names the blocker, cleanup of the users a run created. `npm run check:all` runs CI's list locally; CI checks the `User` count before and after (`npm run gates:users`). `check:people` runs the real `resolveContact`/`describeMatch` instead of its own copy; `check:approvals` computes its request date from today.
- **Dependencies.** Patch/minor bumps (next 16.4.1, react 19.3.1, base-ui, supabase, next-intl, zod, lucide, tailwind-merge); `npm audit --omit=dev` 0; the major bumps are listed in `07-security-audit.md` §1.2 for a decision.

**Delivered:** no migration and no schema change. `07-security-audit.md` §1.1 now reads **69 FIXED, 3 OPEN (SEC-29, SEC-62, SEC-73), 3 DEFERRED to M20 (SEC-36, SEC-66, SEC-75)**, each with its reason; §1.2 reports the SEC-62 options, the `check:one-off-sessions` hang investigation (not reproduced; the gates now fail fast on it) and the major-version bumps. **One assertion edited, by instruction:** the `check:permissions` nav pin (and `seed:verify`'s copy) gains `"audit"` for OWNER and MANAGER. **One gate is red, by instruction:** with its private matcher replaced by the real module, `check:people`'s "a contact that exists only on another center's local record still matches" fails — the real module (since SEC-2a, D-01/D-02) finds that person but never links through another centre's local record. Left as found, for a decision (`07` §1.2). `backlog` entry 22 (enforce the CSP) moved into M20.
**Done when:** every remaining finding is FIXED, OPEN with the decision it waits on, or DEFERRED with its milestone; `check:security` asserts each new fix; every `check:*` gate, `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .`, `prisma validate`, `check:rtl` and `check:design` pass (bar the `check:people` assertion above); the `User` count does not grow across a full gate run.
**Gates:** `npm run check:security` (SEC-25/26/32/49/52/61/71/75 and the SEC-2c gate-infrastructure assertions added), `npm run check:all`, `npm run gates:users`, plus every existing gate.

## SYS — Whole-system workflow & architecture investigation + end-to-end smoke suite (delivered)
**Goal:** trace every role's journey through the real code and prove it in a headless browser; audit layering, single owners, raw SQL, transactions, errors, performance, dead code and docs drift; get the codebase deployment-ready for Vercel with a swappable storage seam — and fix what is clearly a bug on the way. The report is `08-system-audit.md`.
**Decisions applied (user-confirmed):** (1) **a class price edit reprices its open sessions** — sessions dated today or later, not closed, not cancelled, holding no payment, take the new price; a session with any payment keeps its own; recorded payments never change; audited with the counts (`00` §5.1). The class cut needed no change (a payment reads it at payment time). (2) **SEC-62 option (a)** — the visibility-window floor moves into `lib/db/scoped.ts` beside `centerId`, with `scopedIgnoringVisibilityWindow(ctx, reason)` as the one opt-out. (3) Dependency majors (prisma 7, eslint 10, typescript 7, @types/node 26) deliberately **not** upgraded. (4) CLAUDE.md rule 1 amended: browser testing only when a milestone prompt enables it, headless, with playwright-cli or `@playwright/test` — this milestone enables it.
**Build:**
- **Price decision** — `repriceClassSessions` inside `updateClassWithSessions`' transaction (row locks, then a fresh read of the payment fact); `recordPayment` now freezes the price read under its locks (`lockAttendeeRow` returns it); the class form's notice says how many sessions took the new price and how many kept theirs; class-form copy corrected in both locales.
- **SEC-62** — `centreReadFloor`; `scoped()` floors sessions/attendees/attendance changes/payments/expenses, `attendees.absenceStreak`, `settlements.findForSession`, `sessions.findForEdit`, `payments.findActiveForAttendee`; the integrity opt-outs in the student/teacher/room/expense-category delete guards; `scoped.ts` takes its client by parameter (`scopedWith`) with relative imports so the gate calls the real helpers.
- **Storage seam** — `lib/storage` (`provider.ts`, `supabase.ts`, `index.ts`); `lib/db/storage.ts` reaches the provider only through `storage()`; every bucket private, signed URLs only.
- **Bugs fixed from the journeys:** the admin sidebar's missing `Nav.catalog` key (raw key on screen in both locales); the New session dialog's one-time fields read `Classes.*` keys that live under `Classes.fields.*` (ten raw keys); a verified parent landed on the branch hub's "not connected" state with no way to `/parent` (root, sign-in and hub now send them to the parent portal); every dialog was half its width off-centre in Arabic at ≥ 640 px (`start-1/2` + a physical translate); the dialog close button's `aria-label="Close"` was hard-coded English; Cancel session was one irreversible tap (now a confirmation, like End and Delete); the teacher's session-note card said "no edit deadline is enforced yet" (M10 enforces one — the hint now states the branch's setting); the hub's empty state said its title twice; the parent-links hint described verification that is switched off (D-03).
- **Code health:** pages no longer call `prisma.<model>` (nine reads moved into `lib/db`); ten best-effort catches log instead of swallowing (`lib/log.ts`); money/seat/lifecycle transactions all carry `WRITE_TRANSACTION_OPTIONS`; the invite page's inline `Intl` date → `formatDate`; four indexes from read-only `EXPLAIN` (migration `20261019090000_sys_hot_query_indexes`, additive, applied with `migrate deploy`, `migrate diff` empty); 20 provably unused exports and 16 unused message keys removed.
- **Deployment:** `"postinstall": "prisma generate"` (Vercel restores a cached `node_modules`); readiness checklist and the M20 list in `08` §5.
- **Tooling:** `@playwright/test` (dev dependency), `playwright.config.ts`, `e2e/` (support + one spec per journey + navigation + account claim), `npm run e2e`; `npm run perf:queries` (`scripts/query-count.ts`, statements per page request with `KAYEDU_QUERY_LOG=1`); `npm run perf:db` (`scripts/db-timing.ts`, queries and ms per route as the owner, no browser, `KAYEDU_DB_STATS=1`); the env inventory (`.env.example`, `02` §16).
**Gates added (assertions only — no existing assertion edited):** `check:class-periods` section 10 (the price decision: counts, which sessions move, payments untouched, audit row, new payments freeze the new price, a cut-only edit reprices nothing, new sessions carry the price, the locked price read); `check:visibility` SEC-62 block (per centre-side role by direct call through `scopedWith`: out-of-window session/attendee/payment/expense refused by id, lists/counts floored, change log empty, refunds windowed, settlements/audit carved out; TEACHER/STUDENT/PARENT and platform admin unbounded; the opt-out sees everything; the absence streak stops at the floor; static: no raw centre-page reads of those models, the opt-out used only with a stated reason); `check:security` `SYS-STORAGE` (one adapter talks to Storage; only it imports the service-role client; signed URLs only); `check:rtl` "every literal message key resolves" (every `t("…")` against its translator's namespace, and every nav `labelKey`).
**Done when:** every `check:*` gate (incl. `check:security`, `check:rls`), `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:rtl`, `check:design`, `npm run build` and `npm run e2e` pass (or each e2e failure is a listed NEEDS-DECISION); `08-system-audit.md` gives the verdict.
**Gates:** the above, plus every existing gate unchanged.

## P1b — `seed:verify` nav check made structural (delivered)
**Goal:** fix `seed:verify`'s red "OWNER and MANAGER hold every permission their nav items need".
**Root cause (diagnosed first, read-only):** not a missing permission. `audit.view` is in `MANAGER_BASELINE` (= `DEFAULT_ROLE_GRANTS` for OWNER and MANAGER), is written by `provisionCenterBaseline`, and OWNER passes `can()` regardless of rows (SEC-45); the per-centre nav assertions in the same run were green. The failing line was a stale literal: it asserted `EXPECTED_CENTER_NAV.OWNER.length === 12` and tested one fixed permission, and SEC-2c's `audit` item made the list 13 (SEC-2c edited the list and the `check:permissions` pin but missed this count). Hypotheses (1)–(3) of the brief were refuted by evidence.
**Delivered:** no schema change, no `can()` or ceiling change, no baseline change, no migration — so **existing centres need nothing**: no wipe, no re-seed, and re-running `db:seed` is not required. `seed:verify` now derives the check from `CENTER_NAV`: for OWNER and MANAGER every nav item's permission must be in `DEFAULT_ROLE_GRANTS[role]` **and** pass `can()` with exactly that baseline, and the hand-written expected nav must list every nav item. `check:permissions` gains section (f): every nav item a role is *meant* to see (its hand-written expected nav) is in that role's provisioning baseline — OWNER, MANAGER, RECEPTIONIST, ACCOUNTANT — plus the teacher nav against `TEACHER`'s baseline, and OWNER's/MANAGER's expected nav must list every `CENTER_NAV` item. A new nav item with no baseline row now fails a gate. **One existing assertion replaced, by instruction** (the `seed:verify` line above). `scripts/settlements-check.ts` stopped writing a `reason` key into a `settlement.adjust` audit fixture (D-20 forbids it; it used the audit table directly, so the append-only rows it left behind trip `seed:verify`'s D-20 check — see Open items below).
**Open items (data, not code):** `seed:verify` still reports two failures caused by test data left in the shared dev database: 2 historic `settlement.adjust` audit rows with a `reason` key (append-only — cannot be edited or deleted without a reset) and 3 seed users with no relationship (`hana.zaki.res`, `adam.khalil.res`, `teacher.new`). Both clear on the user-approved dev wipe + reseed.
**Done when:** `seed:verify`'s nav check passes; the gates named below pass.

## Post-testing batch, Oct 2026

### T1 — Shell and Settings presentation fixes from manual testing (delivered)
**Goal:** four presentation fixes found in manual testing. Presentation only: nothing under `prisma/`, `lib/db/`, `lib/money.ts` or `lib/auth/`, no server action, zod schema, message-key meaning or gate assertion changed.
**Build:**
1. **Sidebar scrollbar** — `scrollbar-sidebar` utility (thin, `--sidebar-scrollbar` thumb, transparent track, brighter on hover; Firefox + WebKit) on the nav shared by the sidebar, rail and drawer; two new tokens in 04 §2. A matching `scrollbar-neutral` on the popover, select, combobox, type-ahead and time-picker lists.
2. **Header start cluster** — the sidebar toggle and branch switcher sit at the start edge of the main column beside the sidebar (expanded and rail, EN and AR); the end cluster still ends at the content edge via the new `content-edge-end` utility. Phone layout unchanged.
3. **Settings lists** — one shared layout (`components/settings/settings-list.tsx`): Status in its own column, Actions header and buttons end-aligned in one width, fixed button order, stacked cards below `md` (name + badge, fields, full-width action bar, two columns on a phone). Moved onto it: Rooms, Payment methods, Terms, Expenses categories, Staff members, Catalog (education systems, grades, subjects).
4. **Whole Management** — the trigger shows `Layers` + "Whole Management" + the organization; the sidebar brand and mobile bar show the organization name (initial, no logo); Reception / Sessions / Approvals are disabled nav items with a one-line reason (`RollupNavItem`); every other page shows a `RollupNotice` naming the branch whose data it renders. The page-by-page audit is in the T1 summary; the decisions it asks for are the owner's. *(Superseded by T6: every item but Analytics is disabled, the notice is removed, and pages show a pick-a-branch screen.)*
**Done when:** no default light scrollbar on the sidebar, rail or drawer; at 1280 and 1920 the toggle sits beside the sidebar edge in EN and AR, rail and expanded; every Settings list uses the shared layout and nothing overlaps at 375 / 768 / 1280; Whole Management never names a single branch in the shell.
**Delivered:** as above. Gates run: `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl` — no database gates. The "NavItem has no disabled/'Soon' branch" assertion in `check:design` is untouched: the disabled item is a separate component that exists only while a roll-up is the active scope. Docs: 04 (§2, §5, §11.1, §14.3, §14.8), 06 (Shell, Payment methods, Rooms, Terms, Catalog, Staff), this file.

### T3 — Room links are edited on the room (delivered)
**Goal:** no separate "combinations" area; editing a room is where its "Can be combined with" is chosen, and the link shows on both rooms.
**Build:** Settings → Rooms loses its combinations card; **Add room** and **Edit room** are one dialog (name, capacity, "Can be combined with" — removable chips plus a checklist of the branch's other **active** rooms; a link to a since-deactivated room stays as a chip until removed). Save = one action, one transaction (`saveRoomWithLinks`): the link set as a diff (add missing pairs, drop removed ones; pairs stay stored once, so a link made from Room 1 is on Room 2's dialog with no second write). A removal that is in use is refused **before anything is written** — the room's name and capacity included — and the refusal now **names** what uses it (classes; sessions by day and time; the first five of each, the counts stay exact). The list gains a "Combines with" column. `isValidCombination` (star, not transitive) and the in-use rule (active class, or non-closed non-cancelled future session) are unchanged; auditing is `room.link` / `room.unlink`, one row per pair (written by the action after the save commits here; moved into the save's transaction by T7). **Capacity edits keep today's rule: there is none** — a plain update of `Room.capacity`; seats are derived from the room set at read time and enrolment is checked against them when a student is added, so lowering a capacity never touches existing enrolments. New in the data layer: a new link to an **inactive** room is refused (`roomInactive`); the old per-room "Save combinations" form and `setRoomLinksAction` are removed (`setRoomLinks` stays as the link-only entry point).
**Done when:** Room 1's dialog links Room 2 and Room 2's dialog shows Room 1; unlinking works from either side; a link used by a class or a future session cannot be removed and the message names what uses it; no combinations section remains; 375px and RTL fine.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl` run. **Assertions added, none edited:** `check:rooms` section 8 — symmetric visibility (stored once, normalized, listed on the other room), diff add/remove and the no-op diff, unlink from either side, Add room creating room + links together, the in-use refusal (class and session names, nothing saved, from either side; a kept link still saves), inactive rooms (new link refused, existing link kept and removable), self-link, and cross-branch refusal (Add and Edit in Branch B cannot link or touch Branch A's rooms). **Not run** (writes to the database): `npm run check:rooms`. **e2e:** `e2e/owner.spec.ts` "room: add one, then delete it" now adds through the dialog and deletes from the row (it filled the removed inline card) — not run.
**Docs:** 00 §5.1 (joinable rooms), 01 (RoomLink), 02 (§11 and the settings tree), 04 §11.12, 06 (Rooms), this file.

### T2 — Session-page check-in is confirm-then-write; the type-ahead stops reopening; reception gets a Cancel (delivered)
**Goal:** on the session page nothing is written until staff confirm; a picked suggestion stays picked everywhere; the reception desk can be cleared.
**Build:**
1. **One confirm panel** — extracted from reception's session mode into `components/center/check-in-confirm-panel.tsx` (inline on the desk, `variant="sheet"` in a dialog). It owns the confirm: the only caller of `checkInAction` in the UI.
2. **Session page** — the roster's **Check in**, a suggestion in "Add a student" and a candidate row open that panel via `SessionCheckInProvider` (React state only; no Server Action runs, nothing is stored, until confirm). The confirm is `checkInAction` — attendance and payment in one step. The attendance-only `checkInRosterAction` and `addExistingAttendeeAction` (and their schemas) are removed. Registering a brand-new student keeps its own form. Older Present/Late rows with no payment keep **Record payment**.
3. **Behaviour that changed by switching to reception's function** (recorded in 00 §4.1 item 7): `reception.use` is now required alongside `attendance.record`; only **today's** open sessions of an active class can be checked in (a future-dated or deactivated-class session is refused); the roster Check in now also refuses a blocked student (it did not before); a student already on the roster is a success ("already checked in"), not an error; a role without `payment.record` checks in with no payment.
4. **Type-ahead** — a pick (and URL-supplied text) is "settled": no search, no reopen on refocus, late result or a new `search` identity until the next keystroke. Optional `onClear` adds a clear (X) and Escape.
5. **Reception** — once a student is selected, X / Escape return to the default desk (student-first: bare desk; session mode: drops the panel, stays in the session). The scanner path is unchanged.
**Done when:** Check in on the session page calls no Server Action until confirm; Cancel / Escape / navigating away writes nothing; one confirm creates the attendee and the payment through `checkInAction`; after a pick the dropdown stays closed until typing on Reception, session add-student, class enrol and the teacher block lists; Reception's Cancel returns to the default desk; the scanner burst still resolves the right student.
**Delivered:** as above. Gates run: `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl` — no database gates; `check:reception` gained static assertions (its database block is for a later run). Docs: 00 (§4.1 item 7, one line in the M15 guard note), 04 (§11.8, §14.5), this file.

### T4 — Filters, dates, tabs and pagers update in place (delivered)
**Goal:** changing a filter, date, search, tab or page updates the content without reloading the document or jumping the scroll; the URL is still the source of truth. Presentation and navigation only: nothing under `prisma/`, `lib/db/`, `lib/money.ts` or `lib/auth/`, no Server Action, zod schema, message key or existing gate assertion changed.
**Build:**
1. **One mechanism** — `useUrlFilters` / `UrlFilterForm` (`components/shared/url-filters.tsx`) on next-intl's router, in `startTransition` with `scroll: false`; `mergeFilterParams` (`lib/ui/url-filter-params.ts`, pure) merges a submitted form into the query (own fields replace, empty removes, unrelated params kept, `page` resets when the filters changed). `SoftLink` for pager / tab / reset links; `ResultsRegion` + `lib/ui/url-pending.ts` dim the old results while pending (`aria-busy`, opacity only).
2. **Every GET filter form moved onto it** — `FilterBar` (Analytics, Expenses, Sessions), Classes, Discover, teacher Sessions, student Schedule, Parent (child selector, date), and `TypeAheadSearch`'s submit (Students, Teachers, teacher Students and Sessions, and the `?q=` candidate searches on the class, session and teacher pages). `Pagination`, the Audit tabs, the date filter's Reset and the empty-state "Clear" buttons are `SoftLink`s.
3. **Controls follow the URL** — `Select`, `DatePicker`, `DateFilter` and `TypeAheadSearch` re-seed when the URL moves, so Back restores the previous filter.
4. **Left alone, on purpose** — Reception's scan field keeps the full-page GET submit (scanner contract, 04 §11.8); allow-listed in `check:design`.
**Done when:** on every page above a control change does not reload the document (no white flash, scroll kept), the URL updates, Back restores the previous filter, a pasted URL renders the same view, and the forms still work with JavaScript off.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl` run — no database gates. **Assertions added, none edited:** `check:design` section 12 (the merge by running it; the interceptor shape; no bare GET `<form>` and no `method="get"` outside it; the six filter forms and `FilterBar` use `UrlFilterForm`; `TypeAheadSearch` submits through the hook and only the allow-listed scan field opts out; pager, Audit tabs and Reset are `SoftLink`s; `ResultsRegion` is opacity-only; every page with URL-state controls renders it). Docs: 02 (§14), 04 (§5 Filter row, §11.7, §11.8), this file.


### T5 — A void or refund on a live session resets the student; they check in and pay again (delivered)
**Goal:** while a session is live, voiding or refunding a student's payment puts that student back where they were before check-in, and they can be checked in and pay again from the normal controls. A closed session stays immutable. `lib/money.ts` is untouched.
**Why it failed before (reproduced first):**
- `voidPayment` / `refundPayment` never touched the roster row; only "set Absent" reset it.
- Reception's `studentSessionState` and `checkInAction` treated **any** existing row, ABSENT included, as already checked in, and the roster offered Check in only to unmarked rows.
- A refunded payment still held the active-payment slot in three places: the index (`WHERE voided = false`), `lockAttendeeRow` and the `ON CONFLICT` predicate. The roster also showed it as the row's payment, so there was no Record payment form.
- The Absent → Present correction itself worked, in the data layer and for `attendance.correct`. A Receptionist (`attendance.record` only) had no control, and after a refund the re-payment was blocked.

**Build:**
1. **One attendance-change writer** — `lib/db/attendance-change.ts`'s `applyAttendanceChange` (status, `SessionAttendanceChange`, `attendance.correct` audit), used by `changeAttendanceStatus`, the reset and the re-check-in.
2. **The reset** — `voidPayment` / `refundPayment` lock session (shared) → attendee → payment, flip the flag, then `resetAttendanceAfterReversal`: Present/Late → ABSENT, one change row naming the payment (`voidedPaymentId` or the new `refundedPaymentId`), audit trigger `payment.void` / `payment.refund`. The row is never deleted. `historicalBackfill` skips it.
3. **Re-check-in** — `recheckInAttendee` (session → attendee lock, fresh re-read): ABSENT → Present/Late decided by the server, `recordedAt` = now, trigger `attendance.recheckIn`, and the F6 booking is linked if it is not already. `checkInAction` uses it for an ABSENT row, re-checks both block scopes, then records a **new** payment. "Already checked in" now means PRESENT/LATE only.
4. **Refund frees the slot** — migration `20261027190000_t5_void_refund_reset_attendance` creates `Payment_one_active_per_attendee_t5` (`WHERE voided = false AND refunded = false`), drops the old index and renames the new one. It also adds `SessionAttendanceChange.refundedPaymentId` (nullable, SetNull). `lockAttendeeRow`, the `ON CONFLICT` predicate, the receipt's and audit's "unpaid" readers and the roster's row payment all use the same predicate. `Payment_not_voided_and_refunded` is kept.
5. **"Not arrived" and the drop-in rule** — `lib/attendance-rules.ts`: `attendanceDisplay` (a live ABSENT row, or a drop-in's, reads Not arrived) and `EXCLUDE_DROP_IN_NO_SHOWS`.
   - **The display rule is applied in:** the session roster (badge, Check in, a Present/Late correction), the receipt's third count, the teacher session roster and absent count, the teacher's student history, both print sheets.
   - **The drop-in filter is applied in:** `sessionReceiptTotals`, `consecutiveAbsenceCount` (so the roster and profile absence alerts too), `attendanceStatusCounts` (dashboard), `studentPastAttendance` (student and parent), `studentSessionsWithTeacher`.
   - `attendedSessionsToRate` already required PRESENT/LATE (SEC-59) and is unchanged.
6. **Receipt change log** — one timeline per changed student (`sessionChangeLog` / `orderChangeLog`), in causal order: checked in → paid → voided/refunded (amount, reason) → reset → checked in again → paid.

**Done when:** at a live session, a checked-in and paid student who is voided shows **Not arrived**, leaves the checked-in count, and can be checked in and paid again: a second `Payment`, with the first still voided. The same holds for a refund. The change log shows who was voided or refunded, and why. A closed session refuses all of it. A voided or refunded drop-in is never absent anywhere. Revenue and settlements count only the live payment.

**Gates:**
- **Run:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:design` and `check:rtl`. `check:design` has one failure, `app/[locale]/page.tsx renders <PublicHeader />`; that file is not touched here.
- **Assertions added:**
  - `check:session-lifecycle` static section: the ON CONFLICT predicate equals the migration's index predicate; the index swap order; the lock order; one change writer; the display and drop-in rules; every consumer uses the filter; the change-log order under skewed clocks.
  - `check:session-lifecycle` section 7 (database): void and refund reset, re-check-in, re-pay (two rows), refusals, void ∥ re-check-in, booking survives, change-log sequence, closed counts, drop-in exclusions (streak, analytics, student/parent and teacher history), ratings exclude ABSENT, revenue and settlement count only live payments, closed refuses void/refund/re-check-in, cross-centre re-check-in refused, backfill does not reset.
  - `check:security` SEC-05: the index predicate in the database, the predicate match, refund → re-check-in → two concurrent payments ×3.
- **Edited by instruction (old → new):**
  - SEC-07: `unpaidCount` 2 → 1, plus `notArrivedCount` 1, because the void resets the voided student.
  - SEC-08: "no absences" counts enrolled rows only (`isDropIn: false`), because the refunded drop-in now leaves an ABSENT row.
- **Not run (they write to the database):** `npm run check:session-lifecycle`, `npm run check:security`, `npm run check:payments`, `npm run check:settlements`, `npm run check:reception`, `npm run check:student-portal`, `npm run check:parent`, `npm run check:teacher-portal`, `npm run check:analytics`, `npm run check:ratings`, `npm run check:one-off-sessions`, `npm run check:isolation`.

**Docs:** 00 (§5 "Void vs. refund", "Attendance", ledger invariants, §5.1 "Session lifecycle"), 01 (SessionAttendanceChange, SessionAttendee, §3, index list), 02 (folder tree, §11 locking), 04 (Attendance control, Receipt), 07 (SEC-05), this file.

### T6 — Whole Management is analytics-only, enforced on the server (delivered)
**Goal:** while an Owner is in "Whole Management", no page shows or changes a single branch's data until the owner picks that branch. The server enforces it; the UI does not just hide it. Only Analytics works in this mode. Replaces T1's "Showing {branch}" notice.
**Build:**
1. **The guard** — `lib/auth/branch-scope.ts`: `requireBranchScope(ctx)` → `PICK_BRANCH` (`{ error: "pickBranch" }`) under a roll-up, else `null`; `isPickBranch` narrows a scope helper's result. No Next-runtime imports.
2. **Actions** — there is no single choke point, so every `"use server"` file under `(center)` (13 files) calls the guard on the line after each `getTenantContext(`, before permission, validation, read or write. Scope helpers return `Scope | PickBranch | null`, so TypeScript forces every caller to return the refusal. Form-state actions return `pickBranch` (message added to every centre `*.errors` namespace, EN + AR); read-only helpers (type-ahead, phone lookup, slot-conflict preview, reception session mode) return their own empty answer. `selectCenterAction`, sign-out and Account actions are outside `(center)` and untouched apart from item 4.
3. **Pages** — every centre page and centre print page except Analytics returns `<PickBranchScreen ctx={ctx} />` right after its context, before `requirePermission` and any read (`components/shell/pick-branch-screen.tsx`: `EmptyState` naming the organization, one button per branch, "Back to Analytics"). The Dashboard redirects to Analytics; the settings layout renders only the page.
4. **Back to the same page** — `selectCenterSchema` takes an optional `next`; `selectCenterAction` redirects a branch pick to `safeNextPath(next)`, else home as before. The screen posts the current path (`NextPathInput`).
5. **Nav** — `disabledHrefs` under a roll-up is every visible centre item except Analytics (was Reception / Sessions / Approvals). `RollupNotice`, its use in `center-shell.tsx` and `Shell.rollupShowingBranch` are removed; `Shell.pickBranch.*` is added.
6. **No data-layer change** — `lib/db/scoped.ts`, `lib/db/tenant-scope.ts`, tenant isolation and the roll-up analytics (a union of per-branch scoped queries) are untouched. A single-branch Owner and every user without a roll-up are unaffected.

**Done when:** under Whole Management every centre URL except Analytics shows the pick-a-branch screen, and calling any centre action directly returns `pickBranch` with no write; picking a branch on the screen returns to normal operation on that page; nothing in the shell names a representative branch; the T1 notice is gone.

**Gates:**
- **Run:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`. `check:design` still has its one existing failure, `app/[locale]/page.tsx renders <PublicHeader />`; that file is not touched here.
- **Assertions added:**
  - `check:design` §13 (static, the action-guard gate): the guard run on literal contexts; every centre `"use server"` file calls `requireBranchScope` within three lines of each `getTenantContext(` (allowlist with a reason per entry, currently empty; stale entries fail); every centre and centre-print page except Analytics returns `PickBranchScreen` on the line after its context; the Dashboard redirect; the settings layout; `RollupNotice` and its message gone; the roll-up `disabledHrefs` rule.
  - `check:branches` §3b: `pickBranch` for the seeded organization's roll-up and nothing for a branch; an action shaped guard → read → write returns `pickBranch`, reads nothing and leaves both branches' expense counts unchanged under the roll-up, and writes one row at the picked branch only.
  - `check:isolation`: `lib/db/scoped.ts` and `lib/db/tenant-scope.ts` never read `rollupOrganizationId` or the guard (static).
- **No existing assertion edited.**
- **Not run (they write to the database):** `npm run check:branches`, `npm run check:isolation`.

**Docs:** 00 (§4.1 branch switcher), 02 (§4 "Roll-up is analytics only"), 04 (§11.1), 06 (Shell), this file.

### T7 — F-series loose ends: schedule bookings, staff cancel, confirm-when-Off, room audit, sign-up cleanup (delivered)
**Goal:** close the known loose ends from F3–F6. No schema change (a comment on `SessionBookingCancelReason.STAFF` only).
**Build:**
1. **`check:design`** — "`app/[locale]/page.tsx` renders `<PublicHeader />`" became "a signed-out `/` redirects to Explore" (and the public shell is added to the `LocaleToggle` renderers; `check:public` asserts both); the page's `PAGEHEADER_EXEMPT` reason says redirect-only; the three stale hex-allowlist entries for the admin, parent and teacher layouts are gone (no hex left in them).
2. **e2e** — the sign-up helper takes an intent (Student by default; Parent / Teacher) and no longer takes a phone; `student.spec` expects `/student/onboarding` after a Student sign-up, completes the grade, skips the parent invite and goes on to Discover (`?grade=all`); `parent.spec` signs up as a Parent (its phone-match step is gone with the field); `claim.spec`'s declined outcome lands on `/student/onboarding`, not the empty hub. `check:reception`'s stale "stays a plain GET form" title now says what the assertion proves.
3. **Register form** — the optional phone field is removed (stored nowhere and matched nowhere since F3); the schema, the action's comments and `Auth.fields.phone` go with it. A contact phone lives only in Account settings.
4. **Room link audit** — `room.link` / `room.unlink` are written by `saveRoomWithLinks` on the same transaction as the link diff (`writeAuditRow(tx, …)`, `actorUserId` passed in), not by the Server Action after commit (rule 7).
5. **Bookings** — (a) a student's CONFIRMED upcoming bookings appear on `/student/schedule` as **Booked**, PENDING ones as **Requested** (cancelled, rejected, expired and moved do not), and the parent portal reads the same rows for its child: `studentBookedUpcoming` / `childBookedUpcoming`, read-only; (b) **staff cancel** — `cancelBookingByStaff`, reason `STAFF`, from the session page's "Booked — not arrived" list under `approvals.manage` (`cancelBookingAction`) and from the teacher's own session page under `reservation.manage.own`, pinned to the teacher's classes (`cancelOwnBookingAction`); the seat frees in the same write, `sessionBooking.cancel` is audited in the same transaction (ids only), and the student reads **Cancelled by the branch** in My requests; (c) `confirmBooking` refuses `bookingOff` when the class's mode is Off at confirm time; confirmed bookings are unaffected and a pending one can still be rejected.
**Not built, on purpose:** the optional **note** on a staff cancel — `SessionBooking` has no text column and an audit row may not carry free text (D-20), so storing it needs a schema change (backlog entry 31). The teacher's own session page had no "Booked — not arrived" list, so it gained a read-only one (name, code, Booked badge) to carry the teacher's Cancel button.
**Done when:** a signed-up Student reaches onboarding; the register form has no phone field; a confirmed booking shows on the student's schedule; staff can cancel a booking and the seat frees; confirm after Off is refused; the room-link audit rows commit with the link change.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design` (no failures), `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `check:account` run. **Assertions added** (database; **not run**): `check:rooms` §8b (audit rows per link / unlink, none for a no-op or a refused save, a rollback after the diff leaves neither link nor row — the same-transaction proof, plus a static check that the action writes neither); `check:student-portal` T7 (schedule shows confirmed and pending only, pending without a profile, expiry, parent read per branch; staff cancel frees the seat, is audited once with ids only, rolls back with its transaction, refuses another branch / a pending booking / a closed session / a second cancel; a teacher cancels only their own class's booking; confirm refused when Off, reject still works, a confirmed booking stands); `check:approvals` (the teacher pin on cancel, the booked list); `check:enrollment` (a staff-cancelled booking frees the seat for an enrolment); `check:isolation` (another branch cannot staff-cancel). **Existing assertions edited:** none in a database gate — the room-audit timing is new coverage (no earlier assertion pinned the after-commit timing); `check:design`'s `<PublicHeader />` assertion and `check:reception`'s title are the two "old → new" changes the prompt listed. **Not run (write to the database):** `npm run check:rooms`, `check:student-portal`, `check:enrollment`, `check:approvals`, `check:isolation`; e2e not run.
**Docs:** 00 (§4.3 item 1, §5.1 "Single-session booking" and "Joinable rooms"), 02 (§11.1), 04 (§11.25), 05 (entry 31), 06 (register, session pages, student schedule, parent), this file.

### F2 — Account settings for every role (delivered)
**Goal:** every signed-in person — owner, manager, receptionist, accountant, teacher, student, parent, platform admin — opens Account settings from the user menu and manages their own account.
**Build:**
1. **One route, every shell** — `/[locale]/account`; each experience's shell became a frame builder (`(group)/*-shell.ts(x)`) its layout and the account page both render; `resolveAccountExperience` picks it from the menu's `?from=`. The user menu gets **Account settings** above Sign out; the branch hub gets the same link.
2. **Profile** — `User.firstName`/`lastName`, new **`User.contactPhone`** (migration `20261026090000_f2_account_contact_phone`, additive; not unique, never an identity key, normalized by `lib/phone.ts`), and **`User.locale`** as the preferred language (sign-in now redirects into it). Read-only "How {branch} has you on file" cards; the account never writes a branch record.
3. **Password** — current password (Supabase password sign-in on a throwaway client, rate-limited per account and per IP: `accountVerifyPerUser` 5/15 min, `accountVerifyPerIp` 30/15 min), new + confirm under the sign-up policy, "Sign out other devices" (default on, `scope: "others"`); audited `account.password.change`.
4. **Email** — current password first; refused for an address another login/user holds, or a staff-created person's ("ask that branch" — never a claim). `ALLOW_UNVERIFIED_EMAIL_CHANGE=yes` → admin API, confirmed; `REQUIRE_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION=true` → Supabase's email-change link; neither → read-only. `User.email` changes only in `syncLinkedUser`, now audited `account.email.change` (ids only). Loud start-up warning; production refuses the override beside `REQUIRE_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION=true`.
5. **Extension points** — `StudyDetailsSection` (F4) and `FamilySection` (F3), empty.
**Out of scope:** forgotten password (M20), delete account (OD-2), a personal profile photo (built at F8, removed at U9), 2FA (backlog entry 12).
**Done when:** every role reaches Account settings from the user menu at 375px and in AR; a wrong current password refuses both changes and is rate-limited; an email held by someone else or by a staff-created person is refused; without the flag email is read-only; `contactPhone` is read by no matching/linking/refusal function; an account rename moves no branch record.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:design`, `check:rtl` and the new no-database **`check:account`** run (also added to CI's static job). **Assertions added, none edited:** `check:account` (new); `check:security` **F2** block (wrong password ×2, flag off, email collision, staff-created email + no claim, direct change through the sync with its audit, per-account limit, password change + sign-out-others audit, confirmation branch, rename moves no branch record, `contactPhone` normalized); `check:people` (a `User.contactPhone` is never found by the add-person lookup). The two database gates are for a later run. `findPeopleByContact`'s local parameters `contactEmail`/`contactPhone` were renamed `recordEmail`/`recordPhone` (no behaviour change) so the identity modules never name the field.
**Docs:** 00 (§4.6, §5.1), 01 (User), 02 (§2 tree, §3, §16), 04 (§5 user menu, §11.20), 06 (Account page, shell), this file (F2, M20 checklist), `deploy-vercel.md`, `.env.example`.

### F1 — Branch limits, owner Add branch, admin organizations, branch-aware invites (delivered)
**Goal:** the platform admin decides how many branches each organization may have; an Owner adds branches up to that limit; the admin can add a branch to an existing organization; inviting staff and adding a teacher can target a specific branch. Closes **OD-12** with options (a)+(b); **OD-3** (what a branch costs) stays open — the limit is the only lever.
**Build:**
1. **Schema** — `Organization.maxBranches Int @default(1)`, `CHECK (>= 1)`; migration `20261026100000_f1_branch_limits` (additive) backfills `max(1, non-ARCHIVED branch count)`. Counted branches = non-ARCHIVED centres.
2. **One provisioning path** — `lib/db/platform.ts`'s `provisionBranch` (the only `center.create`: the row, then `provisionCenterBaseline`), used by `createCenterWithSettings` (new organization, its own name and limit; the pre-F1 call shape still founds a one-branch organization named after the branch), `addBranchToOrganization` (admin, existing organization, no invitation) and `lib/db/branches.ts`'s `addBranchAsOwner`. The limit is enforced under `lockOrganizationBranches` (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the organization row, then a fresh count).
3. **Platform admin (OD-12 (a))** — the Create form's "New organization / Add to an existing organization" toggle (searchable organization pick with its usage; `branchLimitReached` until the limit is raised); `/admin/organizations` and `/admin/organizations/[id]` (branches with lifecycle badge and counts, links to `/admin/centers/[id]`, the limit editor — `updateOrganizationBranchLimit`, audited `platform.organization.limit.update`, `centerId` null, before/after numbers), reached from a **Centers & branch limits** button on `/admin/centers` (no sidebar item — adding one would change `seed:verify`'s and `e2e/navigation.spec.ts`'s expected admin nav).
4. **Owner self-serve (OD-12 (b))** — *removed at U5 (OD-12 re-decided "platform admin only", 29 Oct with Kamal); its "Copy setup from" moved to the admin's Add to an existing organization. Kept as the record:* Settings → Branches, gated on the new OWNER-only `organization.branch.create` (`OWNER_ONLY_ACTIONS`): "N of M branches used", the branch list with lifecycle badges, Add branch (disabled with the reason at the limit). The dialog: Arabic name, English name, theme colour (swatches from `lib/branch-setup.ts`), timezone (default the current branch's), "Copy setup from <branch>" with six ticks (catalog picks with renames and base prices, payment methods, terms, holidays, the permission matrix, general settings). `addBranchAsOwner` re-resolves the actor, takes the organization from the acting branch, refuses a source outside it, gives the new branch the acting branch's lifecycle state, picks a unique slug from the English name, copies (`copyBranchSetup`) and audits `organization.branch.create` — one transaction. Never copied: rooms, classes, sessions, people, money, ID-card layout, logo. The whole locale layout is revalidated, so the switcher and `/select-center` show it at once.
5. **Branch-aware people adds** — Settings → Staff **Invite** and Teachers → **Add teacher** get a Branch select (`branchesWithPermission(…)`: the branches where the actor holds `membership.role.assign` / `teacher.manage`), hidden with one branch, default the active branch. The actions re-resolve the target with `getTenantContextForBranch` (the ordinary relation resolution + `buildContext`), check the same permission there and write through `scoped()` on it; the Add teacher phone lookup follows the chosen branch. A success at another branch names it. D-22 is unchanged.
6. **Seed** — Center 60 `maxBranches` 3 (two branches, one free slot), Al Nokhba and both test centres 1; a re-run resets a seeded organization's limit.
**Done when:** an Owner at the limit can't add a branch (UI and direct action call); under it, the new branch appears in the switcher with the ticked setup copied and nothing else; a Manager can't reach Settings → Branches or the action; the admin can create a new organization, add a branch to an existing one, and change the limit (audited); inviting staff or adding a teacher to another branch works only where the actor holds the permission there, and the row lands in that branch.
