**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:design`, `check:rtl` run. **Assertions added, none edited** (the one old → new the prompt allows — provisioning "creates a new organization, or adds a branch to an existing one within its limit" — needed no existing assertion changed; `check:admin` §8 states the new form): `check:branches` §4 (two concurrent adds at limit-1 → exactly one; at the limit a direct call is refused; the ceiling refuses a MANAGER; another customer's organization is unreachable by acting or by copying; the ticked setup is copied and rooms, classes, sessions, people, invitations, money, card layout and logo never are; nothing ticked = the baseline; archived branches don't count; the schema; static: one provisioning path, organization from the acting branch), `check:admin` §8 (new organization with its name and limit; add to existing under / at / after raising the limit, no invitation; unknown organization; slug taken; invalid limit; lowering below the count; the audit row's shape; the organization list and per-organization branch list; the create schema's two modes), `check:permissions` §8 (OWNER-only by the ceiling, never a cell or a baseline row; link, page and action assert it; one `center.create`), `check:isolation` §6 (an Owner resolves `membership.role.assign` at a sibling branch and nothing at another customer's; a MANAGER resolves nothing at an ungranted branch, `teacher.manage` at a granted one — checked against *that* branch's matrix — and never `membership.role.assign`; a teacher added for the sibling lands only there; static: the actions use `getTenantContextForBranch` and the target context). **Not run** (they write to the database): `npm run check:branches`, `npm run check:admin`, `npm run check:permissions`, `npm run check:isolation`.
**Docs:** 00 (§2 locked decisions, §2.2 OD-3 and OD-12 removed as decided, §4.1 item 13, §4.4, §5.1 "Branch architecture"), 01 (Organization), 02 (§2 tree, §4, §6, §6.2), 04 (§11.1, §11.21), 06 (three new pages, Staff), 07 (§2.1 rows, §2.5), 08 (§3 row 3), `dev-accounts.md`, this file.
### F3 — Family links: parent onboarding with a one-time code (delivered — closes OD-6)
**Goal:** a real parent can get access to their child safely, at any time, even before the child joins a branch; a student can't hand access to a classmate by typing a friend's number; staff see and can revoke every link.
**Build:**
1. **Schema** — migration `20261026110000_f3_family_links` (additive): `FamilyLink` (parent `User` ↔ student `User`, no `centerId`; `ACTIVE | REVOKED`; source `STUDENT_INVITE | CENTER_CODE | LEGACY_VERIFIED`; unique pair; not-self CHECK) and `FamilyInvitation` (`codeHash` sha256 only — a CHECK refuses anything else — `codeHint`, source, `issuingCenterId`, the declared parent, `PENDING | ACCEPTED | REVOKED`; one PENDING branch code per (student, branch), a partial unique index); RLS on both. Backfill: every VERIFIED `ParentLink` → one ACTIVE `FamilyLink` (`LEGACY_VERIFIED`, deduplicated); PENDING not migrated. `ParentLink` stays, deprecated, read and written nowhere.
2. **`lib/db/parents.ts` rewritten** — issue (student invite / branch code), regenerate, cancel, accept (rate-limited 10/h per account and per IP; one transaction with a conditional PENDING → ACCEPTED; `family.link.create`), revoke (branch with reason / parent / platform admin — no student path; `family.link.revoke`), `activeChildLinks` (the only parent-portal read: ACTIVE links, the child's active profiles at accessible branches). `lib/family-code.ts` — the alphabet (no `0 O 1 I L`), normalizer (on `lib/digits.ts`) and display form. Removed: `originateParentLinks`, `isPrivilegedPhone`, `verifiedChildLinks`, `revokeStaleParentLinks`, `verifyParentLinkPlaceholder` and its button/action/schema; sign-up, the student edit and profile completion touch no link.
3. **Account → Family** (F2's extension point) — student view (parents; pending codes with New code / Cancel; Invite a parent → the code once, Copy, Share on WhatsApp; 3 slots), parent view (children with their branches, Unlink, Link another child), both for a brand-new account, none for a platform admin. Actions in `app/[locale]/account/family-actions.ts`.
4. **`/[locale]/family/join`** (`(auth)` group, guests too) — the code read from the URL fragment, kept in the tab across sign-in, stripped from the address bar; every refusal its own message. Linked from the hub's empty state ("I'm a parent: link my child"); the hub also links a staff member's or teacher's own children.
5. **Student page "Parent access"** — replaces "Parent accounts": active parents (account name, how linked, date, the declared parent), slot count, the waiting branch code, **Generate parent code** (shown once in a dialog, **Print** → a bilingual slip) / Replace / Cancel code, **Remove access** with a reason; `parent.link.manage`.
6. **Parent portal** — zero children → "Link your child" (a code field that hands off to `/family/join`; still no Server Action under `(parent)`); a child at no branch yet is named; one selector option per child per branch.
7. **Seed** — Hanan's two children through a student invite and Nasr City's code; Ashraf's Farida through Al Nokhba's code (both branches show); two waiting dev codes `SEED-PRNT` / `SEED-BRCH` (`docs/dev-accounts.md`).
**Decisions recorded:** OD-6 decided (00 §5.1 "Parent role & portal", incl. the residual risk); the M16 privileged-phone refusal superseded (staff and teachers may be parents; platform admins refused); D-04 (a) retired; D-10 (a) kept. Relinking after a revocation re-activates the same row (the prompt's unique pair). The platform-admin revoke has its data function but no screen (backlog). "Signed up as a student" is tested as "holds any profile, a pending code, or a link as a child" until F4's sign-up intent exists.
**Done when:** a student invites a parent who registers later and links, and the child's branches appear in the portal; a branch-printed code works for a student with no account; a student account can't accept a code; two accepts of one code make one link; the student can't revoke an active parent, staff and the parent can, and reads stop at once; seeded families still work; codes exist only as hashes.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:design`, `check:rtl` (and the static `check:account`) run. Data-free gate data added: `check:design`'s auth-screen PageHeader exemption (the join page) and its silent-catch allowlist (the join form's blocked-`sessionStorage` fallback). **Database gates updated, not run:** `check:parent` (rewritten, with only the prompt's old → new changes: "a phone match writes nothing", "a platform admin is refused; staff/teacher accepted with a valid code", "only explicit, audited revocation ends a link", "a pending invitation grants zero reads"; the M16 placeholder's three verify checks went with the placeholder) plus new: hash-only storage, single use under concurrency (two clients), every refusal, revocation ends reads, the student can't revoke, the rate limit, no `"use server"` under `(parent)`; `check:security` SEC-03 / SEC-04 / SEC-16 / SEC-17 (SEC-04 and SEC-17's phone-change steps → "only explicit revocation ends a link"); `check:isolation` new §7 (another branch and a sibling branch read and revoke nothing); fixture-only changes in `check:catalog` (its PARENT actor) and `scripts/lib/gate-db.ts`; `seed:verify`. `e2e/parent.spec.ts`'s known-gap journey became the code journey — not run.
**Docs:** 00 (§2.2 OD-2/OD-6/OD-11, §3, §4.3, §4.5, §4.6, §5.1), 01, 02 (§2 tree, §3, §6, §7), 04 (§11.4, §11.20, §11.22), 05, 06, 07 (SEC-03/04/17), 08 (§3 row 1), `dev-accounts.md`, CLAUDE.md (rule 6), this file.

### F4 — Student onboarding, account grade, safe promotion (delivered)
**Goal:** sign-up asks who you are and sets you up; a student keeps their own grade on their account; a branch's bulk promotion becomes previewable, partial and undoable, and never touches anything outside that branch's record.
**Build:**
1. **Schema** — migration `20261027090000_f4_signup_intent_account_grade_promotion` (additive): enum `SignupIntent`, nullable `User.signupIntent` and `User.gradeId` (FK `CatalogGrade`, set null), `GradePromotionBatch` and `GradePromotionItem` (CHECKs: undone both-or-neither, From ≠ To; RLS on both). No backfill — every existing account keeps null.
2. **Sign-up intent** — three required radio cards on the register form (a parent arriving from a join link has Parent pre-ticked); `signup_intent` rides in the auth metadata and `resolveAppIdentity` stores it; `lib/auth/onboarding.ts` + `landingWithoutBranch` route a person with no branch: Student → **Your studies** (`/student/onboarding`: education system → grade, then an optional Invite a parent — F3's action — then Discover), Parent → the parent portal's Link your child, Teacher → the directory, none → the hub as before. The hub's empty state puts the person's own start first. A STUDENT intent counts in `isStudentAccount` (a refusal, never a grant).
3. **Account grade** — Account → Study details (F2's extension point, now filled) writes `User.gradeId` only; Discover's grade filter is pre-selected from it (`All grades` is the explicit `grade=all`). `ensureStudentProfile` seeds a **new** profile with the account grade (when the branch offers it) and the latest declared parent (F3), and stamps `profileCompletedAt` when both a grade and a parent phone are present — no second form; an existing profile is never touched.
4. **Branch record** — `/students/[id]` shows **Account says: <grade>** beside the grade when it differs, with **Use account grade** (`student.manage`, audited `student.grade.account`); `/students` gets a **Grade differs from account** filter chip (URL state, `SoftLink`).
5. **Promotion** — `/students/promote` (`student.promote`; Manager baseline, Owner always): From → To (To only grades on offer; **no order is invented** — the catalog has none, backlog entry 27), a preview of every active student in the From grade (all ticked, search, select all/none, "Account says", active enrolments with "Enrolment is kept"), one confirm → one transaction: grades for the ticked rows still active and still in the From grade, the batch and its items, one `student.grade.promote` audit row. **Undo** the latest not-yet-undone batch for 14 days (banner on `/students`, history on `/students/promote`), reverting only students still in the To grade, audited `student.grade.promote.undo`. The one-click `promoteGradeAction` and `scoped.students.promoteGrade` are removed.
**Out of scope:** a catalog grade order and a "next grade" pre-selection (backlog 27); changing an account's intent after sign-up; an account grade for a parent or staff account.
**Done when:** a new student signs up, picks a grade and optionally invites a parent, and joining a branch needs no second form when both are known; a parent lands on Link your child and a teacher on the directory; promotion previews, excludes, applies, and undoes only untouched rows; enrolments, other branches and `User.gradeId` are untouched; the account-grade mismatch is visible to staff.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:design`, `check:rtl` and the static `check:account` run. **Assertions added, none edited** (the old `check:people` §5 still parses `gradePromotionSchema`, which is unchanged): `check:account` (static: routing, the metadata parse, nothing in permissions/relations/scoping reads the intent, the account grade never reaches a branch record, promotion's static shape, the 14-day window); `check:people` §5b (preview, partial promotion with an inactive student and another branch's student ticked, refusals, one batch + items + one audit row without names, `User.gradeId` and enrolments byte-for-byte untouched, only the latest batch undoable, undo reverts only unchanged rows, the 14-day edge, cross-branch undo); `check:student-portal` (seeding: grade + parent → complete; either alone → form stays; an unoffered grade is not seeded; a cancelled invitation declares nobody; an existing profile is never overwritten; each intent grants nothing); `check:isolation` §8 (preview, confirm and undo from another customer's branch and from a sibling branch). The three database gates are for a later run (CLAUDE.md rule 8).
**Docs:** 00 (§4.1 items 0 and 4, §4.3 items 0, 4 and 6, §4.6, §5.1 "Sign-up intent and the two-layer grade", D-11), 01 (User, StudentProfile, GradePromotionBatch/Item, §5, §6), 02 (§2 tree, the onboarding-routing paragraph), 04 (§11.23), 05 (entry 27), 06, this file.

### F5 — Public Explore for guests (delivered)
**Goal:** anyone can browse branches, their classes and teachers without an account; sign-in is asked for only when they act.
**Build:**
1. **Schema** — migration `20261027120000_f5_public_listing` (additive): `CenterSettings.publicListing Boolean @default(true)`. Every existing branch is listed by default.
2. **Data** — `lib/db/public-catalog.ts`, the only module a `(public)` page reads: explicit `select` allowlists on every query (branch: slug, names, logo, theme colour; class: catalog labels, schedule, period, price, join options; teacher: that branch's local name and the average-only rating). A branch is listed when ACTIVE or GRACE **and** `publicListing`; a class when active, accepting new students and not ended by the branch's own today. Per-branch reads stay `centerId`-scoped; the class search is a union of per-branch queries (02 §14). Seats leave as a status — Open / Few seats left (3 or fewer, `lib/capacity.ts`'s `seatStatus`) / Full — never a number.
3. **Routes** — route group `(public)`: `/explore` (Branches 12 per page / Classes 20 per page with subject, grade and day filters, T4 URL state), `/explore/[slug]`, `/explore/[slug]/classes/[classId]`, `/explore/[slug]/teachers/[teacherUserId]`; anything unlisted is a 404. `PublicShell`: KayEdu mark, Explore, language toggle, Sign in / Create account (or Go to my dashboard). A signed-out `/` redirects to Explore (the DES landing is gone); signed-in routing is unchanged.
4. **Acting** — Enrol / Request to join: a guest goes to `/login?next=<the class page>` (locale-less, through the existing `safeNextPath`) and returns after sign-in or sign-up; a signed-in person gets the existing `ReserveButton` → `reserveOrEnroll`, with its existing messages.
5. **Shared, not forked** — `ClassCard` and `ClassDetail` (`components/explore/`) render Explore and the signed-in Discover; Discover gets a class page (`/student/discover/[centerId]/[classId]`) under its own rules.
6. **Settings → General** — "List this branch on Explore" (`center.settings.edit`; saved and audited with the rest of `center.settings.update`).
7. **Logos** — `/api/logos/[centerId]` serves a guest a listed branch's logo (signed redirect, `public, max-age=300`); every other guest request is still a 404; signed-in behaviour is unchanged.
8. **Search engines** — every page is `noindex, nofollow` while `ALLOW_AUTO_CONFIRM_DEMO` is set (`noIndexDemo`); per-page titles on the public pages (`%s · KayEdu`).
**Out of scope:** written reviews (backlog entry 28); single-session booking from the class page (F6).
**Done when:** a signed-out browser sees Explore, branch, class and teacher pages in EN and AR at 375px; unlisted, ONBOARDING and SUSPENDED branches 404; Enrol sends a guest to login and back; no public page renders a phone, email or student field; `next` can't redirect off-site; `noindex` is present.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:rtl` and the new no-database **`check:public`** (package.json + CI's static job: `(public)` pages import only `lib/db/public-catalog.ts`; no select names a phone, email, altPhone, parent* or StudentProfile/Enrollment/Payment field — the one ACTIVE-enrolment `_count` goes straight into `seatStatus`; every read has a `select`; no exported type carries a count; `safeNextPath` refuses off-site values; listing, logo, `noindex` and shell shape). `check:design` passes except one pre-existing assertion awaiting an old → new decision (`app/[locale]/page.tsx renders <PublicHeader />` — the page now redirects to Explore). Database assertions **added, not run**: `check:student-portal`'s F5 block (unlisted / ONBOARDING / SUSPENDED / ARCHIVED hidden, inactive / ended / closed classes hidden, branch-scoped class ids, seat statuses, the exact public field set, the teacher page exposing no count).
**Docs:** 00 (§2.1 M20 noindex, §2.2 OD-13, §4.7, §5 "Teacher ratings"), 02 (§2 tree, §10 logos, §14 Discover, §16 env), 04 (§10, §11.24, §12), 05 (entry 28), 06, 07 (SEC-53 row), this file.

### F6 — Joining options + single-session booking (delivered)
**Goal:** a student chooses "Enrol in the class" or "Attend one session"; each class says which are allowed and whether each needs approval; seats are never oversold.
**Build:**
1. **Schema** — migration `20261027150000_f6_session_bookings` (additive): enums `SessionBookingMode` (OFF/INSTANT/APPROVAL), `SessionBookingStatus`, `SessionBookingCancelReason`; `ClassGroup.sessionBookingMode` (default OFF) and `maxBookingSeats Int?` (CHECK ≥ 1); table `SessionBooking` (unique `(sessionId, studentId)`, unique `sessionAttendeeId`, indexed `centerId` / `studentId`, RLS on, a CHECK tying `cancelReason` to CANCELLED).
2. **Data** — `lib/db/session-bookings.ts` (the only writer: request / confirm / reject / student cancel / cancel-with-session; the 10-open-bookings cap; expiry at start), `lib/db/session-seats.ts` (seat usage, `lockSessionRow`, `firstOverflowingSession`), the seat arithmetic in `lib/capacity.ts`, `hasSessionStarted` in `lib/scheduling.ts`. `claimClassSeat` (every enrolment path) now refuses `sessionFull` with the first full date; `reserveOrEnroll` / `confirmReservation` peek it before making a profile. `cancelSession` / `rescheduleSession` cancel open bookings in their transaction. `createAttendance` links the booking in its one insert statement (a CTE). Open bookings join the pristine predicate and the class delete blockers.
3. **Class dialog** — one **Joining** section (create and edit): Accept new students, Enrolment Instant / Needs approval, Single-session attendance online Off / Instant / Needs approval, Max single-session seats per session, and the read-only seat limit from the rooms.
4. **Approvals** — a Session bookings section on `/approvals` (`approvals.manage`) and on `/teacher/approvals` (`reservation.manage.own`, pinned to the teacher's classes), audited `sessionBooking.confirm` / `.reject` in the same transaction; expired ones are not listed and are refused.
5. **Desk** — "Booked" instead of Drop-in in reception session mode, the session page's confirm panel and roster; "Booked — not arrived (n)" on the session page. No money logic changes.
6. **Student** — the Discover class page lists Upcoming sessions (date, time, seat status) with "Attend this session only"; the public class page links to it (it reads no session); `/student/reservations` is "My requests" with Class enrolments / Session bookings tabs (URL state, cancel until start, "moved to <date> — book again").
**Out of scope:** waitlists (backlog entry 3, unchanged); booking a one-time session with no class (backlog entry 29); showing a booked session on the student's schedule before it happens; a staff-side cancel of a booking (`STAFF` reason reserved) *(both delivered in T7)*.
**Done when:** a class set to Instant lets a student book one session and they appear as Booked at the desk; an approval booking waits in both queues; the last seat can't be taken twice; enrolment is refused when a future session is full of bookings; cancelling or rescheduling a session cancels its bookings; existing classes (all OFF, no bookings) behave exactly as before.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:rtl`, `check:public` pass; `check:design` passes except the one pre-existing F5 assertion (`app/[locale]/page.tsx renders <PublicHeader />`). Database assertions **added, not run**: `check:student-portal` (OFF/Instant/Approval, profile at confirm, last-seat race two → one, `maxBookingSeats`, blocks at request and confirm, the 10-booking cap, started → refused, pending → expired with nothing written, student cancel until start, seats as a status, another branch cannot confirm); `check:enrollment` (enrolment refused by future bookings — instant and at confirm, naming the date, no profile made; session cancel / reschedule cancel bookings); `check:approvals` (both queues, teacher queue pinned, pinned confirm refuses another teacher's booking, audit rows, other branch refused); `check:isolation` (by-id, confirm/reject/cancel and request from another branch, its queue); `check:reception` (Booked state, check-in links the booking, no payment written, a walk-in links nothing).
**Docs:** 00 (§4.1 items 3/7/8/9, §4.2 item 6, §4.3 item 2a, §5.1 "Single-session booking"), 01 (ClassGroup joining fields, SessionBooking, §5), 02 (§2 tree, §11.1), 04 (§10, §11.3, §11.17, §11.25), 05 (entry 29), this file.

### F7 — Approvals split by type, with counts and a sidebar badge (delivered)
**Goal:** the Approvals queues are split by kind with how many wait in each, and the sidebar says how many are waiting without opening the page.
**Build:**
1. **Counts** — `lib/db/approval-counts.ts`: `pendingApprovalCounts(ctx)` (class requests, session requests, enrolments, session bookings, account claims, total) and `pendingTeacherApprovalCounts(ctx, teacherId)` (enrolments, bookings — pinned to the teacher's own classes). One `centerId`-scoped count per kind over the queue's own `where` (`scoped.ts`: `changeRequests.countPendingByType`, `reservations.countPending` / `countPendingForTeacher`, `sessionBookings.countPending`; `account-claims.ts`: `countPendingClaimsForCenter`); bookings share `pendingBookingWhere` with `listPending`. `lib/auth/approval-counts.ts` adds the per-request `cache()`.
2. **`/approvals`** — tabs All · Class requests · Session requests · Enrolments · Session bookings · Account claims, `?type=` through the T4 hook (`components/shared/queue-tabs.tsx`: `SoftLink` pills from `sm`, a Select below), each with its count (zero tabs muted), oldest first in every queue (`changeRequests.list` gained an `order` option; its default stays newest first for the teacher's own request list), only the selected tab's queues are read. The header total and the "showing N of M" notes read the same counts; the claims queue now says so when it is capped.
3. **`/teacher/approvals`** — tabs Enrolments · Session bookings (Enrolments is the bare URL), same component, pinned to the teacher's own classes.
4. **Sidebar badge** — `NavItem` draws an amber count (`bg-warning-bg` / `text-warning-fg`): the number for 1–9, "9+" above, on the icon's corner on the collapsed rail; the link's accessible name carries the full count ("Approvals, 12 pending"). Shown only to a user holding `approvals.manage` / `reservation.manage.own`; none under "Whole Management"; read on every navigation, refreshed by the actions' existing `revalidatePath`; no polling.
**Out of scope:** a merged chronological "All" list (All stacks the sections, each oldest first); a count that updates in an already-open tab without navigating (backlog entry 30); per-tab badges in the nav.
**Done when:** tab counts equal the rows in each tab; the badge equals the sum and caps at "9+"; a receptionist sees no badge; a teacher's count excludes other teachers' classes; it works on the rail, the drawer, at 375px and in RTL.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:rtl` pass; `check:design` passes except the one pre-existing F5 assertion (`app/[locale]/page.tsx renders <PublicHeader />`) and gains the approvals-tabs assertion. Database assertions **added, not run**: `check:approvals` (each tab count equals the rows of its queue, the badge total is their sum, a teacher's count excludes another teacher's classes and never counts requests or claims, another branch is counted from its own rows only, resolving a request lowers its tab and the total by one, a receptionist's nav has no Approvals item and no badge). Run later: `npm run check:approvals`.
**Docs:** 00 (§4.1 item 9, §4.2 item 6), 02 (§2 tree, the counts paragraph before "URL state changes softly"), 04 (sidebar, §11.3), 05 (entry 30), this file.
