### S1 — Staging environment for the database gates and e2e (delivered, nothing run)
**Build:** a git-ignored `.env.staging` (committed `.env.staging.example`) and `KAYEDU_ENV=staging`, which makes one shared loader (`scripts/lib/env.ts`, entered via `scripts/with-env.ts`; every `npm run` script, the seed and `playwright.config.ts` use it) read it instead of `.env`: required keys checked up front, dev-only keys blanked, a missing file or a URL naming the dev project stops the run. `npm run db:deploy:staging` (`prisma migrate deploy` against `.env.staging`). **The write-guard** (`assertSafeToWrite`, in `gatePrisma`, `check:all`, `gates:users`, `playwright.config.ts`): refuse unless `KAYEDU_ENV=staging` or the database is on localhost; refuse the same database as `.env`'s (decided by Supabase project ref, because same-region projects share a pooler host); refuse production; no override flag. `check:rls` stays unguarded (read-only); `db:seed`, `db:wipe-dev`, `seed:verify` keep their own fences. CI sets `KAYEDU_ENV: staging`. `docs/staging.md` is the runbook (dashboard settings, migrate, seed, verify, `check:all`, e2e, reset).
**Found by the first staging run:** `check:rls` on a fresh Supabase project failed 3 checks. USAGE on `public` for `anon`/`authenticated` came from the PUBLIC ACL entry — fixed by migration `20261028090000_s1_public_schema_usage_lockdown` (revoke from PUBLIC; dev already had an empty ACL). The `supabase_admin` default privileges naming `anon`/`authenticated` remain, because `postgres` cannot alter them and they only cover objects `supabase_admin` creates: `check:rls` (`scripts/lib/rls-audit.ts`) now audits only the connecting role's default privileges.
**Not changed:** schema, runtime behaviour, any gate's assertions. **Out of scope:** the Vercel/CI e2e job and its secrets stay in M20.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`. Added, **not run**: `check:security` S1 (the guard's decisions on a table of URLs, the callers, the single loader, the git-ignore rules). Nothing was run against any database.
**Docs:** `staging.md` (new), `deploy-vercel.md`, 02 (§16 env table, §17), `CLAUDE.md` rule 8, `.env.example`, this file.

### G1 — Root causes of the first staging `check:all` (delivered)
**Context:** the first `check:all` on staging passed 26 gates and failed six. Each root cause below was found by running only that gate on staging (`KAYEDU_ENV=staging`), one at a time. No gate assertion was edited.
1. **`check:people` — the teacher phone lookup "stopped reusing `describeMatch`".** It never stopped: the action is unchanged since F1 apart from T6's `isPickBranch` guard (30ce61f), and that one extra line pushed the `describeMatch(` call to character 812 of the 800-character window the assertion reads. No SEC-2a regression and nothing inlined. The two scope guards are now one line (`!scope || isPickBranch(scope)`), which brings the call back to character 790. Behaviour is unchanged.
2. **`check:student-portal` — P2028 "Transaction not found".** The F6-per-session hypothesis is **refuted** for this crash: the failing enrolment was into a class with **0** sessions (cap 0, so the seat check returned at once). The transaction ran **21 statements in 5,135 ms (~245 ms each)**, over Prisma's 5 s default. The gate opened it with `prisma.$transaction(fn)` and no options, while every real caller (Discover, staff Enrol, both Approvals confirms) runs it through `runInTransaction` with `WRITE_TRANSACTION_OPTIONS` (20 s), on a co-located database. **Fixes:** (a) `gatePrisma` gives every gate transaction the app's write budget (`transactionOptions: WRITE_TRANSACTION_OPTIONS`). The measured numbers need it: an enrolment is 18–23 statements whatever the seat check does, and statements cost 130–470 ms from a developer machine. (b) The F6 seat check (`firstOverflowingSession`) is now **set-based**: one read of the candidate sessions, one `lockSessionRows` statement, one grouped count. That is **1 + 2k → at most 3 statements** for k booked future sessions (k = 0: 1 → 1; 1: 3 → 3; 5: 11 → 3; 20: 41 → 3; 60: 121 → 3, counted against a stub transaction). Each instant enrolment runs it twice. The lock order (class, then sessions) and the first-full-date refusal are unchanged. **Gate after:** enrolment transactions 18–23 statements in 2.4–3.7 s, the slowest transaction 4.9 s (an INSTANT booking waiting on a lock), and the whole gate ran to the end through F5, F6, T7 and the new G1 block. It now stops on one assertion: **F5's public-class field allowlist predates F6's `sessionBookingMode`**, which docs/02 records as the one booking field a public class carries. This is a stale allowlist, waiting on OD-14.
3. **`check:one-off-sessions` — `session-bookings.ts:322`.** The line was already null-safe (`!session?.classGroup || …` short-circuits), but in a shape the sweep cannot see. The real hazard sat downstream: `confirmBooking` and `lockAndCheckSeat` re-read the class as `session.classGroup!`. The resolve path now narrows the class once (`const classGroup = session?.classGroup`) and passes it on. A one-time session is "not found", never a crash, and there is no non-null assertion left. Staff cancel, the teacher booked list (`listConfirmedForSession`), the student and parent schedule (`bookedUpcoming`, `listBookingsForStudent`) and reception's booking lookup were checked: none of them dereferences the class.
4. **`check:timezone` — five `startOfUtcDay(now)` floors.** All five were SQL floors with a day of UTC slack, followed by an exact per-session clock rule, so no booking was ever wrongly refused. **What was wrong:** they were a second derivation of today. Between 00:00 and 02:00/03:00 Cairo the floor reached back **two** Cairo days, so the row-capped reads (the class page's upcoming list at 10 + 4 rows, the approvals queue at 500, the booked schedule at 100) spent rows on past sessions. **Now:** the branch's own lists use the branch's today (`todayInTimezone`): the approvals queue and its counts (`pendingBookingWhere`, from `ctx.settings`) and the class page's upcoming sessions (the branch's `CenterSettings.timezone`). Reads across branches, where no single centre's day applies and each row carries its own frozen zone, use the new `earliestTodayAnywhere` (UTC−12, `lib/scheduling.ts`) and then judge each row in that zone: the per-account open-booking cap, the student and parent booked schedule, and the enrolment seat check, which keeps that floor so it adds no settings read inside the transaction. **New assertion** (`check:student-portal`, G1 block): at 00:30 Cairo (22:30 UTC the day before), a session at 18:00 the same Cairo day is bookable, is on the class page's upcoming list, and is on the student schedule.
5. **`check:session-lifecycle` — failed in `check:all`, passed alone.** Three isolated staging runs passed (108/108 each). `check:all` runs gates one at a time, the fixtures use fixed December 2026 dates and their own slugs, and the gate pre-cleans, so it is not order or clock dependent. **Root cause: timing**, the same as (2). The "void racing a re-check-in" test opens the void with the gate's bare `prisma.$transaction(fn)` (5 s before this fix). Whichever side waits on the other's attendee-row lock carries its own ~16 statements plus the winner's whole transaction: a waiting re-check-in measured **4,660 ms for 10 statements**, just under 5 s, so a slower moment pushes it over. (2a) covers it.
6. **`check:security`.** **SEC-03:** fixed. A comment inside `signUpAction` named `acceptFamilyCode`, so the assertion had failed since it was written (ae25ffe); the comment no longer names the function, and no code changed. **Stale since a deliberate change, waiting on OD-14 (not edited):** **SYS-STORAGE.** F2's documented `ALLOW_UNVERIFIED_EMAIL_CHANGE` path (`app/[locale]/account/actions.ts`) is a second importer of the service-role client until M20. **SEC-25.** F7 moved every Approvals count into `pendingApprovalCounts` (one source for the tabs and the badge) and the change-request lists to `oldestFirst`; each list still takes `APPROVALS_PAGE_SIZE`. **SEC-49.** F2 moved the student layout's body into `student-shell.tsx`, which does call `centerGrades(prisma, incomplete.center.id)`.
**Diagnostics:** `GATE_TX_TRACE=1` (`scripts/lib/gate-db.ts`) prints each gate transaction's statement count and time when it failed or ran at least `GATE_TX_TRACE_MS`.
**Not changed:** schema, money, permissions, tenant scoping, any gate assertion. **Behaviour changed:** none a user can see, except that the approvals queue and the class page's upcoming list no longer spend rows on past days.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public` pass. On staging, one at a time: `check:people`, `check:one-off-sessions`, `check:timezone` pass; `check:session-lifecycle` passes ×3; `check:student-portal` fails only F5's allowlist and `check:security` only SYS-STORAGE, SEC-25 and SEC-49 (all four waiting on OD-14). **Not run** (not authorised): `check:enrollment`, which holds the enrolment-versus-full-session assertions the set-based seat check must keep; run it before merging.
**Docs:** 02 (§ gate client, "One owner of today", single-session booking locks and clock), 00 (OD-14), `staging.md`, this file.

### G2 — Stale gate assertions answered (OD-14) and a sturdier `check:all` (delivered)
**Part 1 — five assertion edits, the founder's approved list and nothing else.** Each keeps proving what it proved; none is a weakening.
(a) **`check:security` SYS-STORAGE.** *Old:* `only the adapter imports the service-role client` — `importsAdmin.length === 1 && importsAdmin[0] === "lib/storage/supabase.ts"`. *New:* `only the adapter and app/[locale]/account/actions.ts (ALLOW_UNVERIFIED_EMAIL_CHANGE path; remove together with the flag in M20) import the service-role client` — the adapter must still import it **and** no importer other than those two files may exist. *Why not weaker:* one named file, with its reason, is allowed; any other importer still fails (proved: a throwaway `lib/g2-mutation-tmp.ts` importing the client made SYS-STORAGE fail, then was deleted). The path is `app/[locale]/account/actions.ts`, not the `lib/auth/account.ts` the request guessed. M20 already lists removing the flag; it now also says to delete this allowlist entry.
(b) **`check:security` SEC-25.** *Old:* one step — `take: APPROVALS_PAGE_SIZE` in `approvals/page.tsx`, three `, page)|listPending(page)` calls, `changeRequests.count(` and `reservations.countPending()` on the page, and `options?.take ? { take: options.take }` in `scoped.ts`. *New:* three steps. (1) *every list read is capped*: `const page = { take: APPROVALS_PAGE_SIZE }`, `oldestFirst = { ...page, … }`, exactly two `changeRequests.list(` calls and both pass `oldestFirst`, `reservations.listPending(page)`, bookings sliced to `APPROVALS_PAGE_SIZE`, and the `options?.take` pass-through in `scoped.ts`; (2) *the counts are grouped counts* in `lib/db/approval-counts.ts`'s `pendingApprovalCounts` (`countPendingByType()`, `reservations.countPending()`, `sessionBookings.countPending(`, no `.list(`/`.listPending(`/`findMany(` in it), `prisma.changeRequest.groupBy(` in `scoped.ts`, and the page reads `getPendingApprovalCounts(ctx)`; (3) *the booking count's lean read keeps its explicit `take: 500`*. *Why not weaker:* the same bound and the same counts, asserted where F7 put them; it adds the no-list-to-count and `take: 500` checks the old line did not make.
(c) **`check:security` SEC-49.** *Old:* `layout` = `app/[locale]/(student)/layout.tsx`. *New:* `app/[locale]/(student)/student-shell.tsx`, where F2 moved the grade picker. The regexes are unchanged: `centerGrades(prisma, incomplete.center.id)` present, `listCatalogGrades` absent.
(d) **`check:student-portal` F5 allowlist.** *Old:* `endDate, grade, id, price, requiresConfirmation, schedules, seats, startDate, subject, teacherName, teacherRating, teacherUserId`. *New:* the same plus `sessionBookingMode`. The check is still an exact key-set equality, so any other field fails — `maxBookingSeats`, a phone/email/student/enrolment/payment field, a rating count (proved: `maxBookingSeats` added to the public class made exactly that assertion fail, then removed).
(e) **`check:people` teacher/student phone lookup.** *Old:* `contents.slice(start, start + 800)` after `indexOf("lookupPersonByPhoneAction")`. *New:* `exportedBody(file, "lookupPersonByPhoneAction")` — the whole function up to the next top-level export, a local copy of the helper `check:security`, `check:account` and `check:parent` each carry. The `describeMatch(` / no `.findMany(` / no `prisma.user.findUnique` tests are untouched; the neighbouring `resolveContact(` / `rateLimited` test reads the same body, so it no longer depends on a window either. *Why not weaker:* a longer read can only find more, and the action cannot fall out of the window again; the function not being found now throws instead of silently passing the `start !== -1` guard that is gone.
**Part 2 — `check:all` (`scripts/run-gates.ts`, helpers in `scripts/lib/gate-runner.ts` and `scripts/lib/connection-limit.ts`).** (1) *Wait for the database*: before each database-backed gate (a gate script that opens `gatePrisma()`/`PrismaClient`; `check:rtl`, `check:filters`, `check:timezone` do not), `SELECT 1` on a fresh one-connection client, retried every 2 s for up to 60 s; if it never answers the gate is **not started** and reported **`DB UNREACHABLE`**, counted on its own line of the verdict, exit code still non-zero. (2) *Fast failures explained*: output is tee'd, and a gate that fails in under 5 s has the first error line of its output (credentials in a connection string masked) printed under its summary row. (3) *Connections*: the top of the run prints each client's `connection_limit`. **Lowered:** a race's other desk (`gatePrisma({ sweepUsers: false })`) now has 3 connections, not `DATABASE_URL`'s 10, so one gate with two desks asks the pooler for at most 10 + 3 + 3 instead of 30 (Supabase's default pooler pool is 15). Three, not two, because `check:security` SEC-06's six-way enrolment race puts three transactions on one desk at once. The main client and the app are unchanged. **This is a precaution, not a diagnosis:** the cause of the 13 sub-second failures was never observed, and 16 still exceeds 15 in the worst case (about 7 in practice, connections open on demand). The user-leak check is unchanged and nothing is cleaned up or deleted by the runner.
**Demonstrated without a database:** `waitForDatabase` with an injected probe and clock — refuses three times then answers (gate starts after 4 attempts), never answers (gives up at 58 s, `DB UNREACHABLE`, password masked); a Prisma-style startup error reduced to one line under a `FAIL` row, while a slow failure gets no extra line; `connection_limit` 10 → 3 keeping `pgbouncer`/`pool_timeout`. The runner has no gate filter and `check:all` was not run.
**Not changed:** schema, money, permissions, tenant scoping, any other assertion, app behaviour. **Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public` pass; on staging, one at a time, `check:security`, `check:student-portal`, `check:people` pass. Not run: the other 30 gates, `check:all`, `e2e` — in particular the desk-limit change has only been exercised by `check:security`'s two race blocks; `check:parent` and `check:branches` use desks too and should be run before merging.
**Docs:** 00 (OD-14 resolved), 02 (§ gate client), `staging.md` (§6), this file (G2; M20 note).

### E1 — The first staging e2e run: five causes, one product bug (delivered)
**Context:** the first full `npm run e2e` on staging: 142 passed, 19 failed, 4 skipped (all 32 database gates pass). Each cause below was found by reading `e2e/.results/<test>/` (error-context, screenshot) before touching a test, then confirmed by running **only the named spec, in `en-1280` first, on staging** (the S1 guard in place). Not run: the full suite.
**A. Student sign-up — a product bug (F4).** `app/[locale]/account/study-actions.ts` is a `"use server"` file and exported `IDLE_STUDY_STATE` (an object) beside `saveStudyDetailsAction`. Next validates every export of such a file when the module is evaluated (`ensureServerEntryExports`, `next/dist/build/webpack/loaders/next-flight-loader/action-validate.js`) and throws *"A "use server" file can only export async functions, found object"* (digest 2878828124); the onboarding page and Account → Study details then rendered "This screen didn't load", so "Continue" never reached "Skip for now". **It is not dev-only:** the check is in the server bundle, so a Vercel build would pass (it is a runtime check, not a compile error) and the two routes would fail on first load in production exactly as here. Every one of the 30 `"use server"` files was checked; this was the only offender. **Fix:** `IDLE_STUDY_STATE` and `StudyDetailsState` moved to `lib/validation/account.ts` beside `studyDetailsSchema` (where the F3 family state already lives in `lib/validation/parents.ts`); the two forms import them from there. **New check** (`check:design` §14): every `"use server"` file under `app/`, `components/`, `lib/` exports only async functions and types, found by parsing with the TypeScript compiler (so `export { x }`, `export *`, `export default`, enums and classes count, not just `export const`); it also runs its detector on seven bad snippets and on a clean one, so it cannot go quiet. *Proved:* with `export const TEMP_VIOLATION = {};` appended to the file the check failed naming it, then it was restored.
**B. Navigation (12 tests) — the test was stale; one product hook added.** F7's sidebar badge made the link text `Approvals\n4`. *Old:* the test read each link's `innerText` and compared it to the label. *New:* the badge element carries **`data-nav-badge`** (both the row badge and the collapsed-rail badge, `components/shell/nav-item.tsx`); the test clones each link, removes `[data-nav-badge]` and compares the remaining text to the exact set of nav items for the role (unchanged), and reads each badge separately, which must be a number (Arabic-Indic digits in Arabic) or the capped `Shell.pendingCapped` ("9+" / "٩+"). No regex on digits in the label, nothing else loosened.
**C. Manager detail pages (4 tests) — the test's selector was stale; no product regression.** The failing page was `/students/promote`: the first `main a[href*="/students/"]` on the Students list is now the **"Promote students"** header button (F4, `43f58c2`), not a student row, and that page's own back link is "Back to students" (not "All students"). The student detail header and its back link (`Students.backToList`, "All students" / "كل الطلاب") are unchanged since R10b. *Old:* `main a[href*="${list}/"]`. *New:* a shared `firstDetailLink(page, list)` (`e2e/support/ui.ts`) that finds the first link whose href is `${list}/<uuid>` (retried until the list has rendered).
**D. Parent code journey — the same stale selector, plus a race.** The journey opened `/students/promote` (cause C) and so never found a Parent access card. It now opens a real student through `firstDetailLink`. *Skip logic, old → new:* `test.skip(await generate.isDisabled(), …)` (a missing button timed out for 30 s inside `isDisabled`) → wait for the card's title, **fail with a named assertion if the button count is 0**, and skip only when the button exists and is disabled (the three parent slots are used). With the journey now running to its end, a second test race showed: after confirming **Unlink** the test navigated to `/parent` at once, before the action had finished, so the parent still saw the child. *Old:* click, `goto`. *New:* click, wait for the empty "No child is linked yet" card (the outcome), then `goto`. The student/manager used are the seeded manager (Hala Mostafa, Downtown) and its first student (Youssef Hassan); the card renders and the manager holds `parent.link.manage`.
**E. Teacher session — not reproduced.** The failure page was the **sign-in screen** (the test's own `/login` bounce), after 1.6 minutes: a session lost mid-run, the known shared-storage-state expiry under load (`e2e-run-conditions`), not a missing "Session note" card. The spec ran alone in `en-1280` three times: **3/3 passed**, 32–36 s each. No change.
**F. "The destination stream errored while writing data" (digest 461193751) — noise.** It is Next logging a response stream whose client went away. Evidence from 13 staging runs: it appeared in exactly two of them. In one, **9 times during the auth setup project's 15 real sign-ins** (each sign-in test ends the moment its landing URL is reached and its browser context is closed while that page is still streaming); in the other, **twice at the very end** of the run, when the last test's context closed. In the 11 runs where setup reused the saved sessions (`0ms`) and tests ended on their own it never appeared. It is never next to a failed assertion, is not logged by any code of ours, carries no stack frame outside Next, and no test failed because of it. Left as is. (Not proven at the Next source level; the stack is `ignore-listed`.)
**Seen once, not reproduced:** `student.spec` @write's grade picker timed out once on a cold server (the system list's option still in the DOM when the grade list opened) and passed on the next run; `navigation` owner en-1280 failed once with a browser-side `Unexpected end of JSON input` on `/en/settings` while two workers compiled cold, and passed 2/2 alone. Both are first-compile races of the dev server, not edited.
**Found, not fixed (outside this prompt):** after **Unlink** on Account → Family the *"Child unlinked"* toast never shows: the `UnlinkChildButton` that owns `useActionFeedback` unmounts when the revalidation removes its row, so the success is silent (docs/04 §14.2 "every mutation answers"). The same shape may exist wherever a row's own button deletes the row.
**Not changed:** schema, money, permissions, tenant scoping, any other test or gate assertion. **Behaviour changed:** the Study details form and student onboarding work again; the sidebar badge gains a data attribute.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public` pass. On staging (en-1280): `student.spec:37`, `parent.spec:38`, `manager.spec:29`, the owner/manager/teacherDowntown navigation tests, `teacher.spec:13` ×3 pass; the 12 navigation tests pass across all four projects (one cold-start flake, above); `manager.spec:29` and navigation pass in `ar-375`. The two account-creating journeys run in `en-1280` only by design (sign-up is rate-limited), so they have no `ar-375` run.
**Docs:** this file, 02 (§14 conventions, §17), 04 (sidebar badge hook), `staging.md` (§6), `CLAUDE.md` (the `"use server"` rule).

### U1 — Password field, password policy, Account settings from the hub, trusted teacher at creation (delivered)
**Context:** four fixes from testing.
1. **Password field.** `PasswordField` is now `dir="ltr"` on the whole control (it was only the `<input>`), so the eye sits inside the field at the physical right in EN and AR and typed text never runs under it; label and errors keep the page direction. One component for sign-in, sign-up and Account.
2. **Password policy** on sign-up and change (never sign-in): 8+ characters, a letter, a digit. `lib/password-policy.ts` holds the constant and the rules; the shared zod `password` schema (`lib/validation/auth.ts`) returns `passwordTooShort` / `passwordNeedsLetter` / `passwordNeedsDigit` (en + ar, in both `Auth.errors` and `Account.errors`); `NewPasswordField` shows the live checklist. The register form's old "At least 8 characters" hint is replaced by the checklist. *Observation:* the repo has no separate "accept invitation" password step — an invitee registers through sign-up — so there are two setting sites, not three. The register form validates server-side only (it never ran a client schema); the live checklist is its client feedback.
3. **Account settings from the hub.** Cause: the hub link carried no `?from=`, so `resolveAccountExperience` fell to `getTenantContext()`, which redirects to the hub when no branch is active. Now `?from=hub` (`ACCOUNT_HUB_HINT`) → a neutral `HubShell` (app bar + user menu, no sidebar) with a back link to `/select-center?switch=1`.
4. **Trusted teacher at creation.** `teacherCreateSchema.isTrustedTeacher` (explicit `"true"`/`"false"`, absent = the column default) → `addTeacherMembership`'s `isTrustedTeacher` option, applied to a **newly created** membership in the same transaction (both Add teacher via `createTeacherAction`, and the class dialog via `createClassWithNewTeacher`). The switch lives in the shared `NewTeacherFields`, so both forms get it; hidden when the matched person already teaches here. No new permission; no schema change; no audit shape change.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public` pass. On staging, one at a time: `check:people` (new: schema default + explicit false, Add teacher saves false / default true), `check:security` (new **U1**: sign-up and change refuse 7 chars / no digit / no letter and accept a compliant one; the sign-in schema still accepts a short password), `check:class-creation` (new: default trusted; created untrusted through the class path), and the `@write` tests of `student.spec` and `parent.spec` (en-1280; their sign-up password `e2e-Passw0rd!` already met the policy, so no helper changed) pass. Not run: the full suite, `check:all`. No existing assertion was edited.
**Docs:** 00 (§4.1 Teachers), 02 (§3), 04 (password field rule, policy), this file.

### U2 — Reschedule frees the slot; the session page and receipt never clip; Not arrived offers only Check in (delivered)
**Context:** three fixes from testing. "Rescheduled" is `rescheduledToId IS NOT NULL` — `SessionStatus` has no `RESCHEDULED` value and none was added; the display status is derived (`deriveSessionDisplayStatus`).
1. **Reschedule frees the slot (A).** Migration `20261030120000_u2_session_slot_partial_unique` (index swap only): creates `Session_classGroupId_date_startTime_live_key` — `UNIQUE ("classGroupId","date","startTime") WHERE "rescheduledToId" IS NULL` — first, then the plain index the schema now declares, then drops `Session_classGroupId_date_startTime_key`, so there is no gap. `schema.prisma` carries `@@index([classGroupId, date, startTime])` with a comment pointing at the hand-written index; future generated migrations must drop their `DROP INDEX` line for it (docs/02 §11, CLAUDE.md rule 7). **Changed calls** (none used the compound-unique key): `createManualSession`/`createOneOffSession`'s slot check (`class-sessions.ts`, was a `count` over every session) and new pre-checks in `rescheduleSession` (`sessions.ts`) and `resolveSessionRequest` (`approvals.ts`) all go through `slotTakenError` (`slot-conflicts.ts`, `findFirst` with `rescheduledToId: null`): `duplicateSlot`, or **`duplicateSlotCancelled`** when the blocker is cancelled — a cancelled session still holds its slot and there is no reinstate, so the message says to pick another time (en + ar, `Sessions.errors`, `Approvals.errors`). The P2002 fallbacks are unchanged. The engine's taken-key read is unchanged and still counts every session (A2). **Readers** add `NOT_MOVED_SESSION`: `scoped.sessions.search` (staff and teacher lists; `includeMoved` for the staff list's **Show moved sessions** switch, `?moved=on`), `listForDay` (dashboard, reception), `studentUpcomingSessions` (student + parent), `teacherCenterSummary`, `classSessionCounts`; reception, occupancy and every bookable read (Discover/Explore) already excluded it. The two links (`SESSION_MOVE_LINKS_SELECT`) are read only by `sessions.search` and the detail include — not by the shared `sessionListSelect`, which the day list and reception also use (their statement budget is asserted in `check:reception`; adding the links there had taken it from 16 to 17). "Moved from <date, time>" on staff + teacher lists and pages, the student schedule and the parent portal; "Moved to …" on a greyed moved row when the switch is on.
2. **No clipping (B).** Causes: the session page's `stickySide` column (capped at the viewport, scrolling inside itself — the receipt's voided/refunds/change-log sections needed an inner scroll and were cut off in print) and the `Card`'s `overflow-hidden` clipping unbroken text. The session page drops `stickySide`; `DetailLayout`'s sticky column is never a scroll box under `print:`; the Session card's rows are `stacked` and gain **Status** (and **Moved from**); the receipt's names, reasons and label→value rows wrap (`min-w-0 break-words`).
3. **Not arrived (C).** The roster's Not-arrived row offers only **Check in**; the "Or correct the mark — they were here all along" control and its message are removed, and so is `AttendanceControl`'s now-unused `statuses` prop. Void / Refund wording per the spec (00 §5 "Void vs. refund"), with the reset line in both forms. Same actions, permissions and rules.
**Done when:** reschedule a session, then reschedule the replacement back — both work, only one session shows in every list, the replacement says "Moved from …"; a resync never brings back a moved or cancelled slot; nothing is cut off on the session page or receipt at 375px and 1280px; a Not-arrived row shows only Check in.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `prisma validate` pass. **New assertions** in `check:session-lifecycle` (section "U2 — reschedule frees the slot"): the schema has a plain index, not `@@unique`; the live database has the partial unique index with `WHERE ("rescheduledToId" IS NULL)` and not the old one; reschedule then reschedule back; a second not-rescheduled row at the slot is still P2002; `slotTakenError` (moved = free, cancelled = `duplicateSlotCancelled`, live = `duplicateSlot`); rescheduling onto a cancelled slot is refused; resync creates nothing at a moved or cancelled slot; the session lists (with and without Show moved), the day list, the student/parent schedule, the class count and occupancy hide the moved original; the replacement carries moved-from and the original moved-to; display status. **Old → new (allowed by the prompt):** `check:one-off-sessions`' "`@@unique([classGroupId, date, startTime])` stays" now asserts the plain `@@index` plus the partial unique index in the U2 migration. **Run on staging, one at a time — all pass:** `check:session-lifecycle`, `check:classes`, `check:class-periods`, `check:one-off-sessions`, `check:enrollment`, `check:reception` (failed once on its 16-statement cap, fixed in code as above, assertion untouched), `check:student-portal`, `check:teacher-portal`; e2e `manager.spec` one-time session `@write` (en-1280) passes. The migration is applied to staging and dev.
**Docs:** 00 (§5 "Void vs. refund", §5.1 reschedule, "Not arrived", set-Absent note, T5 re-check-in), 01 (Session uniqueness), 02 (§11 — partial index, the `DROP INDEX` rule, the `ON DELETE SET NULL` edge case, readers), 04 (§5 attendance control, detail page, the session row of the page table, new §11.26), CLAUDE.md (rule 7), this file.

### U3 — A subject exists once and links to its grades; every picker and filter goes system → grade → subject (delivered)
**Context:** `CatalogSubject` had one parent grade, so a subject existed once per grade (Mathematics 14 times on dev) and every subject picker and filter repeated it.
1. **Model (expand-only).** `CatalogSubject` is grade-independent, its names unique across the catalog (case-insensitive, English and Arabic each). New `CatalogSubjectGrade(subjectId, gradeId)` — composite key, any education system, RLS on, cascade from the subject, restrict from the grade. `CatalogSubject.gradeId` stays as a deprecated, unread, never-written nullable column with its FK and index dropped, so the build deployed before U3 keeps working on the migrated database; dropping it is backlog entry 32.
2. **Migration `20261030150000_u3_subject_grade_links`.** Generated DDL (read-only diff against dev) plus hand-written SQL, one implicit transaction. A guard first raises — before any change — if two canonical subjects would still share an English or an Arabic name. Then subjects are grouped by normalized (English, Arabic) names (`catalogNameKey`'s rule: trim, collapse whitespace, lower-case); the oldest of each group is canonical; every member's old grade becomes a link; `ClassGroup.subjectId`, `Session.subjectId`, `CenterCatalogSelection.subjectId` and `ChangeRequest.payload.subjectId` are repointed; a centre's duplicate subject picks merge into one (active if any was; each display name the most recently updated non-empty one; disagreements reported as a NOTICE); the duplicates are deleted; a request still naming a deleted id raises; an unlinked class pair is reported, never invented. `AuditLog` keeps the ids it was written with (append-only).
3. **Admin catalog.** Subject create/edit: both names + a **Grades** checkbox list grouped by education system; the list shows each subject once with grade chips, a grade filter on the Subjects tab. Delete is blocked by classes, sessions or picks — never by the subject's own links. Removing a link is refused (`linkInUse`) while an **active** class anywhere teaches the pair, naming each grade and its count; a grade with subject links or classes is not deleted.
4. **Centre catalog.** Settings → Catalog lists each subject once, naming which of the branch's grades it covers; a branch picks a subject once; renames stay per subject, bilingual. A grade's deselect is no longer blocked by subject picks.
5. **The pair rule.** Offered at a branch = grade picked and active, subject picked and active, neither deactivated by the platform, pair linked (`isPairOffered`). Class create / any change of the pair, `createOneOffSession` and the teacher's class request refuse an unoffered pair as `subjectNotInGrade`; an unchanged class still saves. The centre-directory request (a teacher with no branch) checks the catalog link only — it reads no branch's picks. A subject with no grade has no pair (the old shape, accepted as before; the UI cannot produce it).
6. **Pickers and filters.** `lib/catalog-picker.ts` + `CatalogPickerFields` (class create/edit, one-time session, both teacher requests) + `GradeSubjectFilterFields` (classes, Discover, Explore). Teacher screens have no subject filter and analytics has no by-subject breakdown, so neither changed; the Sessions list's subject filter (no grade filter) lists each subject once.
7. **Seed.** Each subject once with its links (11 subjects, 71 links); `catalog.pair(system, grade, subject)` replaces the per-grade lookup and throws on an unlinked pair; a class is found by its pair on a rerun. **Bookable classes** (F6): Downtown English P6 — INSTANT, cap 3; Downtown English Grade 10 — INSTANT, no cap; Nasr City Mathematics S3 — APPROVAL (`docs/dev-accounts.md`).
**Merge report (pre-check, SELECT-only in a READ ONLY transaction, 2026-10-30) — identical on dev and staging except the selection count:** 71 subjects in 11 name groups, all 11 with duplicates → 60 rows deleted, 71 links created; groups: Mathematics 14, English 11, Physics 8, Biology 7, Chemistry 7, Arabic 6, Computer Science 6, History 4, French 3, Geography 3, German 2. **Name conflicts: 0.** Repointed: 24 classes, 1 session, 2 change requests (0 dangling). Centre picks merged: dev 44 groups / 223 rows removed (Al Nokhba, Downtown, Nasr City and a dev-only branch "تجمع"), staging 33 groups / 163 rows removed; **renames that disagreed: 0** on both. **Unlinked class or one-time-session pairs after the merge: 0.** A dry run on a throwaway local copy (the pre-U3 seed, plus a disagreeing rename and an inactive duplicate pick) left every class's and session's (grade, subject-name), every request's subject name and all 707 payments byte-identical, and the guard refused a planted whitespace/case clash naming both rows.
**Done when:** no picker or filter repeats a subject; choosing a grade narrows subjects to that grade's; the merge report is clean; existing classes and payments are untouched; the seed has the three bookable classes.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `prisma validate` pass. **Old → new (allowed by the prompt), `check:catalog`:** subject names unique among one grade's subjects → unique across the catalog; "a subject has one parent grade" and the parent-move assertion → a subject links to zero or more grades through `CatalogSubjectGrade` (several systems, set replaced on edit, `gradeNotFound`, links audited, zero allowed); grade delete refused while a child subject exists → while subject links or classes use it. **New:** `check:catalog` §17 (the pair rule — linked, unlinked, other branch, pick off, grade deactivated; `linkInUse` with the count and nothing written; an inactive class does not block; the picker cascade as pure functions) and the static writer scan covers `catalogSubjectGrade`; `check:one-off-sessions` — an unlinked pair → `subjectNotInGrade` (its O0 fixture gained the link); `seed:verify` §6 (no two subjects share a name, each seed subject once with its link count, every class / one-time-session pair linked, the three bookable classes with sessions in the coming two weeks and free seats). Dry-run on a throwaway local Postgres (seeded): every named gate passes. **Run on staging, one at a time, after the migration — all pass:** `check:catalog` (141), `check:classes` (47), `check:class-creation` (173), `check:one-off-sessions` (82), `check:student-portal` (109), `check:analytics` (22), `check:teacher-portal` (23), `check:approvals` (49), `check:payments` (31, untouched); e2e `manager.spec` class `@write` and `student.spec` `@write` (en-1280) pass. **Post-migration report (SELECT-only), dev and staging alike:** migration recorded, 11 subjects with 71 links (Mathematics 14 … German 2), 0 duplicate names, 0 subjects without links, 0 duplicate branch picks, 0 unlinked class or one-time-session pairs, 0 requests naming a deleted subject, the old FK gone, RLS on `CatalogSubjectGrade`. The migration is applied to staging and dev. `seed:verify` passes on a locally seeded copy; on staging it waits for a reseed.
**Docs:** 00 (§2 catalog row, §5.1 R8 rules), 01 (catalog layer, `CatalogSubjectGrade`, selection rules, migration, relationship line), 02 (§8 "Catalog pickers and filters", §14 Discover filter), 04 (§5 "Catalog pickers", §11.18 admin and centre catalog screens), 05 (entry 32), `dev-accounts.md` (catalog line, bookable classes), this file.

### U4 — Every Egyptian mobile stored in one form: E.164 (delivered)
**Context:** phones were stored as `01XXXXXXXXX`, typed in several shapes and shown as stored; a phone search for `+20101…` or `0101 234` depended on how the query happened to be split.
1. **`lib/phone.ts` (still the only normalizer).** `normalizePhone` returns E.164 `+20XXXXXXXXXX` for `01012345678`, `1012345678`, `+201012345678`, `00201012345678`, `201012345678` with any spaces, dashes, dots or brackets and Arabic-Indic or Persian digits; only 010/011/012/015 (anything else is still `invalidPhone`). New: `formatPhone` (`+20 101 234 5678`), `phoneTelHref` (`tel:+20…`), `phoneWhatsAppNumber` (digits without `+`), `looksLikePhone`, and the stored/migratable patterns the migration and the report share.
2. **Writers.** The zod schema normalizes forms as before; `addTeacherMembership` and `createPerson` now normalize too (`phone` and `altPhone`), so a caller that skips zod cannot store a raw string.
3. **Search.** `studentSearchWhere`/`teacherSearchWhere`: a query written like a phone is one token, matched by `phoneSearchDigits` with `contains` on the stored column (a `0…` tail is inside `+20…`), so `0101 234`, `+20101234` and `٠١٠١` all find the person; the pickers' in-memory match is unchanged in behaviour.
4. **Display.** Every phone on screen and on the print sheet goes through `formatPhone`, LTR: the teacher list, teacher header and type-ahead, the student list's contact column, the student page's parent card, the print sheet, the match panel, the searchable picker, Account → records on file, and the edit forms' prefilled values. No `tel:` or WhatsApp-to-number link exists yet; the helpers are there for the first one.
5. **Migration `20261031090000_u4_phone_e164`** (data only — the read-only diff against dev is empty). One `DO` block: pre-check (strip spaces, dashes and one leading `+`; require `01…` or `201…`; RAISE with table, column and count), the `User.phone` collision check after normalizing, the conversion of all 11 phone columns, a post-check. Dry-run on a throwaway local Postgres: converts every shape, a re-run is a no-op, an invalid value and a post-normalize duplicate each RAISE with nothing changed.
6. **`npm run report:phones`** — SELECT-only in a READ ONLY transaction; per column: E.164 / `01…` / punctuation-only / invalid, and the `User.phone` clash count.
**Pre-check report (2026-10-31), identical shape on dev and staging:** dev 320 values to convert (User.phone 105, Membership.phone 26, StudentProfile.phone 76, parentPhone 110, FamilyInvitation.declaredParentPhone 3), staging 338 (113 / 26 / 87 / 110 / 2); every other column empty; **0 invalid, 0 `User.phone` clashes**. **10 seeded teacher rows in `Membership.phone` needed punctuation stripping** (`012-7681-7691`, `+20 10 7325 3370`, …) on each database: the seed handed `createTeacherMembership` its raw display string and that writer stored the phone as given — only the form's zod schema normalized. Fixed at the source (item 2); the seed still passes the raw string so the normalization is exercised.
**Rollout order:** apply to dev and push the code within minutes of each other — the old code writes `01…` (a new row would be the old form until the next migration) and searches/matches with `01…` against `+20…` rows, so a duplicate check across the gap can miss.
**Done when:** every phone column holds only `+20…`; every input shape normalizes; search by any shape finds the person; display is `+20 101 234 5678` everywhere.
**Gates:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `check:account`, `prisma validate`; on staging `check:people`, `check:security`, `check:reception`, `check:parent`, `check:student-portal`, `check:isolation`, `check:performance`, `check:class-creation`, e2e `reception.spec` (en-1280). **Old → new (allowed by the prompt):** every expected stored phone `01…` → `+201…` (`check:people` `SHARED_PHONE`, `CONTACT_PHONE`, the local-only phone; `check:parent` declared phone; `check:security` `contactPhone`; `check:student-portal` seeded parent phones), and fixtures that write a phone straight to the database now write E.164 (`check:parent`, `check:security`'s `freePhone`, `check:class-creation`'s `PHONE_PREFIX`). **New:** `check:people` — every input shape (incl. Arabic/Persian digits) normalizes; only 010/011/012/015; display/tel/WhatsApp forms; `createTeacherMembership` stores a raw typed phone in E.164 on the membership and the new User.
**Docs:** 00 (§5.1 UX & data integrity), 01 (contact normalization, "Phone numbers — the stored form"), 02 (`lib/phone.ts`), 04 (§7 phone display), 07 (SEC-41 gate text), `dev-accounts.md` (phones), this file.

### U5 — Only KayEdu manages branches: admin edit, pause, delete and restore (delivered)
**Context:** decided with Kamal on 29 Oct — branch management is the platform admin's alone; it supersedes F1's Owner self-serve (OD-12 re-decided "platform admin only", `00-master-product-spec.md` §2 and §5.1).
1. **Removed from centres.** Settings → Branches — the page, its sub-nav item, `addBranchAction`, the dialog, `lib/validation/branches.ts`, `addBranchAsOwner`/`organizationBranchUsage` and the `organization.branch.create` permission (it was ceiling-forced, so no stored grant row existed). Owners still see and switch between their branches. Unchanged: F1's branch-aware Invite and Add teacher; `Organization.maxBranches` as the admin's record of what the customer pays for.
2. **Add with Copy setup.** The admin's "Add to an existing organization" gains F1's "Copy setup from <branch>" (same six ticks, same never-copied list), on `addBranchToOrganization`'s own transaction (`copyBranchSetup`); the source must be a non-deleted branch of the same organization (`sourceNotFound`).
3. **Edit** — names and theme colour (`updateBranchDetails`, audited `platform.branch.update` with the changed field names). Slug and timezone fixed.
4. **Pause** — `BranchPause` + `Session.pauseId`. Preview, then one transaction cancelling the scheduled, not-yet-started sessions in range through `cancelSession` (bookings with them); started/live/closed sessions and enrolments untouched. While a pause covers today: check-in, extra/one-time sessions refused; a session dated in a current or upcoming pause is refused however it is made (by hand, reschedule, teacher request, NEW_SESSION approval) — `branchPaused`, one reader (`lib/db/branch-pauses.ts`). Centre shell banner; "Paused until <date>" on Explore and Discover. Ends by itself (no cron). End pause now restores the untouched tagged sessions still ahead, keeps and reports those that now clash.
5. **Delete / restore** — `Center.deletedAt`/`deletedBy`/`restorableUntil` (+30 days). Typed-name confirmation; refused for the only branch, a live session, a DRAFT/APPROVED settlement; organization row locked. One filter (`ACCESSIBLE_CENTER`) removes a deleted branch from every resolution, list and count; `COUNTED_BRANCHES` frees its slot. Restore within the window and the limit. Purge: backlog entry 33 (waits on OD-2).
6. **Migration `20261031120000_u5_admin_branch_management`** (additive): the three `Center` columns, `Session.pauseId` (+ index, FK `SET NULL`), `BranchPause` (RLS enabled), CHECKs for the pause range (≤ 90 days) and `deletedAt`/`restorableUntil` together.
**New open decision:** OD-15 — a class created during a pause still materializes sessions inside it.
**Done when:** no centre user can reach or call any branch management; an admin can add (with copy setup), edit, pause (with preview), end a pause early, delete (typed confirmation) and restore; a deleted branch is invisible everywhere and frees its slot.
**Gates (staging):** `check:branches` (§4 rewritten, §5–§6 new), `check:admin`, `check:permissions` (§8 rewritten), `check:isolation`, `check:session-lifecycle`, `check:enrollment`, `check:reception`, `check:student-portal`, `check:teacher-portal`, `check:parent`, `check:approvals`, `check:analytics`; e2e `owner.spec`, `admin.spec` (en-1280). **Old → new (allowed by the prompt):** `check:branches` §4 "an Owner can add a branch within the limit" (and its owner self-serve assertions) → "no centre role can create a branch, by UI or direct call; a platform admin can, within the limit" (the copy assertions now run through the admin's add); `check:permissions` §8 `organization.branch.create` OWNER-only → "no permission any centre role can hold is a branch action, and Settings → Branches is gone"; `e2e/owner.spec.ts` "sub-nav lists all eight" → "all nine (no Branches)" (the list now names Branding, which the old one skipped, and counts the links). **New:** every centre role refused (no centre file reaches a branch writer; each admin action starts with `requirePlatformAdmin()`); the pause cancels only scheduled in-range sessions and tags them; started/closed/paid untouched; refusals while paused; early end restores only untouched tagged sessions; each delete refusal; a deleted branch absent from every resolution and count; restore within window and limit; `e2e/admin.spec.ts` "branch tools".
**Docs:** 00 (§2, §2.2 OD-3 and OD-15, §4.1 item 13, §4.4 "Branch management", §5.1 "Branch architecture" and "Branch pause and delete"), 01 (Organization, Center, BranchPause, Session), 02 (§4, §6, §6.2), 04 (§11.21), 05 (entry 33), `dev-accounts.md`, this file.

### U6 — Three history limits: centre data, individual teachers, the teacher's own portal (delivered)
**Context:** decided with Kamal on 29 Oct — the single M19 window (default 30 days) was too short for a centre's own analytics and the wrong tool for a teacher's own history. Follow-ups decided during the build (31 Oct): the Sessions list's teacher filter stays on the centre window; Analytics with a teacher selected reads **wholly** under the teacher window; the two centre windows are **independent**; expense backdating follows the centre window.
1. **Two centre windows.** `CenterSettings.centerDataVisibilityDays` (1–365, default 365) — the centre's own analytics, money, sessions and attendance, i.e. everything the single window covered — and `teacherDataVisibilityDays` (1–30, default 30) — the Analytics by-teacher breakdown and every Analytics view filtered to a teacher, per branch in the roll-up too. `lib/db/visibility.ts`: `visibilityWindowFloor` (centre; what `scoped(ctx)` injects) and `teacherVisibilityWindowFloor`. The read-by-read table is in `00-master-product-spec.md` §5 "Data visibility window". The teacher detail page has no date-bounded stats, so nothing there needed a window.
2. **Editing.** Settings → General's card and the admin's override on `/admin/centers/[id]` each show both fields with their caps and one line on what each covers; same permission (`center.settings.edit`) and the same platform-admin override as before; both audited before/after. The server refuses anything outside the caps (`visibilityWindowField`).
3. **Teacher portal.** Never limited by a centre window (it was not before either: `centreReadFloor` is `null` for TEACHER — nothing to remove). New `User.teacherHistoryDays` (0 = all, default; 1–3650): Account → "History shown in my teacher portal", only for a person with an active TEACHER membership (refused server-side otherwise). Applied to the dashboard (week count and month earnings start at the floor), the session list and its past days, students and their type-ahead, a student's history and a session's detail/print (out of view → not found). Never to settlements or a write's integrity check.
4. **Migration `20261031150000_u6_split_visibility_windows`** (additive): the two `CenterSettings` columns and `User.teacherHistoryDays`; existing branches backfilled from `dataVisibilityDays`, clamped to each cap. `dataVisibilityDays` stays, deprecated, read by nothing but the branch-copy carry-over — drop after the pilot (backlog entry 34).
**Done when:** centre analytics look back up to 365 days and teacher reads up to 30; both caps are refused server-side; a teacher sees their whole history by default and can limit it in Account settings; settlements and the audit log stay unwindowed.
**Gates (staging):** `check:visibility` (§4 new), `check:analytics`, `check:teacher-portal` (§2b new), `check:timezone`, `check:admin`, `check:settlements`, `check:isolation`; e2e `owner.spec` general settings, `admin.spec`, `teacher.spec` (en-1280). **Old → new (allowed by the prompt):** `check:visibility`/`check:timezone`/`check:admin` assertions on `dataVisibilityDays` → the two fields, their caps and their backfill; "no settings row falls back to the 30-day window" → each field's default (365 / 30); `e2e/owner.spec.ts` "general settings … window" and `admin.spec.ts` "override its window" → both windows. **Also renamed, approved in the session:** the hand-built contexts in `check:security` SEC-51 and `check:session-lifecycle` (`dataVisibilityDays` → `centerDataVisibilityDays`, same values). **New:** each read uses its window (by direct call and statically); caps refused server-side (settings, admin override, account preference); teacher portal unlimited at 0 and limited when set; the windows independent; the backfill SQL; `check:branches` copies both windows.
**Docs:** 00 (§1 table, §5 "Data visibility window", D-24, criterion 28, glossary), 01 (CenterSettings, User, §7), 02 (`visibility.ts`, Layer 1b), 04 (§11.10, §11.20, §11.27), 05 (entry 34), this file.

### U7 — Straight to your portal, the teacher's All branches, and a Calendar for students, teachers and parents (delivered)
**Context:** students never pick a branch; a teacher at several branches sees them all at once; students, teachers and parents get a month calendar. Decided in the session (31 Oct): the teacher portal's choice lives in **its own cookie** (`kayedu_teacher_branch`, absent = All branches), never `kayedu_center`; under All branches, Approvals lists **only branches that grant** `reservation.manage.own` (no new visibility).
1. **Routing.** `singleExperienceHome` / `experienceHomes` (`lib/auth/tenant.ts`): one experience → straight in (student → `/student/schedule`, teacher → the dashboard in All branches, parent → `/parent`, admin → `/admin/centers`, one staff branch → its dashboard, made active); several → the hub. Sign-in, `/` and the hub ask the same function. The hub offers **Student / Teacher / Parent portal** cards above the staff branches (staff roles only on a branch card). Sign-in and sign-out reset the teacher portal to All branches.
2. **Student shell.** No branch switcher, no branch in the shell (platform brand). What the active branch affected for a student before: the shell's brand (the first student branch's name, logo and colour) and a switcher whose rows set `kayedu_center` — no student read or action depended on it (the schedule, Discover, My requests, the rating pop-up and the completion gate were already identity-scoped or per-branch unions). `studentBrand`/`StudentSession.studentRelation` removed.
3. **Teacher All branches.** `lib/auth/teacher-scope.ts`: `getTeacherScope` (contexts per branch, the choice, `inView`), `branchesInView` (the `?branch=` filter, within the scope only), `findInBranches` (a detail page's row, by the same scoped lookup per branch), `firstGranting` (page gates), `teacherBranchContext` (the write guard: `getTenantContextForBranch(centerId, "teacher")`) and `teacherActionScope` (type-aheads). Dashboard sums with a per-branch breakdown; Sessions, Students (counted paging across branches), Classes, Approvals, Settlements and Calendar are unions of per-branch scoped reads with a Branch label and filter. Every teacher write posts its row's branch (or a Branch select — the request forms list only trusted, granting branches) and re-resolves it. Header switcher: All branches pinned, then each branch (`selectTeacherBranchAction`). Nav = union of each branch's own nav (`teacherNavAcross`). The print layout no longer resolves an active branch.
4. **Calendar.** One component, `SessionCalendar` (`components/shared/session-calendar.tsx`), rules in `lib/calendar.ts`; `/student/calendar`, `/teacher/calendar`, `/parent/calendar` (selected child). Student/parent entries come from `studentCalendarMonth` (the schedule's `studentUpcomingSessions` / `studentPastAttendance`, now with optional month bounds) plus `bookedCalendarEntries`; teacher entries from `scoped(ctx).sessions.search(teacherSessionListWhere(...))` per branch, counts from `sessionAttendanceCounts`. Week start shared with the date control (`calendarWeekStart`). No schema change.
**Done when:** a student signs straight in with no branch anywhere in the shell; a teacher at two branches sees totals and lists for both and acts on each session at its own branch; the calendar shows the right colours past and future and lists a tapped day's sessions, in EN and AR at 375px.
**Gates (staging, run 31 Oct):** `check:teacher-portal` ✓ (new: day-list counts — live absence not counted, drop-in no-show never absent, centre-scoped; tones), `check:student-portal` ✓ (new: month/day/grid rules; Present/Late/Absent/drop-in/live/Cancelled/Upcoming/moved; enrolled wins over a booking; branch isolation), `check:parent` ✓, `check:isolation` ✓, `check:permissions` ✓, `check:approvals` ✓, `check:analytics` ✓, `check:timezone` — one **pre-existing** failure in files U7 did not touch (`app/[locale]/(admin)/admin/centers/[id]/page.tsx:152`, `lib/db/branch-admin.ts:458`, from U5); every other assertion passes. e2e: `navigation`, `teacher`, `student`, `parent` in en-1280 — 40 passed; `navigation` in ar-375 — 31 passed. **Old → new (allowed or approved in the session):** `seed-verify`, `permissions-check` and `e2e/navigation.spec.ts` nav lists gain `calendar`; `teacherShared` lands without the hub; `e2e/teacher.spec.ts` "two centres" → lands in All branches, the switcher narrows and returns; `check:security` SEC-44 → a teacher action resolves its TEACHER role at the row's branch (`teacherBranchContext` / `teacherActionScope`, never `getTenantContext(`). `check:design`'s void-action allowlist gains `selectTeacherBranchAction`.
**Docs:** 00 (§4.2 items 0, 0a, 2a; §4.3 items 0a, 1a; §4.5 item 3 and landing), 02 (§3 steps 3–4, §4 "The teacher portal's scope", `requireTeacherUser`, §14 union paging), 04 (§11.28, §11.29), 06 (three new pages), this file.

### U8 — Leave a class, cancel what you asked for, confirm before joining (delivered)
**Context:** decided with Kamal on 31 Oct. A student could join a class or ask for one in a single tap and could not undo it. Leaving is the student's own `REMOVED`, marked as theirs; every join states what happens first.
1. **Leave class.** `Enrollment.leftByStudentAt`; `leaveClass` (`lib/db/reservations.ts`) / `leaveClassAction`; the button on the class page, the schedule's upcoming rows and the calendar's day list; audit `enrollment.leave` (ids only); the centre's student page reads "Left <class> on <date>, by the student". `claimClassSeat` now resets `enrolledAt` and clears the mark when it reactivates a non-active row, and `reserveOrEnroll` no longer treats a CONFIRMED reservation without a live enrolment as "already enrolled" (it is reused as a new request) — both needed for a rejoin.
2. **Cancel.** `CancelReservationButton` / `CancelBookingButton` open a confirmation and appear on the class page (pending request; each booked session), My requests, the schedule and the calendar's day list. The same existing functions.
3. **Confirm before joining.** `ReserveButton` / `BookSessionButton` open a summary dialog (`lib/join-summary.ts`, `JoinSummaryList`); `EnrolAction` takes the class and branch to build it; Explore's guest link carries `join=1` and the class page reopens the dialog, never submitting it. `discoverClassSelect` gains `startDate` / `endDate`.
4. **Migration `20261031180000_u8_student_leave_class`** (additive): the nullable column.
**Done when:** a student leaves a class and isn't marked absent afterwards; another student's or another branch's enrolment can't be ended; every join shows the confirmation first; every cancel is available where the item appears.
**Gates (staging, run 31 Oct):** `check:student-portal` (new U8 block), `check:session-lifecycle` (new U8 block), `check:enrollment`, `check:approvals`, `check:isolation`; e2e `student.spec` @write in en-1280. **Old → new:** `e2e/student.spec.ts` "enrols instantly" now confirms the dialog; no gate assertion was edited. **Note:** one pre-existing, timing-dependent assertion in `check:session-lifecycle` ("the receipt's change list reads them back in order") failed once and passed on the immediate re-run; it orders rows written in the same millisecond and U8 does not touch it.
**Docs:** 00 (§4.3 items 2b, 2c), 01 (Enrollment), 02 (reservations module, §11.0, the student-portal gate), 04 (§11.30), this file.

### U9 — Only teachers have photos; a class can have a cover (delivered)
**Context:** decided with Kamal on 31 Oct. F8's personal profile photo never had a storage destination and nobody needed it; what students actually look for is *who teaches the class*. So personal photos go, and the teacher's photo and a class cover appear on the class cards.
1. **Personal photos removed.** The Account → Profile photo section, `updateProfilePhotoAction` / `removeProfilePhotoAction`, `MEDIA_AVATARS_DISPLAY`, `profilePhotoForDisplay` and the `photoSrc` plumbing through `ShellUser`/`UserMenu`/`UserAvatar` are gone; everyone keeps the initials avatar. `User.avatarUrl` stays, unused, deprecated (backlog entry 35). Student ID-card photos are unchanged.
2. **Teacher photo, per branch.** `Membership.photoKey`; `lib/db/media-writes.ts`; the teacher's new **Profile** page (`/teacher/profile`, nav item *Profile*) with *Use this photo at all my branches*; the centre teacher page's *Photo* card (`teacher.manage`). Audited `teacher.photo.update` / `teacher.photo.remove`.
3. **Class cover.** `ClassGroup.coverKey`; the centre class page's *Cover image* card (`class.manage`) and the teacher's own classes on their Profile page. Audited `classGroup.cover.update` / `.remove`.
4. **Display.** `ClassCard` / `ClassDetail` take `imageUrl` (cover → teacher photo → the generated look); the public teacher page shows the teacher's photo. `/api/teacher-photos/[centerId]/[teacherUserId]` and `/api/class-images/[centerId]/[classId]` (the `/api/logos` pattern; guests only for listed branches, listed classes).
5. **Uploads.** `ImageUploadForm`; `MEDIA_UPLOADS_ENABLED` off → *Coming soon* on every image control **including the Branding logo form**, nothing stored. Buckets reused (`avatars` for teacher photos, `logos` for covers) — no bucket SQL.
6. **Migration `20261031210000_u9_teacher_photo_class_cover`** (additive): the two nullable columns.
**Done when:** no account has a personal photo control; a teacher and the branch can set the teacher's photo; a class cover can be set; cards show cover → teacher photo → generated; guests see images only for listed branches.
**Gates (staging, 31 Oct):** `check:people` (new U9 block), `check:security` (new `U9` assertion), `check:student-portal` (two new checks), `check:isolation`; plus `check:account`, `check:public` (new block), `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`. **Old → new (allowed by the prompt):** `check:student-portal`'s class allowlist gained `imageUrl`. **Not changed:** the teacher-page assertion ("exactly teacherUserId, name, rating and classes") — the teacher's photo is read by a separate `publicTeacherPhotoUrl`, so that shape holds.
**Notes:** the teacher portal had no profile area and no class cards, so the Profile page is new (photo card per branch + the teacher's classes as `ClassCard`s whose action slot is the cover control). The public Branch page's teacher list keeps initials (photos are for teacher pages and class cards).
**Docs:** 00 (§2.1 M20 media box, §4.6 out-of-scope line, §5.1 U9), 01 (User, Membership, ClassGroup), 02 (§2 tree, §10 buckets, media switch, teacher photos and class covers), 03 (this entry, F2, F8, M20), 04 (§5 avatar and image controls, §10 class card, teacher page), 05 (entry 35), 06 (Profile page), `deploy-vercel.md`.

### V1 — Three small defects from the U-batch (items 1 and 3 delivered; item 2 open)
**Context:** no schema change.
1. **`check:timezone` (delivered).** The two spots U5 introduced now resolve "today" through `todayInTimezone` in the **branch's own** zone: `hasLiveSession` (`lib/db/branch-admin.ts`) and the admin branch page (`admin/centers/[id]/page.tsx`, which derives the pause form's `todayKey` and the pause's *current* flag from `centreTimeZone(settings)` instead of `toISOString().slice`). The gate is unchanged.
2. **Receipt change-list order (open — awaiting a decision).** `listSessionAttendanceChanges` orders by `changedAt` alone, and `changedAt` is stamped by the **app's** clock (`applyAttendanceChange`, `params.now`), so two changes made with the same `now` tie — which is exactly what `check:session-lifecycle` does (one `vNow` for every change). The ids are random `uuid`s and the model has no sequence column, so nothing available recovers true insertion order; a tiebreaker on `id` would be deterministic but arbitrary (and would not satisfy the gate's "S1 first" expectation). Nothing was changed. Options are with the owner: (a) add a monotonic `seq` column (`BigInt @default(autoincrement())`, additive migration) and order by `changedAt, seq`; (b) have the writer stamp a strictly increasing `changedAt` under the session lock it already holds; (c) accept a deterministic non-causal tiebreak and edit the assertion.
3. **Teacher Approvals tabs keep `?branch=` (delivered).** `QueueTabs` takes an optional `keep` map folded into every pill `href`; `/teacher/approvals` passes the validated branch filter. (The phone Select already merged into the live query string.)
**Done when:** `check:timezone` passes in full ✔; switching Approvals tabs keeps the branch filter ✔ (manual check); `check:session-lifecycle` passes twice in a row — **not claimed**, item 2 is unchanged.
**Gates (staging, 31 Oct):** `tsc`, `eslint`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `check:timezone`, `check:approvals`, `check:teacher-portal`, and `check:session-lifecycle` (passed once; the flaky assertion is the open item). No assertion edited.
**Docs:** 03 (this entry), 04 (§11.3 type tabs).

## W-batch — Assessments, notifications, role switcher, premium teachers, assistants, PERSONAL workspaces (W1–W9 delivered)
**Context:** decided 3 Nov 2026 with the founder (prompt W0, docs only). Decisions D-W01…D-W29 (D-W27–D-W29 from the W0 follow-up, which decided OD-16 and OD-17) and the contradictions with earlier locked decisions W-C1…W-C9 are in `00-master-product-spec.md` §5.1 "W-batch" and §2.2. Planned entities: `01-data-model.md` "Planned — W-batch". Architecture: `02-architecture.md` §18. Screens: `04-design-system.md` §11.31–§11.38. **Every DB change below is additive** (a new table, or a nullable / defaulted column). Each milestone's migration is checked with the read-only `migrate diff --from-url` and applied with `migrate deploy` (CLAUDE.md rule 7). **No milestone adds a cron, a route handler on a timer, or polling.** Order: W1 first (W3 and W7 send notices); W2 → W3 → W4; W6 before W7 and W8; W8a → W8b → W8c → W8d; W9; W10 (share codes, after W8b) last.

### W1 — Notifications foundation (delivered 3 Nov 2026)
**Build:** the `Notification` table and `NotificationKind` enum (`01-data-model.md` "Notifications layer"); `lib/db/notifications.ts` (`notify` in the causing transaction, the four producers, the reads with the access re-check, mark one / all read, the read-time name loader); `lib/notifications/kinds.ts` (each kind's payload ids, audience and deep link); `lib/notifications/present.ts` (text from next-intl at read time) and `reach.ts` (the per-request reach and the bell's cached count); the **bell** with an unread count in **every** shell header — centre, teacher, student, parent, admin, the hub shell, and the `(auth)` header on the branch hub — opening a panel with the latest 10 and a **See all** link; the **inbox** `/notifications` (inside the shell it came from, `?from=`, paged 20, Mark all as read); `EMAIL_ENABLED` (off) and its one hook point (`lib/notify/email.ts`); `formatRelativeTime` in `lib/format.ts`; Account settings and the inbox share one shell selector (`components/shell/experience-frame.ts`).
**Producers (v1)** — this milestone's prompt **widened the plan**, which had emitted no kinds before W3/W7 (D-W12's producers list): an **enrolment request** approved or declined, and a **single-session booking** approved or declined (both on the student's "My requests", so both count as "their request") → the student; a **session cancelled** (by staff, its teacher, a **branch pause** or a class deactivation — all `cancelSession`) or **rescheduled** (old and new time) → every ACTIVE enrolled student and every CONFIRMED booker. Each producer runs inside its event writer, on the event's own `tx`. Parents are not notified, nothing is pruned, and an "End pause now" restore sends no notice (backlog 36–38).
**DB:** yes — migration `20261103120000_w1_notifications`: the enum, the table (unique `(userId, dedupeKey)`, index `(userId, readAt, createdAt)`, `centerId` cascade), **RLS on with zero policies**, and a CHECK that the payload is a flat object of uuids (ids only). Additive; generated with the read-only `migrate diff --from-url`, no `DROP INDEX` to remove; applied with `migrate deploy` to staging and dev.
**Done when:** a student whose request is approved or declined, or whose session is cancelled or moved, sees the bell count rise, opens the item and lands on the right page (My schedule / My requests); another user sees nothing; producing the same event twice creates one row; nothing is emailed.
**Gates (staging, 3 Nov, one at a time):** new **`check:notifications`** (registered in CI, so in `check:all`, and in the gate-fixtures registry — stem `notifications-check`; it needs no seeded fixture, so `staging.md` is unchanged): every producer writes one row per recipient and nobody unrelated gets one; a rolled-back approval, decline, booking approval or cancellation leaves no row, and every producer call passes the event's `tx` (static); dedupe; own rows only (list, open by id, mark all); the access re-check hides on a deactivated profile, a suspended and a deleted branch, deletes nothing, and restores; ids-only payloads, refused by the writer and by the CHECK; RLS on, zero policies; `EMAIL_ENABLED` off sends nothing and no cron exists — **passed**. `check:rls`, `check:approvals`, `check:isolation`, `check:gate-fixtures` — passed. `check:session-lifecycle` — passed on the re-run; the first run hit the known same-millisecond ordering flake (U8 note above), unrelated. `check:branches` — **one static failure, pre-existing and environmental, not W1**: "requirePlatformAdmin() is its first statement" regex-matches `{\n` in `app/[locale]/(admin)/admin/centers/branch-actions.ts`, untouched here; this Windows checkout (`core.autocrlf=true`) has CRLF endings, so it matches 0 of 5 actions — with LF endings all 5 match. Statics: `tsc`, `eslint`, `prisma validate`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`. **E2E (staging):** `navigation.spec` en-1280 and ar-375 — **71 passed** (the setup project, plus every role and the hub in both projects — 24 of the runs are the new bell tests). **No existing assertion was edited**; `navigation.spec` gains a per-role "the header's bell opens the notification inbox" test and a hub-bell test.
**Docs:** 00 (D-W12 producers list), 01 ("Notifications layer"), 02 §18, 03 (this entry), 04 §5 App bar and §11.31, 05 (entries 36–38, entry 30 note), 06 (the inbox page), `deploy-vercel.md` (`EMAIL_ENABLED`).

### W2 — Assessment model + builder (delivered 4 Nov 2026)
**Build:**
- **Data:** the three tables (`01-data-model.md` "Assessments layer").
- **`lib/assessments.ts`** (pure): the per-type defaults; the D-W03 deadline; the save limits (`draftErrors`); "Ready to publish" (`readiness`); the shuffled order (`drawOrder`); the **student serializer** (`STUDENT_ASSESSMENT_SELECT` + `toStudentAssessment`, never `isCorrect`).
- **`lib/db/assessments.ts`:** `resolveAssessmentTeacher`, create / save / delete (actor and owning teacher as separate parameters, audited), the owner's reads, `homeworkDeadlineFor`, and `isAssessmentLocked` (false until W3, asked by every question/option write).
- **The new permission `assessment.manage.own`** (D-W30): in TEACHER's baseline (70 → 71 rows), with a hard ceiling for the other staff roles.
- **Teacher portal:**
  - the nav item **Exams & homework** (`assessments`);
  - the list `/teacher/assessments`, filtered by type, class and branch;
  - `/teacher/assessments/new` (choose a class, or the builder in create mode — nothing is written until the first save);

**DB:** yes — migration `20261104090000_w2_assessments`.
- The two enums and the three tables, plus RLS on with zero policies.
- CHECKs: type fields, window order, duration 1–600, points 1–100, text lengths.
- The partial unique index `AssessmentOption_one_correct_per_question`.
- The idempotent backfill of the TEACHER `assessment.manage.own` row for every existing branch.

Additive. It was generated with the read-only `migrate diff --from-url`, and produced no `DROP INDEX` to remove. Applied with `migrate deploy` to staging and dev.
