### W3a — Publishing + student taking (delivered 5 Nov 2026)
**Scope split:** W3 became W3a (this entry) and W3b (below), per the W3a prompt.
**Built:**
- **Publish** (DRAFT → PUBLISHED, moved here from W2), **unpublish** while no attempt exists, **extend** only later and only before it has passed, and **delete** for a draft or a published row with no attempt. A published row opens read-only on the teacher's page.
- **`AssessmentAttempt` / `AssessmentAnswer`** and `isAssessmentLocked` given its body (any attempt exists; no `lockedAt`, D-W31).
- **Student side:** the `assessments` nav item, the list grouped by state and branch, start with a confirmation, autosave (rate-limited), one question at a time with a question grid, a server-driven countdown, a review screen, submit with the unanswered count, one-sitting heartbeat and close signal, the result, and the reveal after close.
- **Server rules:** derived end, lazy finalisation (`finaliseIfDue`), grading, the seeded shuffle, and correctness read only by grading and the reveal.
- **OD-18 decided (D-W31):** an assessment makes its session non-pristine (kept reason `assessments`), and session and class deletes are refused while one exists.
- **Also:** teacher and student delete blockers count assessments and attempts, and the centre audit screen omits both (D-W11).
- **Tooling:** the e2e title prefix and its staging-guarded teardown (`npm run e2e:clean-assessments`). `E2E_PORT` already existed.
**DB:** migration `20261105090000_w3a_assessment_attempts`: additive, RLS on both tables, plus `AssessmentAttempt_final_check`.
**Gates:** `check:assessments` sections 10–20; `check:class-periods` gains the `assessments` reason, the kept report and the delete blocker; e2e `assessments.spec.ts` (`@write`); the student nav list in `seed:verify` and `navigation.spec`.
**Docs:** 00 (D-W31, §4.3 item 1b, OD-18 closed), 01 ("Assessments layer"), 02 §18, 03 (this entry and W3b), 04 §11.33–§11.34, 05 (entry 39), 06 (two new pages), `staging.md`.
**Follow-up (test hardening, no product change):** the one-sitting close route's origin check is now `lib/same-origin.ts` — an exact `scheme://host[:port]` match against `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` when it is set (otherwise the request's own forwarded scheme and host); a missing, unparsable or foreign `Origin` is refused, and so is a `Sec-Fetch-Site` other than `same-origin`. It replaced a scheme-stripping comparison with the `Host` header. Every single-timestamp `orderBy` under `lib/db` gained `id` as its tiebreaker (rows written in one transaction share a timestamp); `check:session-lifecycle` asserts it for the session change log. `check:assessments` asserts the origin check. **V8 (old → new):** the `check:session-lifecycle` assertion "The receipt's change list reads them back in order…" leaned on the physical order of rows that share a `changedAt` (it read `changeRows[0]` as the voided-payment row and `changeRows[3]` as not). It now asserts the list equals the rows sorted by `(changedAt, id)`, that `changedAt` never decreases, and finds the voided-payment row by content. `check:notifications` replaces the kind count with a hand-written list of the ten expected kind names. The fixtures are minute-aligned and the extend test passes the exact closing instant; the access test waits for each role's destination (the same URL for a 404, `/teacher/centers` for a student on the builder) before reading the page. `e2e/assessments.spec.ts` gains four `@write` tests — a one-sitting exam submitted by closing the page, the reveal in four cases, extend with locked controls, and access by other roles — using exams inserted straight into the database under the shared prefix.

### W3b — Notices + calendar (delivered 6 Nov 2026)
**Built:**
- **Four notice kinds (D-W32):** `ASSESSMENT_PUBLISHED` and `ASSESSMENT_EXTENDED` in their writers' transactions; `ASSESSMENT_OPENS_SOON` and `ASSESSMENT_DUE_SOON` computed when a student's bell or inbox renders. Ids only, text at read time, the deep link is the assessment.
- **The re-check:** hidden when the assessment is no longer published, the class is inactive, the enrolment is gone or the branch is out of reach; a "soon" notice also once the attempt is final; an earlier publish's notice after a republish. Deleting an assessment deletes its notices. The publish confirmation says students get a notification.
- **Calendars:** exams and homework on the student and teacher calendars, on the right day in the session's frozen timezone, with a legend entry each, type and state badges and a link. The teacher's row adds started and submitted on a published row. The parent calendar is unchanged.
**DB:** migration `20261106090000_w3b_assessment_notices`: additive, four enum values and `Assessment(centerId, status, windowStart)` / `(centerId, status, deadline)`.
**Gates:** `check:notifications` sections 9–15; `check:assessments` sections 21–23; e2e `assessments.spec.ts` (`@write`: the bell rises, the panel opens the assessment, the calendar shows it; the teardown removes the notices by key prefix). Also run on staging: `check:student-portal`, `check:teacher-portal`, `check:timezone`, `check:performance`, `check:isolation`, `check:rls`, `check:enrollment`, `check:gate-fixtures`, `seed:verify`, `navigation.spec`.
**Results (staging, 6 Nov 2026):** the named gates pass, and `check:notifications` passes except the one assertion below. The new W3b journey passed in en-1280 and ar-375 on both runs, `navigation.spec` is 71 passed, and a second run left the same row counts.
**Open, waiting on the founder (no existing test was edited):**
- `check:notifications` still asserts `NOTIFICATION_KIND_LIST.length === 6`. The four new kinds make it 10, so it fails until that literal changes.
- `assessments.spec` "a published exam with an attempt" fails intermittently on W3a too: the fixture passes `endsInMs` relative to its own later `Date.now()`, so `windowEnd` can land 1 ms off the time the test compares it with.
- `assessments.spec` "other roles cannot open…" moves on before the redirect of the page before it has finished, so its next `goto` can be aborted (`ERR_ABORTED`). It passed on W3a and failed 4 of 4 full-spec runs here. A trace shows the student is bounced correctly and the two navigations about 8 ms apart: a race in the test, which W3b's extra bell reads appear to have shifted.
**Docs:** 00 (D-W32, §4.2 item 2a, §4.3 items 1a and 1b), 01 (Notifications and Assessments layers), 02 §18, this entry, 04 §11.28, §11.31, §11.34, `staging.md`.

### W4 — Results (delivered 6 Nov 2026)
**Built (D-W33):**
- **`/teacher/assessments/[id]/results`**, published only (a draft opens its builder): eligible now, started, submitted, in progress, not started and missed; average, highest and lowest over submitted attempts, or "No submissions yet"; the students with status, score, percentage, time taken, how it ended, started and submitted, with search, a status filter and three sorts; a Left class badge; each question's percentage correct and how many chose each option.
- **`…/results/[attemptId]`:** each question in the assessment's order, the chosen and correct options, points, and a text badge; an in-progress attempt shows the time left and returns no answers.
- **`lib/db/assessment-results.ts`,** a separate read from the student's, over the pure `lib/assessment-results.ts`: the actor and the owning teacher as separate parameters, the membership and the cell re-read on every call, and what is due finalised first (D-W10).
- **Entry points:** "n / m submitted" and a **Results** action on a published row in the teacher's lists, a header action on the assessment's page, and a published row on the calendar now links to its results.
- **The history window (U6) now applies to published assessments,** superseding that sentence in D-W31 and D-W32: older than `teacherHistoryDays` by closing day is not listed, and its pages read as not found. Drafts are always listed.
**Decided with the founder at W4, against the prompt or the plan as written:** a student who joined after it closed is left out of the table, Eligible and Missed, not shown as Missed (the rule of the student's own list, one function, `joinedAfterClose`). The reveal toggles stay locked after an attempt exists, so "editable afterwards" left this entry for the backlog (entry 43).
**Also, beyond the prompt's list:** an ACTIVE enrolment of a **deactivated** profile with no attempt is not listed or counted, since it cannot start. The same applies to its "Left class" badge: an attempt whose student has no ACTIVE enrolment of an active profile reads as left.
**DB:** no. No migration and no new index.
**Gates:** `check:assessments` sections 24–33: every refused role by direct call and the permission switch-off; summary counts; average, highest and lowest over submitted only; a derived percentage; unchanged scores after a question changes (and edits refused through the writer); time taken capped; in-progress attempts returning no answers; per-question and per-option counts; lazy finalisation inside the read; the left-class flag; the history window; cross-centre and All-branches isolation; the student path still free of correctness; statics. A temporary mutation run (authority, the cell, finalisation, the history window, in-progress answers, late joiners) failed 22 checks and was reverted. e2e `assessment-results.spec.ts` (`@write`, `en-1280` and `ar-375`).
**Results (staging, 6 Nov 2026):** `check:assessments` 443 passed, `check:performance`, `check:isolation`, `check:teacher-portal`, `check:permissions` and `check:gate-fixtures` pass. The new spec passes in both projects. Row counts were the same before and after each run (no assessment rows left, audit rows unchanged). The full e2e suite and `check:all` were not run.

### W5 — Role switcher (delivered 6 Nov 2026)
**Built (D-W34):**
- **`listExperiences(userId)`,** the one list behind the switcher, the hub and sign-in's landing: staff at each branch, Whole Management, Teacher (All branches), Student, Parent (an ACTIVE link only) and Platform admin, built only from the existing resolvers. `lib/auth/experiences.ts` is pure, so the gates run the real thing, and `resolveOwnerOrgGroups` moved to `relations.ts` for the same reason. `experienceHomes` now reads the list.
- **The switcher** (`components/shell/experience-switcher.tsx`) next to the user menu in the centre, teacher, student, parent and admin shells and the hub shell, through each shell's frame, so Account settings and the inbox have it too. It is not rendered with one experience or none. At 375px it is a full-width bottom sheet with 44px rows, and it is a keyboard-operable menu with `aria-current`.
- **`switchExperienceAction`:** looks the key up in the current list, runs the existing selector and lands on `entry.href`, with no query and no `next`.
- **`kayedu_last_experience`:** `{key, centerId?}`, httpOnly, sameSite lax, secure in production, 90 days. A hint only: `resolveLastExperience` ignores a stale or tampered value and the hub clears it. Only the hub reads it.
- **The hub** lists the last used first, labelled **Last used**, with **Continue**, and never redirects on it. One experience still goes straight in.
**Decided at W5, beyond the prompt's list (recorded in D-W34):** choosing Teacher clears the teacher portal's narrowing, because the row says "All branches". The centre's branch switcher records the last used too, since moving branch is moving experience. The cookie survives sign-out, because remembering across sign-ins is its purpose. The `/select-center` page carries no switcher, because the page is the list. The hub's portal cards are now forms, not links, so choosing one counts as a switch.
**Read against the prompt:** the prompt's e2e line "teacher.shared switches between both branches' teacher view and back" cannot use the role switcher, because a teacher at two branches has one experience (U7, the prompt's decisions 1 and 4). The test asserts that account has no role switcher and that the portal's own branch switcher moves between both branches and back to All branches.
**DB:** no. No migration and no seed change.
**Gates (staging, 6 Nov 2026):** `check:people` W5 block (real rows: multi-kind, Owner, two-branch staff, a branch grant, verified, revoked and declared parents, suspended, archived and deleted branches, no centre, access lost, a forged cookie), and it is where the database cases live because `check:account` runs in CI's no-database job. `check:account` W5 block (the list's order and shape, the cookie's rules, who may read it, the action's order, every shell). `check:isolation`, `check:permissions`, `check:student-portal`, `check:teacher-portal`, `check:parent`, `check:gate-fixtures`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `tsc` and `eslint` pass. E2E: `navigation.spec` and the new `experience-switcher.spec` in `en-1280` and `ar-375`: 89 passed, 2 skipped by design (the 375px layout test on the desktop project, the sign-up journey on `ar-375`). The admin shell has no e2e journey, because no seeded account is an admin with a second experience and the e2e account cleanup refuses platform admins; `check:account` asserts its frame carries the switcher.
**One assertion edited (a registry entry, not a meaning):** `check:design`'s `VOID_ACTION_ALLOWLIST` gains `switchExperienceAction`, which ends in `redirect()`, the same entry U7 added for `selectTeacherBranchAction`.
**Open, waiting on the founder (no existing test was edited):**
- `check:account` "the account page renders every experience's shared frame" **has failed since W1** (bb5d63b), and it fails on this milestone's base commit too. W1 moved the five shell calls from the account page to `shellFrameFor` (`components/shell/experience-frame.ts`), and the assertion still looks for them in the page. The behaviour is intact. It fails CI's static job until the assertion reads the new file.

### W6 — Premium entitlement (delivered 7 Nov 2026)
**Built (D-W35):**
- **`User.premiumUntil`** (UTC, nullable): the Cairo midnight after the last covered day. Premium is `premiumUntil > now`, decided only by `isPremium` in `lib/auth/premium.ts`, worked out per request; no stored flag, no cron.
- **`requirePremium(db, userId, now)`**: reads the account fresh and answers the typed `premiumRequired`. Built and tested, **called by nothing yet**: W7 and W8 call it. *(Since W8a its callers are `lib/db/assistants.ts` and `lib/db/workspaces.ts`.)*
- **Admin → Teachers** (`/admin/teachers`, nav key `premium`): an exact-email lookup (case-insensitive, trimmed; no list, no partial search), the account's name, email and status, and **Grant** (end date required), **Extend** (strictly later) and **Revoke** (to null, confirmed). An end date is today or later, at most 400 days ahead, the end of that day in Africa/Cairo. `lib/db/premium.ts` is the only writer: it re-checks the platform admin before anything else (`forbidden`, the catalog's lock), writes conditionally on the value it read, and audits `platform.premium.grant|extend|revoke` with the user's id and the new end date only.
- **Account → Premium**, for every account: the status (Not premium, Premium until <date>, Expired on <date>, "Ends in n days" within 14 days), what premium adds and "Contact KayEdu to subscribe or renew". One `PremiumStatusBadge` for both pages.
**Decided by the W6 prompt, against this entry's plan:** three audit actions, not `platform.premium.update`; the Premium card on Account settings for every account, not a badge on the teacher's own pages; an admin page found by email, not a control on "the teacher's admin page" (none exists). Recorded in D-W35.

A mutation run (`>=` in `isPremium`, the writer's admin re-check removed) failed 18 checks and was reverted. Also on staging, one at a time: `check:permissions`, `check:rls`, `check:isolation`, `check:people`, `check:gate-fixtures`, `check:account` and `seed:verify` pass. `check:design` (after adding the page to `06-design-pass-survey.md`), `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `tsc`, `eslint` and `prisma validate` pass.

The teardown removes the login (the `e2eTag` fixture) and its `platform.premium.*` rows; a second run left the same row counts (users 145, premium audit rows 0, audit rows 1,653). `navigation.spec` 71 passed. ### W7a — Assistants: invites, the catalogue and the assistant shell (delivered 7 Nov 2026)
**Built (D-W36):**
- **`AssistantInvite`, `TeacherAssistant`, `AssistantPermissionGrant`** and the `AssistantPreset` enum; `lib/db/assistants.ts` the only writer, `lib/assistant-catalog.ts` the fixed catalogue (8 keys), the three presets, Custom detection and the 12-symbol code.
- **Teacher → Assistants** (`/teacher/assistants`, nav key `assistants`, every teacher): without premium a card explaining it and "Contact KayEdu", no action; with premium the slots line ("n of 5"), **Invite an assistant** (preset tiles, then the code shown once with Copy code / Copy link and its expiry), unused invites with their expiry and **Revoke**, and each assistant with their preset or Custom, permissions, **Edit permissions** (a preset or the toggles) and **Remove** (confirmed). Every teacher write: a TEACHER membership + `requirePremium`, inside one transaction holding a row lock on the teacher's `User`; the 5-slot cap counts pending invites.
- **Redemption**, two steps (Continue previews and writes nothing; only Confirm links): `/assistant-join` for guests (sign in or sign up and come back, the code in the URL fragment and the tab) and Account settings → **Join as an assistant**. Rate-limited per account and per IP (10 an hour each). One message for unknown, malformed, expired, used and revoked codes.
- **The assistant experience**: `listExperiences` kind `assistant` ("Assistant of <teacher>", one per **effective** link — live and the teacher premium now); route group `(assistant)`, `/assistant/[teacherId]`, its own shell (platform brand, nav **Home** only, the role switcher), a landing page with the teacher's name and the granted permissions as text. Account settings → **Assistant roles** lists each effective link with Open and **Leave** (confirmed). Nothing to act on yet; no centre data, no `Membership`, no tenant context.
- **Audit:** `assistant.invite.create|revoke|redeem`, `assistant.link.remove|leave`, `assistant.permission.update` — no centre, ids, preset and permission names only.
**Decided at W7a, beyond the prompt's list (recorded in D-W36):** the teacher side also needs an active TEACHER membership, and premium is required on **every** teacher write (revoke and remove too), so a lapsed teacher removes nobody until renewal; an `assistant.invite.redeem` audit row (D-W17 audits every redemption); a self-link CHECK in the database; distinct refusals for own code, a lapsed teacher, an existing link and an unconfirmed email; the stored preset follows the grants when they equal a preset; an unchanged set writes nothing; Account settings lists only effective links, so a lapsed teacher's name is never shown; Account settings and the inbox open from the assistant shell in the hub shell; the join page is `/assistant-join`, outside `/assistant/`; the toggles are checkboxes saved by Save (the permission-matrix rule, `04` §5), the preset tiles follow §11.36's plan; the planned `ASSISTANT_INVITATION` notice is not built — a code invite addresses nobody before redemption.
**Against this entry's W7 plan:** the plan's single W7 is split. W7a is the model, the invites, the catalogue and the shell; **acting on behalf** moved to W7b below. The table names and shapes changed (`AssistantInvite` with no code hint and no status; grants as rows; a partial unique index instead of one re-activated row per pair; a row lock instead of an advisory lock) — `01-data-model.md` "Assistants".
**DB:** migration `20261107120000_w7a_assistants`: additive; generated with the read-only `migrate diff --from-url`, applied with `migrate deploy` to staging and dev. Hand-written: RLS on the three tables, the `codeHash` sha256 CHECK, the no-self CHECK and the partial unique index `TeacherAssistant_pair_live_key … WHERE "revokedAt" IS NULL` (CLAUDE.md rule 7; `check:assistants` fails if it is gone). No `DROP INDEX` was generated.
**Assertions changed (the prompt's old → new list):** `check:admin` "requirePremium has no caller outside the tests yet" → "requirePremium is called only from the assistant invite and permission actions (W7a)" (its one caller, `lib/db/assistants.ts`). The shared teacher nav constant (`scripts/lib/expected-nav.ts`, read by `seed:verify`, `check:permissions` and `navigation.spec`) gains `assistants`. **Registry entries, not meanings:** `check:design`'s `PAGEHEADER_EXEMPT` gains `/assistant-join` (an `AuthCard` page, like `/family/join`), and the gate-fixture registry gains `assistants-check`. `VOID_ACTION_ALLOWLIST` is unchanged (every new action returns a state).
**Gates (staging, 7 Nov 2026):** new **`check:assistants`** (90 checks, in CI and the gate-fixture registry): sha256-only storage and the database's refusal of a plaintext code; the partial index present; one answer for unknown, malformed, expired, used and revoked codes; the expiry boundary (1 ms before redeemable, the instant itself expired) and an expired invite freeing its slot; single use under two concurrent redemptions on two clients; the rate limit per account and per IP; the cap with pending invites under two concurrent creations, the 6th refused, a revoke freeing a slot; no self-assist (writer and CHECK); one live link per pair (writer, index, relink after removal); preset → grants, Custom detection, `students.phones` in no preset, unknown names and presets refused with nothing written, an unchanged set writing nothing; premium expiry with W6's helpers and fixture instants (hidden at the end instant, link and grants kept, every teacher action `premiumRequired`, a lapsed teacher's code `teacherUnavailable`, renewal and a regrant restoring it in the real `loadExperiences`); no actor, a non-premium teacher, premium OWNER / MANAGER / RECEPTIONIST / ACCOUNTANT, a premium student and the platform admin refused on every teacher action and another teacher `notFound`, with nothing written; Leave by anyone else refused; an unconfirmed email refused under `REQUIRE_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION`; a receptionist and a student may redeem; a swapped teacherId finding nothing; every audit action present, no row carrying a centre, a code, its hash, a name or an email; assistants holding no Membership, profile or centre relation; statics (the lock-then-gate order in every teacher writer, rows keyed on the actor, no centre write, the rate limit before any lookup, actions taking the user from the session, the `(assistant)` surface reading no tenant module and declaring no Server Action, `ASSISTANT` not a `TenantRole`, the fragment, Confirm as the only redemption post). Two runs back to back passed. A mutation run (expiry `>=` and the premium gate disabled) failed 8 checks and was reverted. Also on staging, one at a time: `check:admin`, `check:people`, `check:parent`, `check:permissions`, `check:rls` (59 tables locked down), `check:isolation` and `seed:verify` pass; `check:account`, `check:gate-fixtures`, `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `tsc`, `eslint` and `prisma validate` pass. `check:all` and the full e2e suite were not run.
**E2E (staging, `en-1280` and `ar-375`):** new `assistants.spec.ts` (`@write`): two logins made through the Supabase admin API and given TEACHER memberships at Downtown; the teacher's page offers nothing without premium; the admin grants premium; the teacher invites (Co-teacher) and reads the code once; the second account gets the one message for a wrong code, then the confirmation with the teacher's name and permissions (nothing linked yet), confirms, lands in "Assistant of <teacher>", switches to Teacher and back through the role switcher and finds the role on Account settings; the teacher removes them; the route and the Account card are gone on the next request. The teardown deletes both accounts (cascading memberships, invites, links and grants), their audit rows and the assistant-join rate buckets (the local dev server sees one client address); the test asserts nothing of its run is left, and two back-to-back runs left the same row counts (users 145, audit rows 1,654, no assistant rows). `navigation.spec` 71 passed.

### W7b — Assistants act on behalf (delivered 7 Nov 2026)
**Built (D-W37):**
- **Resolution and the one decision.** `lib/db/assistant-acting.ts`:
  - `resolveAssistantActing` reads fresh an effective link (live, the teacher premium now), the teacher's ACTIVE TEACHER memberships at accessible branches whose new `CenterSettings.allowTeacherAssistants` is on, and each branch's stored TEACHER cells and trusted flag.
  - `assistantCan(acting, permission, centerId)` decides alone: the grant ∧ the teacher's equivalent cell (`assessment.manage.own`; `changeRequest.create` + trusted; `analytics.own.view`; `classes.view` = the membership) ∧ the branch setting. Manage covers Review.
  - `assistantMayAt` re-runs it inside the assessment writers, the results reads and the request writer whenever the actor is not the owning teacher.
  - `lib/auth/assistant-context.ts`'s `getAssistantScope` / `assistantActionScope` hand the pages **the teacher's** `TenantContext` per allowed branch (`getTeacherContexts`) and the assistant's floor.
- **Pages** under `/assistant/[teacherId]` (the nav per grant, `lib/assistant-nav.ts`):
  - **Exams & homework**: list, new, builder, preview, publish, unpublish, extend, delete, results and one student's answers. These are the teacher's own bodies, moved into `components/teacher/assessments/*` and driven by a serializable `AssessmentSurface`, over the unchanged W2/W3a/W4 functions with actor = the assistant.
  - **Classes**, read-only: classes, sessions inside the window, roster names.
  - **Requests**: NEW_SESSION and NEW_CLASS through the teacher's forms, stored as the teacher's with `ChangeRequest.viaAssistantUserId`, audited `changeRequest.create`, plus each request's status.
  - **Overview** (Full): `teacherCenterSummary` per branch.
  - The landing page lists the branches where the assistant may work, or "Not available at the teacher's branches".
- **Windows:** `assistantReadFloor`, the stricter of the branch's teacher window and the teacher's `teacherHistoryDays`. It applies to the lists, pages, results (re-read in the data layer), sessions and requests, and writes re-find their row inside it.
- **Audit:** `AuditLog.onBehalfOfUserId` on every assistant write (actor = the assistant), null on the teacher's own.
- **Centres:** the audit log names the teacher with a "via assistant" badge and never resolves the assistant. `scoped(ctx).changeRequests.list` drops the assistant's id (`withoutAssistantId`), so the approvals queue shows the teacher's card with the badge. Settings → General gains **Teacher assistants** ("Allow teacher assistants", on).
- **W7a changed:** revoking an invite and removing an assistant no longer need premium (`teacherActorGate`). Without premium, Teacher → Assistants lists pending invites with Revoke and assistants with Remove, and offers no Invite and no Edit permissions.

**Decided with the founder at W7b, against the prompt (recorded in D-W37):**
- The prompt's **reschedule** request and **cancel a pending request** do not exist for the teacher, so assistants file NEW_SESSION and NEW_CLASS and see the status (backlog 50, 51). The e2e files a new-session request.
- The teacher's portal shows no student phone, so **`students.phones` reveals nothing yet**: rosters are names only, with or without it (backlog 52).
- Two W7a statics in `check:assistants` were replaced (approved; below).

**Decided at W7b, beyond the prompt's list:**
- Manage implies Review.
- `classes.view` maps to the TEACHER membership alone (the teacher's Sessions and class pages carry no cell).
- Assistant writes re-find their row inside the assistant's floor.
- A Grader's list holds no drafts, and a row opens its results.
- The request writer is the only new audit action (`changeRequest.create`); the teacher's own request actions still write none.
- The session change log and the receipt list need no tag: no assistant path writes attendance or money (asserted).
- The e2e's "the teacher switches the branch setting off" is done by the **manager**: a teacher holds no `center.settings.edit`.
- The setting is not copied by "Copy setup from".
- No notices (backlog 48); the teacher's view of assistant activity is backlog 49.

**Against this entry's W7b plan:** the plan's `assistantContext(teacherUserId, centerId, key)` became `resolveAssistantActing` + `assistantCan` + `assistantMayAt`. The plan's "centre screens that name the recorder (the roster, the receipt)" became the audit log and the approvals queue: no assistant writes a roster or receipt row. The plan left assessments to W9; the prompt moved centre-branch assessments here, and W9 keeps workspaces.

**DB:** migration `20261107180000_w7b_assistants_on_behalf`: `AuditLog.onBehalfOfUserId`, `ChangeRequest.viaAssistantUserId` (nullable text, no relation) and `CenterSettings.allowTeacherAssistants` (boolean, default true). Generated with the read-only `migrate diff --from-url`; no `DROP INDEX` was generated, and no hand-written SQL was needed (the three tables already have RLS on). Applied with `migrate deploy` to staging and dev.

**Assertions changed:**
- **Old → new, the prompt's list:** `check:assistants` "revoke / remove require premium" → "do not require premium". The lock-then-gate static for `revokeAssistantInvite` / `removeAssistant` now expects `teacherActorGate` and no `requirePremium`. "A lapsed teacher is refused every teacher action" became two checks: refused what widens; may revoke, remove and be left. The non-premium intruder gets `notFound` on revoke and remove instead of `premiumRequired`.
- **Approved by the founder during W7b:** `check:assistants` "The assistant experience resolves no tenant context and reads no centre data module" → **(a)**, seven statics: the scope is built by `resolveAssistantActing` first and refused without it; then only `getTeacherContexts(acting.teacherUserId)`, narrowed to exactly the branches it returned; `getTeacherContexts` is called once on the surface and never with the session user's id; no `getUserCenterRelations`, `resolveCenterRelations` or `buildContext`; no Membership, PermissionGrant, branch grant, StudentProfile or invitation write; `resolveAssistantActing` reads only the teacher's memberships. "Nothing under (assistant) declares a Server Action" → **(b)**: `assistantActionScope` reaches `loadAssistantScope` with no data call before it, and `resolveAssistantActing` is the loader's first await. Every exported action resolves the acting (directly or through `manageScope`, itself checked) **before its first data-layer call** (any name imported from `lib/db` or the scope helper, `prisma`, `scoped`), refuses without it, and trusts no posted user, link or centre id. Tightened at the founder's review: `loadAssistantScope` no longer reads the link before `resolveAssistantActing`, which now returns it. A mutation run (contexts from `user.id`; a `prisma` read before the scope) failed four of these and was reverted. Also unchanged: the money, premium and account import ban.
- Untouched: "assistants hold no Membership and no centre permission", `seed:verify`, `check:permissions` and the centre and teacher navigation expectations.

**Gates (staging, 7 Nov 2026):** `check:assistants` gains §13–20, **154 checks** in all:
- **The matrix**: every catalogue key at a branch, allowed exactly by Co-teacher, Grader, Full and a Custom set. Each of the five branch keys is refused for a missing grant, the teacher's own cell off (or membership inactive), the branch setting off (the other branch unaffected), the teacher not trusted (requests), premium lapsed (at the end instant, allowed 1 ms before), a swapped teacherId and another centre.
- **Assessments**: Co-teacher creates, saves, publishes, extends and reads results through the unchanged writers. A Grader reads results and an attempt but is refused all six writes. `classes.view` alone reads and writes nothing. Refused by direct call: another teacher's assistant, a swapped teacher, another teacher's class or assessment, another centre's class, a forged centre. Branch off cuts the assistant's read and write while the teacher's still works.
- **Audit**: every on-behalf row is actor = assistant, onBehalfOf = teacher, ids only; the teacher's own row is null.
- **Requests**: stored as the teacher's, `viaAssistantUserId`, audited with no note; refused for a Grader, another teacher's class, another centre, a swapped teacher, untrusted.
- **Centre-facing reads**: the audit feed and the approvals rows (`withoutAssistantId`) carry the teacher, `viaAssistant`, and no assistant name, email or id.
- **The window**: the stricter of 30 / 10 / 60 / 7 days. Class sessions stop at the floor. A 40-day-old exam is readable by the teacher, not the assistant.
- **Rosters and nav**: rosters are id + names only, with phones on file and with `students.phones`; the nav per preset; every branch off → Home only.
- **Removal**: removal and lapse cut access; the assistants still hold no Membership.
- **Old → new edits**: revoke, remove and leave without premium.
- **Statics**: no own context, no money or account import, every page re-resolves, every action resolves `assistantActionScope` first, assessment actions never read `centerId`, `TEACHER_EQUIVALENT` in one file, no phone in the reads, no attendance or money writer, no settlements or account in the nav.

Run alone it passes twice; two copies at once collide on the shared stem (`staging.md`). Also on staging, one at a time, all pass: `check:assessments` 443, `check:teacher-portal` 29, `check:approvals` 49, `check:permissions` 49, `check:config` 15, `check:rls` (59 tables locked down), `check:isolation` 38, `check:admin` 115, `check:visibility` 68, `check:gate-fixtures` 14, `seed:verify` 213. `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `tsc`, `eslint` and `prisma validate` pass. `check:all` and the full e2e suite were not run.

**E2E:** `assistants.spec.ts` (`@write`, `en-1280` and `ar-375`) gains the W7b journey on the seeded teacher.downtown:
1. The admin grants premium.
2. A tagged assistant redeems the teacher's invite and sees the Co-teacher nav (no Overview, no Settlements).
3. The assistant builds and publishes an exam in the teacher's class (owned by the teacher's membership; every audit row actor = assistant, onBehalfOf = teacher) and opens its results.
4. The assistant files a new-session request (stored as the teacher's).
5. The manager sees it in the approvals queue and the audit log with the teacher's name and "via assistant", never the assistant's name.
6. The manager switches "Allow teacher assistants" off, and Downtown disappears from the assistant's branches and the exam page.

The two projects take turns on a lock row. The teardown restores premium and the setting and removes the run's rows. Two back-to-back runs passed (19 with setup, 6.3–8.2 min) and left the same whole-table counts: users 145, audit rows 1,656, change requests 3, invites, links, assessments and lock rows 0. `navigation.spec` 71 passed. The browser logs Base UI's "uncontrolled Switch changing its default" notice after the settings save, the same pattern the form's other switches already have; not a failure.

### W8 — PERSONAL workspaces, split (W8a–W8d delivered; W9 delivered)
The original W8 entry was split into four milestones in the W8a prompt (7 Nov 2026): **W8a** workspaces, classes and sessions; **W8b** students and invitations; **W8c** check-in and payments; **W8d** Earnings, combined analytics and assistant access. **W9** keeps assessments in workspaces. Every rule they build on is in `00-master-product-spec.md` §5.1 (D-W20–D-W29, D-W38).

| Milestone | What it adds | Depends on |
|---|---|---|
| **W8a** (delivered) | `Center.kind`, `Organization.ownerUserId`, the archive flag; `createWorkspace` / `createPersonalWorkspace`; `workspaceCan` + `assertWorkspaceWritable`; the 100-ACTIVE cap; archive / unarchive; workspace classes and sessions through the class engine; the `BRANCH_ONLY` exclusions; the workspace-only Teacher experience | W6 (premium) |
| **W8b** (delivered) | Students and invitations: add a student by phone (D-W27), the hub invitation, "Not linked" / "Linked", enrolment | W8a, W1 (notices) |
| **W8c** (delivered) | Check-in (D-W23) and payments with the forced 0 cut, voids and refunds, and the settlement refusal (D-W24) | W8b |
| **W8d** (delivered) | The Earnings page (D-W29), combined teacher analytics (D-W25), assistant access to workspaces (`checkin.workspace`, `payments.workspace`, D-W19 windows) | W8c, W7b |
| **W9** (delivered) | Assessments in workspaces and assistant access to them (D-W26, D-W42) | W8b, W8d |

### W8a — PERSONAL workspaces: the model, creation, archive, classes and sessions (delivered 7 Nov 2026)
**Built:** `Center.kind` (`BRANCH | PERSONAL`, default BRANCH), `Center.archivedAt` / `archivedByUserId`, `Organization.ownerUserId` (unique, `RESTRICT`); triggers refusing a PERSONAL centre under an organization with no owner, and CHECKs on the archive pair. `createWorkspace` (`lib/db/workspaces.ts`: the `User` lock, `requirePremium`, the cap) and `createPersonalWorkspace` (`lib/db/platform.ts`, the second and last `center.create`: personal organization, Center, minimal `CenterSettings`, the teacher's TEACHER membership, one hidden room — no baseline). `resolveWorkspaceAccess` / `workspaceCan` / `assertWorkspaceWritable`, the fixed `WORKSPACE_OWNER_CAPABILITIES`. Archive through the engine's `deactivateClassInTransaction` (`deactivateClass` is its wrapper, unchanged in behaviour), unarchive under the cap. Workspace class and session writers over the unchanged engine with the forced values (owner, hidden room, PERCENTAGE 0, no booking, never browsable) and the PERSONAL branch of the pair rule (`isWorkspacePairOffered`). `BRANCH_ONLY` (`lib/db/center-kind.ts`) spread into `ACCESSIBLE_CENTER`, `COUNTED_BRANCHES`, Explore and the platform console. `getWorkspaceContext` (the owner's TEACHER context, no grants). Teacher nav **Workspaces**; `/teacher/workspaces`, `/teacher/workspaces/[id]` (Classes), `…/classes/[classId]`, `…/sessions`, `…/settings`. A workspace-only account's Teacher experience lands on `/teacher/workspaces`. Decisions: D-W38.
**DB:** yes — `20261107210000_w8a_personal_workspaces` (one enum, three Center columns, one Organization column with its unique index and foreign key, two CHECKs, two triggers). Additive; no `DROP INDEX` was generated.
**Done when:** a premium teacher creates a workspace and a class with a weekly slot (cut fixed at 0), sees its sessions, reschedules and cancels one, archives the workspace (future sessions handled as a class deactivation) and unarchives it; the 101st active workspace is refused; with premium lapsed everything stays readable and every write is refused; no workspace appears in Discover, Explore, public pages, admin lists, the hub, the switcher, analytics or any seed-world check; a workspace-only teacher lands on `/teacher/workspaces`; every other role is refused by direct call.
**Gates:** new **`check:workspaces`** (staging; in CI and the gate-fixture registry as `workspaces-check`): the creation baseline, owner-only authority for nine other roles by direct call, premium on every write but archive, read-only on lapse and restored on renewal, the cap at 99 + 1 + 1 and under two concurrent creations, archive and unarchive (and the cap at unarchive), the forced cut and the offered pairs, Discover / Explore / reservations / bookings, every exclusion by a read, isolation between two teachers and against a branch, ids-only audit, the workspace-only experience, and statics (actions take the actor from the session; no matrix; the migration touches no RLS; the one predicate spread; the pages resolve their own context). **Old → new (the prompt's allowed changes):** `check:permissions`' "exactly one `center.create`" → "exactly two, and only `createPersonalWorkspace` makes a PERSONAL centre, without the baseline"; `seed:verify`'s seed world (baseline, orphans, counts) → BRANCH centres only; `check:admin`'s `requirePremium` callers → `lib/db/assistants.ts` and `lib/db/workspaces.ts`; the shared expected teacher nav gains `workspaces`. Also on staging: `check:permissions`, `check:admin`, `check:class-periods`, `check:classes`, `check:session-lifecycle`, `check:isolation`, `check:rls`, `check:people`, `check:teacher-portal`, `check:student-portal`, `check:branches`, `check:analytics`, `check:account`, `check:gate-fixtures`, `seed:verify` (and `--strict-clean`); e2e `workspaces.spec.ts`, `navigation.spec.ts`, `experience-switcher.spec.ts`.
**Docs:** 00 (D-W38, §4.2 item 11, §4.4), 01, 02 §4 §6.2 §18, this entry, 04 §11.37, 05 (entries 53–55), 06, `CLAUDE.md` rule 13, `staging.md`.

### V9 — Two gates red after W8a: classified and fixed (delivered 8 Nov 2026, test-only, no product change)
`check:all` on staging after W8a failed three assertions in two gates. Each was reproduced first. C was also run at V8 (`9deabf0`), at W7b (`975fd28`) and its follow-up (`fc01cb5`), and at W8a (`f4803fb`), in throwaway git worktrees against the current staging schema.
- **A. `check:class-creation`: "Only lib/db/teacher-membership.ts creates a TEACHER membership" → STALE ASSERTION.** W8a's `createPersonalWorkspace` (`lib/db/platform.ts`) is the second creator, by design (D-W38: a workspace owner is a TEACHER member of their own PERSONAL centre only). **Old → new:** exactly two creators in `app/` and `lib/db/`: `teacher-membership.ts` and `createPersonalWorkspace`, once. Two new checks pin the latter. It creates only the membership of the account its personal organization is found or created for (`ownerUserId: input.owner.id`), in the PERSONAL centre it has just created under that organization, and it has one caller: `createWorkspace` (`lib/db/workspaces.ts`), which locks the owner's row and re-reads premium first.
- **B. `check:one-off-sessions`: "No reader dereferences a session's classGroup unguarded" (teacher session page :306, :310) → SAFE, a false positive.** The lines arrived in W2 (`044ece0`). Line 306 is `{session.classGroup && session.classGroup.teacherId === … ? (`, and line 310 is inside that block. The scan only knew the ternary form `session.classGroup ?`. It now also accepts `session.classGroup && …` written before every dereference on its line, and a JSX block opened by `{session.classGroup && …` / `{session.classGroup ? …` and closed by `) : null}` / `)}` at the same indentation. An unclosed block guards nothing. A new pure check proves the scan still flags a bare dereference, one after a guarded block, one in a block with another condition, and one before its `&&`. Confirmed in a browser: the new `@write` e2e (`teacher.spec.ts`) opens a one-time session as teacher.downtown, with the page unchanged. Because the static is time-independent and failed on V8's code too, the "last green `check:all` after V8" cannot have included this assertion passing.
- **C. `check:one-off-sessions`: "Approving a teacher's NEW_SESSION request creates an isManual session" → FIXTURE (time-dependent; not a regression).** The resolver answered `{"ok":false,"error":"pastDate"}` at all four commits. The step called `resolveSessionRequest` without the gate's clock, so SEC-2b's "no past date" rule read the real date, and the fixed payload date 5 Nov 2026 became past on 6 Nov in Cairo. `approvals.ts` has not changed since before V8. Minimal edit: pass `now: NOW` (12 Oct 2026, the clock of every other write in the gate), and print the result as the check's detail.
**DB:** no.
**Gates:** `check:class-creation` and `check:one-off-sessions` 0 failed on staging; also `check:workspaces`, `check:teacher-portal`, `check:class-periods`, `check:classes`, `check:approvals`, `check:assistants` (`check:assessments` not run: the session page was not changed); `check:design`, `check:rtl`, `check:public`, `check:gate-fixtures`. e2e `teacher.spec.ts` (en-1280, ar-375) and the new test twice. Session counts were identical after each run.
**Docs:** this entry, `staging.md` (§6b "e2e one-time sessions").

### W8b — Workspace students and invitations (delivered 8 Nov 2026)
**Built:** `lib/db/workspace-students.ts` — `addWorkspaceStudent` / `copyWorkspaceStudent` (the profile at the workspace behind a placeholder `User` with no global contact, `createPlaceholderPerson`; the duplicate check inside the workspace only), the one SQL statement that matches `User.phone` of an account with a login and, on a match, writes the PENDING `WorkspaceInvitation` and its `WORKSPACE_INVITATION` notice (run on every add and every "Invite again", so the answer, the audit row, the limiter and the round trips are the same either way), `inviteWorkspaceStudentAgain` (24 hours from the profile's last add or invite audit row), `cancelWorkspaceInvitation`, `acceptWorkspaceInvitation` (one transaction: the profile moved to the account, the placeholder removed only when it holds nothing else — `deletePersonIfOrphaned` now takes an optional client) and `declineWorkspaceInvitation`, `enrolWorkspaceStudent` / `removeWorkspaceEnrollment` (`claimClassSeat`, REMOVED), and the reads. The capability `workspace.students.manage`; two limiter names (`workspaceStudentAddPerUser` 60/h, `workspaceInvitePerUser` 20/day). The student-side opt-in resolver `getStudentScheduleContexts` (schedule, calendar, leave and the session-notice re-check only), `listExperiences`' `studentInWorkspace`, the notice re-check's `workspaceCenterIds` / `invitationCenterIds`, the notice kind's audience `invitation`. Pages: the workspace's **Students** tab (`/teacher/workspaces/[id]/students`), a **Students** roster on the class page, the hub's invitation card (and the hub stays open while one is pending), "Private class" on the student schedule and calendar. Decisions: D-W39.
**DB:** yes — `20261108120000_w8b_workspace_invitations` (the `WorkspaceInvitationStatus` enum, the `WORKSPACE_INVITATION` notice kind, the `WorkspaceInvitation` table with three indexes and three foreign keys, RLS on, and the hand-written partial unique index `WorkspaceInvitation_one_pending_per_profile`, CLAUDE.md rule 7). Additive; generated with the read-only `migrate diff --from-url`, no `DROP INDEX` was generated; applied with `migrate deploy` to staging and dev.
**Done when:** a teacher adds a student by phone and the answer is identical with or without a matching account; a matching account gets a hub invitation and a notice; accepting links the profile and removes the placeholder only when it holds nothing else; the student then sees their enrolled workspace classes and sessions and nothing else; declining leaves the teacher view unchanged; no workspace appears in Discover or Explore; branch students are unaffected; every other role is refused by direct call.
**Gates:** `check:workspaces` §13 (staging): one answer and one audit shape with and without a match; only `User.phone` of an account with a login matches (not `contactPhone`, a login-less `User.phone` or another tenant's profile phone); an invitation and a notice only on a match; the placeholder has no global contact; the teacher never receives the account's id, email or name; the duplicate rule; the partial index (by a write and in `pg_indexes`) and RLS; "Invite again"'s window, answer and audit shape; cancel and decline, and the notice hidden after; enrolment and its isolation; accept (the move, the placeholder removed or kept, `alreadyInWorkspace`, twice); the linked student's schedule, calendar and history (not an unenrolled class, not another teacher's workspace, not Discover); the workspace-only Student experience; the producers and the re-check (and a deactivated profile); leave; copy; every other role on all six writers; premium lapse and archive; both rate limits; ids-only audit; statics. Also on staging, one at a time, 0 failed: `check:enrollment`, `check:student-portal`, `check:account`, `check:people`, `check:isolation`, `check:permissions`, `check:rls`, `check:parent`, `check:gate-fixtures`; `seed:verify` and `--strict-clean`. **`check:notifications`** fails one assertion — its hand-written list of exactly ten notice kinds, which the new `WORKSPACE_INVITATION` makes eleven — left unedited pending the founder's approval (the W8b prompt allowed no old → new change). e2e (staging, `en-1280` and `ar-375`): `workspaces.spec.ts` (its new `@write` test), `navigation.spec.ts`, `experience-switcher.spec.ts`.

**Built:** `lib/db/workspace-attendance.ts` — `workspaceCheckIn` (ACTIVE-enrolled students only, through `createAttendance` / `recheckInAttendee`; `notEnrolled` otherwise, no drop-ins), `workspaceSetAttendance` (`changeAttendanceStatus`; the Absent switch voids, with its confirmation and reason), `workspaceRecordPayment` (the unchanged `recordPayment`, the hidden Cash method resolved server-side, no amount posted), `workspaceVoidPayment`, `workspaceRefundPayment`, `workspaceCloseSession`, each with a `WorkspaceActor` (actor and teacher separately) and `authorizeWrite`; the reads `workspaceSessionDetail` / `workspaceSessionRoster`. ### W9 — Assessments in workspaces + assistant access to them (delivered 11 Nov 2026)
**Built:** the W2–W4 engine on PERSONAL-workspace classes (D-W26, D-W42). One new fixed owner capability, `workspace.assessments.manage`; `resolveAssessmentTeacher` decides a workspace's own context by `workspaceCan`; every writer in `lib/db/assessments.ts` opens with `writeGate` (`teacherStillActive`, `actorMayWrite`, then `workspaceWriteGate`: the workspace re-resolved for the owning teacher, the capability, `assertWorkspaceWritable` — `premiumRequired` / `workspaceArchived`), and results (`holdsResultsAuthority`) decide a workspace by `workspaceCan` with a `teacherHistoryDays`-only floor. Students: `studentBranches` reads the opt-in resolver; `startAttempt` refuses an unlinked PERSONAL profile (`linkedWhereRequired`); no student path reads premium. Notices: linked-only publish and extend recipients and "soon" read in a PERSONAL centre; `notificationReach` / `ensureSoonNotices` add linked workspaces to `studentCenterIds`. Teacher: `getTeacherWorkspaceScope`, `assessmentBranches` unioning owned workspaces, the Source filter, Private tags, the read-only reason (`assessmentReadOnlyReason`), `LinkedAssessments` on workspace class and session pages, the calendar's workspace assessments, `TEACHER_WORKSPACE_ONLY_NAV` (+ Calendar, Exams & homework) and Exams & homework for any workspace owner. Assistants: `WORKSPACE_EQUIVALENT` maps both assessment keys to the capability; `assistantAssessmentBranches` adds the teacher's workspaces; the nav item counts workspaces; New exam / Add homework on the assistant's workspace pages. Student labels: workspace name, teacher name, **Private class**.
**DB:** no.
**Done when:** a workspace owner creates, publishes, extends and reviews an exam and a homework; a linked enrolled student sees the notice, the calendar entry and the assessment, takes it and gets the result and reveal per the toggles, with no `isCorrect` before reveal; an unlinked profile, an unenrolled student, a student who left, another teacher, centre staff and a parent are refused; a lapsed premium blocks teacher writes but not a student's attempt; a Co-teacher assistant works in a workspace within the grants, audited with both people; nothing reaches a centre-side surface or the hub, switcher, staff or teacher resolvers.
**Gates (staging, 11 Nov 2026, one at a time):** `check:workspaces` **399 passed, 0 failed** (336 → 399; §17: authority, the engine's defaults, validation, publish, locks, extend, attempts, lazy finalisation, reveal, frozen scores, the serializer without correctness, eligibility — unlinked, unenrolled, left, linked enrolled, another workspace, another teacher — every teacher write refused on lapse with none of the student's, archive, notices and the re-check, the assistant matrix — Grader, Co-teacher, Full, check-in only, lapsed, removed link, swapped teacher, another workspace — audit with both ids, centre-side, hub and switcher reads, the statics). `check:assessments` 448, `check:assistants` 175, `check:notifications` 172, `check:student-portal` 138, `check:teacher-portal` 29, `check:enrollment` 45, `check:isolation` 38, `check:rls` PASS, `check:permissions` 50, `check:timezone` 84, `check:performance` 6, `check:gate-fixtures` 14 — all 0 failed; `seed:verify` and `seed:verify -- --strict-clean` pass. **e2e (staging, `en-1280` and `ar-375`):** `workspaces.spec` 24 + the new W9 test, `assessments.spec` 25 + the new W9 assistant test, `navigation.spec` 71 — all pass. Two first-run failures were the tests' own (a stale `now` for the lapse; a hidden table row matched at 375 px; the teacher's `User` not yet created before sign-in) and one a real gap (a placeholder could be written a "soon" notice by a direct call — closed in `syncAssessmentSoonNotices`). `check:permissions` caught the list page's gate written in a form its reader does not parse; the page now keeps `requirePermission(ctx, …)`.

**Built:** a one-time share code that links an unlinked PERSONAL-workspace student to any signed-in account (D-W43; backlog entry 56, closed). `WorkspaceShareCode` (sha256 only, 7 days, single use, at most one active per profile by the hand-written partial index `WorkspaceShareCode_one_active_per_profile`). `lib/db/workspace-share-codes.ts`: **Share code** (`authorizeWrite` with `workspace.students.manage` — premium, not archived — revoking the profile's previous code), **Revoke code** (ownership and the capability only), the preview and the confirm, which claims the code conditionally, cancels any PENDING invitation for the profile and runs **the W8b move** — lifted unchanged out of `acceptWorkspaceInvitation` into `moveWorkspaceProfileToAccount`, which accepting now calls too. The code reuses the assistant code's generator, hash and normalizer (`lib/workspace-share-code.ts`). Three limits on the existing limiter (`workspaceShareCodePerUser`, `workspaceJoinPerUser`, `workspaceJoinPerIp`). ## M20 — Pre-deployment
**Goal:** close `00-master-product-spec.md` §2.1 and actually be deployable.
**Build:** **media storage** — connect Cloudinary (authenticated delivery) behind `lib/storage`, then set `MEDIA_UPLOADS_ENABLED=true`, for **logos, teacher photos and class covers** (F8, U9; `MEDIA_AVATARS_DISPLAY` no longer exists); **domain** purchase and DNS/Vercel wiring (blocked on **OD-1**); **SMTP / transactional email** sender with SPF/DKIM — invites, password reset, confirmations; **SMS/WhatsApp provider accounts** — the *channel* only, credentials and sender IDs; the notification **features** stay Phase 2 (`05-phase-2-backlog.md`); **Sentry** error monitoring; **RLS applied** — since SEC-2a this is a checked-in migration (`20261017090100_sec2a_data_api_lockdown`, deny-all, D-19 (a)) that `prisma migrate deploy` applies like any other; run `npm run check:rls` against the production project after deploying and before any data goes in. The Prisma role on Supabase has BYPASSRLS, so RLS is a Data API backstop only (a dedicated non-bypass role is `05-phase-2-backlog.md` entry 23); **auth hardening (SEC-2a)** — turn on Supabase *Confirm email* **and** set `REQUIRE_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION=true` (a production deployment refuses to start without both), enable CAPTCHA (Turnstile) on sign-up and sign-in (SEC-27, deferred here from SEC-2a), enable leaked-password protection, ship the password-reset flow (SEC-66 — the auth-confirm route already exists), delete the legacy dev auth users that still accept the historical seed password (SEC-36), and add the production project ref to `PRODUCTION_PROJECT_REFS` (`prisma/seed-data/production-refs.ts`, SEC-70); **remove the demo overrides** — unset `ALLOW_AUTO_CONFIRM_DEMO` on Vercel (DEPLOY-1; it lifts the confirm-email guard for a seeded demo and must not exist once a real centre is on the deployment; `docs/deploy-vercel.md`) **and `ALLOW_UNVERIFIED_EMAIL_CHANGE`** (F2; it lets Account settings change an email with no confirmation link — remove before a real centre; once SMTP and `REQUIRE_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION=true` are on, Supabase's email-change flow takes over and production refuses to start with both set; **also delete the `app/[locale]/account/actions.ts` allowlist entry in `check:security` SYS-STORAGE** (G2) so the service-role client goes back to a single importer); **remove the `noindex`** — unsetting `ALLOW_AUTO_CONFIRM_DEMO` also lifts the `robots: noindex, nofollow` every page carries while it is set (F5, `noIndexDemo`); confirm the public Explore pages are indexable once real branches are on them; **enforce the Content-Security-Policy** (moved here from `05-phase-2-backlog.md` entry 22 at SEC-2c — a browser pass over every route group in both locales with the report-only policy, Sentry's CSP intake as the report endpoint, then a per-request nonce through `proxy.ts` and the root layout); a root **`app/global-error.tsx`** built with the Sentry integration (SEC-75's remaining half); the **legal review of retention and erasure** for minors' data (OD-2, SEC-29 — the rule, then the anonymisation routine it implies); a **dedicated staging Supabase project** for CI's `DATABASE_URL`/`DIRECT_URL` (never the shared dev project, never production) — **seeded**, so CI can also run the e2e smoke suite (`npm run e2e`, SYS) with the seed passwords as secrets; **backups + a tested restore** (tested, not configured); **data export** (scope blocked on **OD-4**). Data *import* is explicitly **not** here — it moves to `05-phase-2-backlog.md` entry 10. *(R4: the Vercel cron entry for `app/api/cron/generate-sessions` and its `CRON_SECRET`, listed here until R4, no longer exist — the generator was removed and a class creates its whole period's sessions itself, so nothing needs scheduling.)*
**Done when:** every box in §2.1 is ticked or explicitly re-assigned with a reason, a restore has actually been performed from a backup, and the CI workflow runs green with real `DATABASE_URL`/`DIRECT_URL` secrets against a dedicated staging project.
**Gates:** the whole `check:*` suite green in CI — that is what this milestone is for. Export is a money/isolation surface (it can leak another centre's view of a shared person), so it gets an assertion in `check:isolation` rather than shipping ungated.

## M21 — (post-pilot) Offline resilience for the reception loop
**Goal:** the desk keeps working when the internet does not — **if the pilot proves it needs to**.
**Precondition: OD-7.** Do not start this before the pilot has measured how often, and for how long, Center 60 actually drops during a rush. Building it speculatively is the worst outcome; discovering the need after the pilot fails is the second worst.
**Cost, stated honestly:** this app has **no client data layer today** — every read is a Server Component, every write a Server Action. Scoped offline means **building one**. Budget for that, not for a feature flag.
**Build:** an IndexedDB queue for the **reception loop only** — check-in, attendance, payment; nothing else. **Graduated behaviour:** full function **0–60 minutes** offline → **block new writes at 60 minutes** rather than accumulating unbounded unsynced money → **never auto-discard the queue**, only a deliberate, audited **Owner** discard. **Design constraints:** the **client never computes money** — a queued item stores **intent only**, and the server computes every snapshot at sync time through the same `lib/money.ts`/`recordPayment` path a live write uses (CLAUDE.md rules 2 and 3 apply to a queued write exactly as to a live one); **every queued action carries a client-generated UUID the server dedupes on**, because a queue that retries must be safe to replay.
**Done when:** a reception session survives a tab close and a 60-minute outage with every write landing exactly once on sync; replaying the same queue twice produces no duplicate `SessionAttendee` or `Payment` rows; **no money value is ever computed on the client** (asserted, not reviewed); and a discard is impossible without an Owner and an audit row.
**Gates:** `npm run check:offline` (new — replay idempotency on the client UUID, the 60-minute threshold behaviour, and a static assertion that no client bundle imports `lib/money.ts`). `npm run check:payments` and `npm run check:performance` must both still pass — this milestone changes how money reaches the ledger, and those two are what prove the ledger itself is unchanged.

---

## Sequencing notes
- **Demo shell** can be pulled forward: after M0–M1 + a themed M5 with seed data, you have a clickable, brand-swappable demo for owner meetings — even before the deeper milestones are complete.
- **Multi-center** is proven at M2 (config) and M7 (teacher portal), not bolted on at the end — the architecture carries it from M1. **Multi-*branch*** is proven at M11 the same way, and deliberately as an additive layer: `center_id` stays the sole isolation key, so `check:isolation` passing unchanged is the milestone's real acceptance test.
- **R4 removed the only scheduled job** (session generation). Nothing in Phase 1 runs on a timer: the R2 end-of-day close is lazy and a class materializes its own period. **The W-batch keeps it that way**: assessment deadlines finalise lazily on read (D-W10), notices are written by their event or computed on read (D-W12), and premium expiry is computed per request (D-W15).
- **W-batch money.** W8 (W8a the centre kind, W8c the money) adds a second kind of centre (PERSONAL) whose payments are 100% the teacher's and never settled. Like M13/M14/R1/R2, it must leave `check:payments` and `check:settlements` passing unchanged — it edits who may record a payment and what a settlement may claim, never how a payment's snapshot is computed.
- **Money** is concentrated in M5/M6 and gated hard by tests. **M13, M14, R1, R2 and M21 all touch money again** — the centre-cut rework, refunds/settlements, the frozen payment-method label, void-on-absent and closed-session settlements, and the offline queue respectively — and each is required to leave `check:payments` passing unchanged. A milestone that edits money's *readers* must prove it did not edit money's *history*.
- **M11 before M12 before everything else**, and not by preference: every later milestone's scope depends on whether "a centre" means one branch or several (M11), and every later milestone adds actions that must land in the matrix rather than in a hardcoded check (M12). Retrofitting either is far more expensive than sequencing for it.
- **M20 is a gate, not a feature.** It exists so `00-master-product-spec.md` §2.1 stops being a list nobody owns. Two of its items are blocked on open decisions (**OD-1** domain, **OD-4** export scope, `00-master-product-spec.md` §2.2) — resolve those before the milestone starts, not during it.
- **M21 is conditional on evidence** (**OD-7**). It is the only milestone in this document whose first task is deciding whether to build it at all, and the only one that requires building infrastructure (a client data layer) this app has none of.
- Each milestone = one durable Claude Code working session (or a few), starting from `CLAUDE.md` + the relevant doc section, so context cost stays low.
